        "def log_ok(message):\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ {message}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def log_info(message):\n",
        "    print(f\"  ℹ️ {message}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def log_warn(message):\n",
        "    print(f\"  ⚠️ {message}\")\n",
        "    MANGABD_ENV[\"warnings\"].append(message)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def log_error(message):\n",
        "    print(f\"  ❌ {message}\")\n",
        "    MANGABD_ENV[\"errors\"].append(message)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_command(command, timeout=900):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Safe subprocess runner.\n",
        "    Returns: (success: bool, output: str)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        result = subprocess.run(\n",
        "            command,\n",
        "            capture_output=True,\n",
        "            text=True,\n",
        "            timeout=timeout,\n",
        "            check=False,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if result.returncode == 0:\n",
        "            return True, result.stdout.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        error_output = result.stderr.strip() or result.stdout.strip()\n",
        "        return False, error_output\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        return False, str(exc)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def pip_install(packages, label, optional=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Safe pip installer.\n",
        "    - No broken argument spacing\n",
        "    - Retry with --no-deps if normal install fails\n",
        "    - Optional packages do not block Cell 1\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    print(f\"  📦 Installing {label}: {len(packages)} packages\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    command = [\n",
        "        sys.executable,\n",
        "        \"-m\",\n",
        "        \"pip\",\n",
        "        \"install\",\n",
        "        \"--quiet\",\n",
        "        \"--disable-pip-version-check\",\n",
        "    ] + packages\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok, output = run_command(command)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if ok:\n",
        "        log_ok(f\"{label} installed\")\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    retry_message = f\"{label} normal install failed; retrying with --no-deps\"\n",
        "    if optional:\n",
        "        log_warn(retry_message)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        log_warn(retry_message)\n",
        "\n",
        "    command_no_deps = [\n",
        "        sys.executable,\n",
        "        \"-m\",\n",
        "        \"pip\",\n",
        "        \"install\",\n",
        "        \"--quiet\",\n",
        "        \"--disable-pip-version-check\",\n",
        "        \"--no-deps\",\n",
        "    ] + packages\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok2, output2 = run_command(command_no_deps)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if ok2:\n",
        "        log_ok(f\"{label} installed with --no-deps\")\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    failure_message = f\"{label} install failed: {output2[:300]}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if optional:\n",
        "        log_warn(failure_message)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        log_error(failure_message)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return False\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def module_available(module_name):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Check if a Python module can be imported.\"\"\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        importlib.import_module(module_name)\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return False\n",

        "            break\n",
        "\n",

        "    if secret_value:\n",

        "MANGABD_EVENTS_FILE = Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"events_file\"])\n",
        "MANGABD_SESSION_LOG_FILE = Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"session_log_file\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "logger = logging.getLogger(\"mangabd\")\n",
        "logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)\n",
        "\n",
        "# পুরনো handler remove\n",
        "logger.handlers.clear()\n",
        "\n",
        "log_formatter = logging.Formatter(\n",
        "    fmt=\"%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(message)s\",\n",
        "    datefmt=\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\",\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "file_handler = logging.FileHandler(\n",
        "    MANGABD_SESSION_LOG_FILE,\n",
        "    encoding=\"utf-8\",\n",
        ")\n",
        "file_handler.setFormatter(log_formatter)\n",
        "\n",
        "stream_handler = logging.StreamHandler()\n",
        "stream_handler.setFormatter(log_formatter)\n",
        "\n",
        "logger.addHandler(file_handler)\n",
        "logger.addHandler(stream_handler)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_EVENTS = []\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def log_event(message, level=\"INFO\", **extra):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Structured event logger.\n",
        "\n",
        "    - session.log-এ human-readable log\n",
        "    - events.jsonl-এ machine-readable event\n",
        "    - MANGABD_EVENTS list-এ in-memory store\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    entry = {\n",
        "        \"timestamp\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "        \"level\": level.upper(),\n",
        "        \"message\": message,\n",
        "        \"extra\": extra,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    MANGABD_EVENTS.append(entry)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Human-readable log\n",
        "    log_level = getattr(logging, level.upper(), logging.INFO)\n",
        "    logger.log(log_level, message)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Machine-readable JSONL\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        with open(MANGABD_EVENTS_FILE, \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as events_file:\n",
        "            events_file.write(json.dumps(entry, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        logger.warning(f\"Event file write failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"  ✅ Logger ready\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Log file  : {MANGABD_SESSION_LOG_FILE}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Events    : {MANGABD_EVENTS_FILE}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 8. Save config + manifest\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  8. Saving config and session manifest\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_config():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    CONFIG save করে।\n",
        "    Secrets কখনো save হবে না।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    config_path = Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"config_file\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    config_public = json.loads(json.dumps(MANGABD_CONFIG))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Safety: secret-like keys remove\n",
        "    forbidden_keys = [\n",
        "        \"api_key\",\n",
        "        \"token\",\n",
        "        \"secret\",\n",
        "        \"password\",\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    def sanitize(obj):\n",
        "        if isinstance(obj, dict):\n",
        "            clean = {}\n",
        "            for key, value in obj.items():\n",
        "                if any(bad in str(key).lower() for bad in forbidden_keys):\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "                clean[key] = sanitize(value)\n",
        "            return clean\n",
        "\n",
        "        if isinstance(obj, list):\n",
        "            return [sanitize(item) for item in obj]\n",
        "\n",
        "        return obj\n",
        "\n",
        "    config_public = sanitize(config_public)\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(config_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as config_file:\n",
        "        json.dump(config_public, config_file, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return config_path\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_manifest():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Session manifest save করে।\n",
        "    এটাই pipeline state-এর single source of truth হবে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    manifest_path = Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"manifest_file\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    manifest = {\n",
        "        \"version\": MANGABD_CONFIG[\"version\"],\n",
        "        \"session_id\": SESSION_ID,\n",
        "        \"created_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "        \"status\": \"initialized\",\n",
        "        \"device\": DEVICE,\n",
        "        \"language\": MANGABD_CONFIG[\"language\"],\n",
        "        \"pages\": {},\n",
        "        \"stats\": {\n",
        "            \"uploaded\": 0,\n",
        "            \"detected\": 0,\n",
        "            \"ocr_done\": 0,\n",
        "            \"translated\": 0,\n",
        "            \"inpainted\": 0,\n",
        "            \"rendered\": 0,\n",
        "            \"exported\": 0,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(manifest_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as manifest_file:\n",
        "        json.dump(manifest, manifest_file, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return manifest_path\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "config_file = save_config()\n",
        "manifest_file = save_manifest()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"  ✅ Config saved  : {config_file}\")\n",
        "print(f\"  ✅ Manifest saved: {manifest_file}\")\n",

        "\n",
        "def to_json_serializable(obj):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    numpy, Path, set ইত্যাদি কে JSON-safe object-এ convert করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, dict):\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            str(key): to_json_serializable(value)\n",
        "            for key, value in obj.items()\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):\n",
        "        return [to_json_serializable(item) for item in obj]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, set):\n",
        "        return [to_json_serializable(item) for item in obj]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, Path):\n",
        "        return str(obj)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, np.integer):\n",
        "        return int(obj)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, np.floating):\n",
        "        return float(obj)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, np.bool_):\n",
        "        return bool(obj)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):\n",
        "        return obj.tolist()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return obj\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def atomic_write_json(path, data):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Crash-safe JSON writer.\n",
        "\n",
        "    আগে .tmp file-এ লেখে, তারপর atomic replace করে।\n",
        "    এতে write চলাকালীন crash হলে পুরনো JSON corrupt হয় না।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    path = Path(path)\n",
        "    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    tmp_path = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + \".tmp\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    safe_data = to_json_serializable(data)\n",
        "\n",
        "    with open(tmp_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as json_file:\n",
        "        json.dump(safe_data, json_file, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    os.replace(tmp_path, path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return path\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_json_safe(path, default=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    JSON load করে। corrupt হলে default return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    path = Path(path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not path.exists():\n",
        "        return default\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as json_file:\n",
        "            return json.load(json_file)\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"JSON load failed: {path.name} — {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        return default\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ JSON helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",

        "        log_event(\"Manifest invalid, recreated\", level=\"WARN\")\n",
        "        return MANGABD_MANIFEST\n",
        "\n",
        "    loaded.setdefault(\"pages\", {})\n",

        "# 6. Page ID helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def make_page_id(filename):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Filename থেকে safe page_id বানায়।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Example:\n",
        "        \"page 01.jpg\" → \"page_01.jpg\"\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    base = Path(filename).name.strip().replace(\" \", \"_\")\n",
        "    safe = re.sub(r\"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]\", \"_\", base)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not safe:\n",
        "        safe = f\"page_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    # যদি same page_id আগে থেকে থাকে কিন্তু original filename ভিন্ন হয়,\n",
        "    # তাহলে suffix যোগ করি।\n",
        "    if safe in pages:\n",
        "        existing_original = pages[safe].get(\"original_filename\", \"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if existing_original != filename:\n",
        "            stem, ext = os.path.splitext(safe)\n",
        "            safe = f\"{stem}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4]}{ext}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return safe\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_page_dir(page_id):\n",

        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Manifest থেকে page dict return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "    page = pages.get(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page is None and required:\n",
        "        raise KeyError(f\"Page not found in manifest: {page_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return page\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def list_pages(status=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Page list return করে। status filter optional।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    if status is None:\n",
        "        return list(pages.values())\n",
        "\n",
        "    return [\n",
        "        page for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"status\") == status\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def init_page(\n",
        "    filename,\n",
        "    width=None,\n",
        "    height=None,\n",
        "    image_np=None,\n",
        "    overwrite=False,\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    নতুন page initialize করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        page_id\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global MANGABD_MANIFEST\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not MANGABD_MANIFEST:\n",
        "        load_manifest()\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_id = make_page_id(filename)\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.setdefault(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    # যদি page আগে থেকে থাকে এবং overwrite না হয়\n",
        "    if page_id in pages and not overwrite:\n",
        "        page = pages[page_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if width is not None:\n",
        "            page[\"width\"] = int(width)\n",
        "        if height is not None:\n",
        "            page[\"height\"] = int(height)\n",
        "\n",
        "        page[\"updated_at\"] = _now()\n",
        "        save_manifest()\n",
        "\n",
        "        return page_id\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_dir = get_page_dir(page_id)\n",
        "    page_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    artifacts = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    for artifact_name, artifact_filename in ARTIFACT_FILENAMES.items():\n",
        "        artifacts[artifact_name] = str(page_dir / artifact_filename)\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = {\n",
        "        \"page_id\": page_id,\n",
        "        \"original_filename\": filename,\n",
        "        \"created_at\": _now(),\n",
        "        \"updated_at\": _now(),\n",
        "        \"status\": \"uploaded\",\n",
        "        \"width\": int(width) if width is not None else None,\n",
        "        \"height\": int(height) if height is not None else None,\n",
        "        \"stages\": {stage: False for stage in PAGE_STAGES},\n",
        "        \"artifacts\": artifacts,\n",
        "        \"meta\": {},\n",
        "        \"errors\": [],\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages[page_id] = page\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Original image save করলে upload stage done\n",
        "    if image_np is not None:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            save_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\", image_np, quality=95)\n",
        "            page[\"stages\"][\"upload\"] = True\n",
        "            page[\"status\"] = \"uploaded\"\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            add_page_error(page_id, \"upload\", str(exc)[:200])\n",
        "\n",
        "    save_manifest()\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Page initialized: {page_id}\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        filename=filename,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return page_id\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def update_page_meta(page_id, **kwargs):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Page metadata update করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Example:\n",
        "        update_page_meta(page_id, ocr_engine=\"qwen\", rtdetr_score=0.82)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    page.setdefault(\"meta\", {})\n",
        "    page[\"meta\"].update(to_json_serializable(kwargs))\n",
        "    page[\"updated_at\"] = _now()\n",
        "\n",
        "    save_manifest()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return page\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def add_page_error(page_id, stage, message):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Page-এর error log করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    page.setdefault(\"errors\", [])\n",
        "\n",
        "    page[\"errors\"].append({\n",
        "        \"timestamp\": _now(),\n",
        "        \"stage\": stage,\n",
        "        \"message\": str(message)[:500],\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "    page[\"updated_at\"] = _now()\n",
        "\n",
        "    save_manifest()\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Page error [{page_id}] {stage}: {str(message)[:120]}\",\n",
        "        level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        stage=stage,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return page\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def mark_stage_done(page_id, stage, meta=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর নির্দিষ্ট stage complete mark করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if stage not in PAGE_STAGES:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\n",
        "            f\"Invalid stage: {stage}. \"\n",
        "            f\"Valid stages: {PAGE_STAGES}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    page.setdefault(\"stages\", {})\n",
        "    page[\"stages\"][stage] = True\n",
        "\n",
        "    page[\"status\"] = STAGE_STATUS_MAP.get(stage, stage)\n",
        "    page[\"updated_at\"] = _now()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if meta:\n",
        "        page.setdefault(\"meta\", {})\n",
        "        page[\"meta\"].update(to_json_serializable(meta))\n",
        "\n",
        "    save_manifest()\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"{page_id}: {stage} done\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        stage=stage,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return page\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def is_stage_done(page_id, stage):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    কোনো stage আগেই complete হয়েছে কিনা check করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page is None:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    return bool(page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(stage, False))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def reset_page(page_id, from_stage=None, delete_artifacts=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Page reset করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Args:\n",
        "        page_id: page ID\n",
        "        from_stage: None হলে পুরো page reset\n",
        "                    stage দিলে সেই stage থেকে পরের সব reset\n",
        "        delete_artifacts: True হলে page folder delete\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if delete_artifacts:\n",
        "        page_dir = get_page_dir(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if page_dir.exists():\n",
        "            shutil.rmtree(page_dir, ignore_errors=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        MANGABD_MANIFEST[\"pages\"].pop(page_id, None)\n",
        "        save_manifest()\n",
        "\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"Page deleted: {page_id}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    stages = page.setdefault(\"stages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    if from_stage is None:\n",
        "        # Full reset\n",
        "        for stage_name in PAGE_STAGES:\n",
        "            stages[stage_name] = False\n",
        "\n",
        "        page[\"status\"] = \"reset\"\n",
        "        page[\"errors\"] = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        if from_stage not in PAGE_STAGES:\n",
        "            raise ValueError(\n",
        "                f\"Invalid stage: {from_stage}. \"\n",
        "                f\"Valid stages: {PAGE_STAGES}\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        start_order = STAGE_ORDER[from_stage]\n",
        "\n",
        "        for stage_name, stage_order in STAGE_ORDER.items():\n",
        "            if stage_order >= start_order:\n",
        "                stages[stage_name] = False\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Status আগের completed stage অনুযায়ী\n",
        "        new_status = \"reset\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        for stage_name in reversed(PAGE_STAGES):\n",
        "            if stages.get(stage_name):\n",
        "                new_status = STAGE_STATUS_MAP.get(stage_name, stage_name)\n",
        "                break\n",
        "\n",
        "        page[\"status\"] = new_status\n",
        "\n",
        "    page[\"updated_at\"] = _now()\n",
        "\n",
        "    save_manifest()\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Page reset: {page_id} from_stage={from_stage}\",\n",
        "        level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        from_stage=from_stage,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return page\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Page manager ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 8. Artifact management\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_artifact_path(page_id, artifact_name):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Page artifact path return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if artifact_name not in ARTIFACT_FILENAMES:\n",
        "        raise ValueError(\n",
        "            f\"Invalid artifact name: {artifact_name}. \"\n",
        "            f\"Valid artifacts: {list(ARTIFACT_FILENAMES.keys())}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return Path(page[\"artifacts\"][artifact_name])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_json_artifact(page_id, artifact_name, data):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    JSON artifact atomic save করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Example:\n",
        "        save_json_artifact(page_id, \"detections\", regions)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    path = get_artifact_path(page_id, artifact_name)\n",
        "    atomic_write_json(path, data)\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Saved artifact: {page_id}/{artifact_name}\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        artifact=artifact_name,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return path\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_json_artifact(page_id, artifact_name, default=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    JSON artifact load করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    path = get_artifact_path(page_id, artifact_name)\n",
        "    return load_json_safe(path, default=default)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_image_artifact(page_id, artifact_name, image, quality=95):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Image artifact save করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Args:\n",
        "        image: numpy BGR array বা PIL RGB image\n",
        "        artifact_name: original | inpainted | final | mask | preview\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    path = get_artifact_path(page_id, artifact_name)\n",
        "    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    suffix = path.suffix.lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(image, np.ndarray):\n",
        "        if artifact_name == \"mask\":\n",
        "            cv2.imwrite(str(path), image)\n",
        "        elif suffix in [\".jpg\", \".jpeg\"]:\n",
        "            cv2.imwrite(\n",
        "                str(path),\n",
        "                image,\n",
        "                [int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), int(quality)],\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            cv2.imwrite(str(path), image)\n",
        "\n",
        "    elif isinstance(image, PILImage.Image):\n",
        "        if suffix in [\".jpg\", \".jpeg\"]:\n",
        "            image.save(path, quality=int(quality))\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            image.save(path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        raise TypeError(\n",
        "            \"image must be numpy.ndarray (BGR) or PIL.Image.Image (RGB)\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return path\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_image_artifact(page_id, artifact_name, flags=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Image artifact load করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        numpy array বা None\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    path = get_artifact_path(page_id, artifact_name)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not path.exists():\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if artifact_name == \"mask\":\n",
        "        return cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_UNCHANGED)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if flags is None:\n",
        "        flags = cv2.IMREAD_COLOR\n",
        "\n",
        "    return cv2.imread(str(path), flags)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Artifact manager ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 9. translation_df persistence\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def empty_translation_df():\n",
        "    return pd.DataFrame(columns=TRANSLATION_DF_COLUMNS)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_translation_df(df=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    translation_df CSV + JSON sidecar save করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if df is not None:\n",
        "        translation_df = df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        translation_df = empty_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Column order ensure\n",
        "    for column in TRANSLATION_DF_COLUMNS:\n",
        "        if column not in translation_df.columns:\n",
        "            translation_df[column] = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    translation_df = translation_df[TRANSLATION_DF_COLUMNS]\n",
        "\n",
        "    translation_df.to_csv(\n",
        "        TRANSLATION_CSV,\n",
        "        index=False,\n",
        "        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        translation_df.to_json(\n",
        "            TRANSLATION_JSON,\n",
        "            orient=\"records\",\n",
        "            force_ascii=False,\n",
        "            indent=2,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"translation_df JSON sidecar failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"translation_df saved: {len(translation_df)} rows\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        rows=len(translation_df),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return TRANSLATION_CSV\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_translation_df():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    translation_df load করে। না থাকলে empty DataFrame return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if TRANSLATION_CSV.exists():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            translation_df = pd.read_csv(TRANSLATION_CSV)\n",
        "\n",
        "            for column in TRANSLATION_DF_COLUMNS:\n",
        "                if column not in translation_df.columns:\n",
        "                    translation_df[column] = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            translation_df = translation_df[TRANSLATION_DF_COLUMNS]\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"translation_df loaded: {len(translation_df)} rows\",\n",
        "                level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "                rows=len(translation_df),\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            return translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"translation_df load failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    translation_df = empty_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def upsert_translation_rows(page_id, rows):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর OCR/translation rows DataFrame-এ merge করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Args:\n",
        "        page_id: page ID\n",
        "        rows: list of dict\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        updated translation_df\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        load_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    new_df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(new_df) == 0:\n",
        "        return translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    new_df[\"page_id\"] = page_id\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page is not None:\n",
        "        new_df[\"page\"] = page.get(\"original_filename\", page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Column ensure\n",
        "    for column in TRANSLATION_DF_COLUMNS:\n",
        "        if column not in new_df.columns:\n",
        "            new_df[column] = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    new_df = new_df[TRANSLATION_DF_COLUMNS]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # পুরনো page rows remove\n",
        "    if \"page_id\" in translation_df.columns and len(translation_df) > 0:\n",
        "        translation_df = translation_df[\n",
        "            translation_df[\"page_id\"] != page_id\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    translation_df = pd.concat(\n",
        "        [translation_df, new_df],\n",
        "        ignore_index=True,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    save_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Upserted translation rows: {page_id} ({len(new_df)} rows)\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        rows=len(new_df),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ translation_df persistence ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 10. Initialize manifest + translation_df\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  10. Initializing manifest and translation_df\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "load_manifest()\n",
        "load_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"  ✅ Manifest pages : {len(MANGABD_MANIFEST.get('pages', {}))}\")\n",
        "print(f\"  ✅ Translation rows: {len(translation_df)}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 11. Self-tests\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  11. Self-tests\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "tests = []\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_test(name, condition, details=\"\"):\n",
        "    status = \"✅\" if condition else \"❌\"\n",
        "    print(f\"  {status} {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if details and not condition:\n",
        "        print(f\"     {details}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    tests.append(bool(condition))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 1: manifest exists\n",
        "run_test(\n",
        "    \"Manifest file exists\",\n",
        "    MANIFEST_PATH.exists(),\n",
        "    str(MANIFEST_PATH),\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 2: init test page\n",
        "test_filename = \"__cell3_test__.jpg\"\n",
        "test_image = np.ones((120, 160, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_page_id = init_page(\n",
        "        test_filename,\n",
        "        width=160,\n",
        "        height=120,\n",
        "        image_np=test_image,\n",
        "        overwrite=True,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"init_page creates page\",\n",
        "        get_page(test_page_id) is not None,\n",
        "        test_page_id,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    test_page_id = None\n",
        "    run_test(\"init_page creates page\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 3: original image artifact saved\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    original_path = get_artifact_path(test_page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"Original image artifact saved\",\n",
        "        original_path.exists(),\n",
        "        str(original_path),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"Original image artifact saved\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 4: stage marking\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    mark_stage_done(test_page_id, \"detect\", meta={\"detector\": \"test\"})\n",
        "    mark_stage_done(test_page_id, \"ocr\", meta={\"ocr_engine\": \"test\"})\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = (\n",
        "        is_stage_done(test_page_id, \"detect\")\n",
        "        and is_stage_done(test_page_id, \"ocr\")\n",
        "        and not is_stage_done(test_page_id, \"render\")\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"Stage marking works\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"Stage marking works\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 5: JSON artifact save/load\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_data = {\n",
        "        \"regions\": [\n",
        "            {\n",
        "                \"id\": 1,\n",
        "                \"xyxy\": [10, 20, 100, 80],\n",
        "                \"score\": np.float32(0.91),\n",
        "            }\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    save_json_artifact(test_page_id, \"detections\", test_data)\n",
        "    loaded_data = load_json_artifact(test_page_id, \"detections\", default={})\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = (\n",
        "        loaded_data is not None\n",
        "        and \"regions\" in loaded_data\n",
        "        and loaded_data[\"regions\"][0][\"id\"] == 1\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"JSON artifact save/load\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"JSON artifact save/load\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 6: translation_df upsert/save/load\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_rows = [\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            \"page\": test_filename,\n",
        "            \"id\": 1,\n",
        "            \"region_type\": \"bubble\",\n",
        "            \"detector_class\": \"text_bubble\",\n",
        "            \"detector_score\": 0.91,\n",
        "            \"parent_bubble_id\": \"\",\n",
        "            \"x\": 10,\n",
        "            \"y\": 20,\n",
        "            \"width\": 90,\n",
        "            \"height\": 60,\n",
        "            \"angle\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"original_text\": \"Hello\",\n",
        "            \"translated_text\": \"হ্যালো\",\n",
        "            \"confidence\": 0.9,\n",
        "            \"ocr_engine\": \"test\",\n",
        "            \"translator_engine\": \"manual\",\n",
        "            \"quality_score\": \"\",\n",
        "            \"is_english\": True,\n",
        "            \"bengali_valid\": True,\n",
        "            \"status\": \"ok\",\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    upsert_translation_rows(test_page_id, test_rows)\n",
        "    loaded_df = load_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = (\n",
        "        len(loaded_df) > 0\n",
        "        and any(\n",
        "            (loaded_df[\"page_id\"] == test_page_id)\n",
        "            & (loaded_df[\"id\"] == 1)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"translation_df upsert/save/load\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"translation_df upsert/save/load\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 7: reset page from stage\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    reset_page(test_page_id, from_stage=\"ocr\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = (\n",
        "        is_stage_done(test_page_id, \"detect\")\n",
        "        and not is_stage_done(test_page_id, \"ocr\")\n",
        "        and not is_stage_done(test_page_id, \"render\")\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"reset_page from stage\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"reset_page from stage\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 8: cleanup test page\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    # translation_df থেকে test rows remove\n",
        "    if \"page_id\" in translation_df.columns:\n",
        "        translation_df = translation_df[\n",
        "            translation_df[\"page_id\"] != test_page_id\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "        save_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # manifest + disk থেকে test page delete\n",
        "    reset_page(test_page_id, delete_artifacts=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = get_page(test_page_id) is None\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"Test page cleanup\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"Test page cleanup\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",

        "\n",
        "def run_command(command, timeout=900):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Safe subprocess runner.\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        success: bool\n",
        "        output: str\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        result = subprocess.run(\n",
        "            command,\n",
        "            capture_output=True,\n",
        "            text=True,\n",
        "            timeout=timeout,\n",
        "            check=False,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if result.returncode == 0:\n",
        "            return True, result.stdout.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        return False, (result.stderr.strip() or result.stdout.strip())\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        return False, str(exc)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def pip_install_optional(packages, label):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Optional package installer.\n",
        "    Fail করলে Cell block হবে না।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📦 Installing optional {label}: {len(packages)} packages\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    command = [\n",
        "        sys.executable,\n",
        "        \"-m\",\n",
        "        \"pip\",\n",
        "        \"install\",\n",
        "        \"--quiet\",\n",
        "        \"--disable-pip-version-check\",\n",
        "    ] + packages\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok, output = run_command(command)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if ok:\n",
        "        print(f\"  ✅ {label} installed\")\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    retry_command = [\n",
        "        sys.executable,\n",
        "        \"-m\",\n",
        "        \"pip\",\n",
        "        \"install\",\n",
        "        \"--quiet\",\n",
        "        \"--disable-pip-version-check\",\n",
        "        \"--no-deps\",\n",
        "    ] + packages\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok2, output2 = run_command(retry_command)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if ok2:\n",
        "        print(f\"  ✅ {label} installed with --no-deps\")\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ⚠️ {label} install failed: {output2[:180]}\")\n",
        "    return False\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def download_file(url, destination):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Single file download.\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        bool\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    destination = Path(destination)\n",
        "    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, destination)\n",
        "        return destination.exists() and destination.stat().st_size > 1024\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def download_first_available(urls, destination, label):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একাধিক URL থেকে প্রথম successful file download করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    destination = Path(destination)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if destination.exists() and destination.stat().st_size > 1024:\n",
        "        size_mb = destination.stat().st_size / (1024 * 1024)\n",
        "        print(f\"  ✅ {label} already exists ({size_mb:.2f} MB)\")\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ⬇️ Downloading {label}...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for url in urls:\n",
        "        print(f\"     Trying: {url[:90]}\")\n",
        "        ok = download_file(url, destination)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if ok:\n",
        "            size_mb = destination.stat().st_size / (1024 * 1024)\n",
        "            print(f\"  ✅ {label} downloaded ({size_mb:.2f} MB)\")\n",
        "            return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ❌ {label} download failed\")\n",
        "    return False\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Helper functions ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",

        "print(f\"  Qwen cache   : {QWEN_LOCAL_CACHE}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 3. Async + GPU helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def _run_async(coro):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    zyddnys async functions চালানোর জন্য helper।\n",
        "    Colab/Jupyter event loop-এর সাথে safe।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if loop.is_closed():\n",
        "            raise RuntimeError(\"Event loop closed\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    except RuntimeError:\n",
        "        loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()\n",
        "        asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return loop.run_until_complete(coro)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def clear_gpu_cache():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Python GC + CUDA cache clear।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    gc.collect()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if torch.cuda.is_available():\n",
        "        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            torch.cuda.synchronize()\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def gpu_memory_summary():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    GPU memory status return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not torch.cuda.is_available():\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"device\": \"cpu\",\n",
        "            \"allocated_gb\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"reserved_gb\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"free_gb\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"total_gb\": 0.0,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    allocated = torch.cuda.memory_allocated() / (1024 ** 3)\n",
        "    reserved = torch.cuda.memory_reserved() / (1024 ** 3)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        props = torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0)\n",
        "        total = props.total_memory / (1024 ** 3)\n",
        "        free = total - reserved\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        total = 0.0\n",
        "        free = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"device\": torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),\n",
        "        \"allocated_gb\": round(allocated, 2),\n",
        "        \"reserved_gb\": round(reserved, 2),\n",
        "        \"free_gb\": round(max(0.0, free), 2),\n",
        "        \"total_gb\": round(total, 2),\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def download_url_to_file(url, destination, label=\"file\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Simple file downloader।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    destination = Path(destination)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if destination.exists() and destination.stat().st_size > 1024:\n",
        "        size_mb = destination.stat().st_size / (1024 * 1024)\n",
        "        print(f\"  ✅ {label} already exists ({size_mb:.1f} MB)\")\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ⬇️ Downloading {label}...\")\n",
        "    print(f\"     URL: {url[:90]}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, destination)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if destination.exists() and destination.stat().st_size > 1024:\n",
        "            size_mb = destination.stat().st_size / (1024 * 1024)\n",
        "            print(f\"  ✅ {label} downloaded ({size_mb:.1f} MB)\")\n",
        "            return True\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  ❌ {label} download incomplete\")\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        print(f\"  ❌ {label} download failed: {str(exc)[:160]}\")\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Async + GPU helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 4. Qwen VL OCR wrapper\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def _clean_ocr_text(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Conservative OCR cleaning।\n",
        "    English manga OCR-এর জন্য line break preserve করা হয়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = text.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Multiple spaces/tabs collapse\n",
        "    text = \" \".join(text.split())\n",
        "\n",
        "    return text.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _language_scores(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Simple English/Bengali language score।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"en\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"bn\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"unknown\": 1.0,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    latin = sum(1 for ch in text if ch.isascii() and ch.isalpha())\n",
        "    bengali = sum(1 for ch in text if \"\\u0980\" <= ch <= \"\\u09FF\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    total_letters = latin + bengali\n",
        "\n",
        "    if total_letters == 0:\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"en\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"bn\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"unknown\": 1.0,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    en_score = latin / total_letters\n",
        "    bn_score = bengali / total_letters\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"en\": round(en_score, 3),\n",
        "        \"bn\": round(bn_score, 3),\n",
        "        \"unknown\": round(max(0.0, 1.0 - en_score - bn_score), 3),\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _estimate_ocr_confidence(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Heuristic OCR confidence।\n",
        "    Real token probability না, তবে fake fixed confidence-এর চেয়ে ভালো।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"[OCR_FAILED\" in text:\n",
        "        return 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    score = 0.45\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(text) >= 2:\n",
        "        score += 0.15\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(text) >= 5:\n",
        "        score += 0.10\n",
        "\n",
        "    language = _language_scores(text)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if language[\"en\"] >= 0.60:\n",
        "        score += 0.20\n",
        "    elif language[\"bn\"] >= 0.30:\n",
        "        score += 0.05\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Common hallucination artifacts\n",
        "    bad_chars = [\"{\", \"}\", \"[\", \"]\", \"<\", \">\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not any(ch in text for ch in bad_chars):\n",
        "        score += 0.05\n",
        "\n",
        "    return round(min(score, 0.95), 3)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class QwenVLWrapper:\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Qwen2.5-VL OCR wrapper for MangaBD V12।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns dict:\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            \"text\": str,\n",
        "            \"confidence\": float,\n",
        "            \"engine\": \"qwen\",\n",
        "            \"language_score\": dict,\n",
        "            \"warnings\": list,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __init__(self, model, processor, device, torch_dtype, config):\n",
        "        self.model = model\n",
        "        self.processor = processor\n",
        "        self.device = device\n",
        "        self.torch_dtype = torch_dtype\n",
        "        self.config = config\n",
        "\n",
        "    @torch.no_grad()\n",
        "    def recognize(self, image_np, prompt=None):\n",
        "        warnings_list = []\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            clear_gpu_cache()\n",
        "\n",
        "            if isinstance(image_np, PILImage.Image):\n",
        "                pil_img = image_np.convert(\"RGB\")\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                pil_img = PILImage.fromarray(image_np.astype(np.uint8))\n",
        "\n",
        "            if prompt is None:\n",
        "                prompt = (\n",
        "                    \"Extract all English text visible in this image exactly as written. \"\n",
        "                    \"Do not translate. \"\n",
        "                    \"Do not explain. \"\n",
        "                    \"Preserve readable punctuation. \"\n",
        "                    \"Return only the raw text content. \"\n",
        "                    \"If there is no readable text, return empty string.\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "            messages = [\n",
        "                {\n",
        "                    \"role\": \"user\",\n",
        "                    \"content\": [\n",
        "                        {\"type\": \"image\", \"image\": pil_img},\n",
        "                        {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": prompt},\n",
        "                    ],\n",
        "                }\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "\n",
        "            chat_text = self.processor.apply_chat_template(\n",
        "                messages,\n",
        "                tokenize=False,\n",
        "                add_generation_prompt=True,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            inputs = self.processor(\n",
        "                text=chat_text,\n",
        "                images=[pil_img],\n",
        "                return_tensors=\"pt\",\n",
        "                padding=True,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                model_device = next(self.model.parameters()).device\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                model_device = self.device\n",
        "\n",
        "            prepared_inputs = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "            for key, value in inputs.items():\n",
        "                if isinstance(value, torch.Tensor):\n",
        "                    if value.dtype.is_floating_point:\n",
        "                        prepared_inputs[key] = value.to(\n",
        "                            model_device,\n",
        "                            dtype=self.torch_dtype,\n",
        "                        )\n",
        "                    else:\n",
        "                        prepared_inputs[key] = value.to(model_device)\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    prepared_inputs[key] = value\n",
        "\n",
        "            ocr_config = self.config.get(\"ocr\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "            generate_kwargs = {\n",
        "                \"max_new_tokens\": int(ocr_config.get(\"max_new_tokens\", 512)),\n",
        "                \"do_sample\": bool(ocr_config.get(\"do_sample\", False)),\n",
        "                \"num_beams\": int(ocr_config.get(\"num_beams\", 1)),\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "            output_ids = self.model.generate(\n",
        "                **prepared_inputs,\n",
        "                **generate_kwargs,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            generated = output_ids[:, prepared_inputs[\"input_ids\"].shape[1]:]\n",
        "\n",
        "            text = self.processor.batch_decode(\n",
        "                generated,\n",
        "                skip_special_tokens=True,\n",
        "            )[0].strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "            text = _clean_ocr_text(text)\n",
        "\n",
        "            confidence = _estimate_ocr_confidence(text)\n",
        "            language_score = _language_scores(text)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if language_score[\"en\"] < 0.50 and len(text) > 0:\n",
        "                warnings_list.append(\"OCR output does not look strongly English\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            return {\n",
        "                \"text\": text,\n",
        "                \"confidence\": confidence,\n",
        "                \"engine\": \"qwen\",\n",
        "                \"language_score\": language_score,\n",
        "                \"warnings\": warnings_list,\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            return {\n",
        "                \"text\": f\"[OCR_FAILED: {str(exc)[:40]}]\",\n",
        "                \"confidence\": 0.0,\n",
        "                \"engine\": \"qwen\",\n",
        "                \"language_score\": {\n",
        "                    \"en\": 0.0,\n",
        "                    \"bn\": 0.0,\n",
        "                    \"unknown\": 1.0,\n",
        "                },\n",
        "                \"warnings\": [str(exc)[:200]],\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Qwen VL wrapper ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. ModelManager\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "class ModelManager:\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Central model loader/unloader for MangaBD V12।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    def __init__(self, config):\n",
        "        self.config = config\n",
        "        self.instances = {}\n",
        "        self.load_times = {}\n",
        "        self.errors = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "    # Status\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "\n",
        "    def status(self):\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"device\": DEVICE,\n",
        "            \"torch_dtype\": str(TORCH_DTYPE),\n",
        "            \"loaded\": {\n",
        "                name: True\n",
        "                for name in self.instances.keys()\n",
        "            },\n",
        "            \"load_times\": self.load_times.copy(),\n",
        "            \"gpu\": gpu_memory_summary(),\n",
        "            \"errors\": self.errors.copy(),\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    def print_status(self):\n",
        "        status = self.status()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "        print(\"  Model Manager Status\")\n",
        "        print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  Device      : {status['device']}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  Dtype       : {status['torch_dtype']}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        gpu = status[\"gpu\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if gpu[\"device\"] != \"cpu\":\n",
        "            print(\n",
        "                f\"  GPU memory  : \"\n",
        "                f\"{gpu['allocated_gb']} GB allocated / \"\n",
        "                f\"{gpu['total_gb']} GB total\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"  GPU memory  : CPU only\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        loaded = status[\"loaded\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if loaded:\n",
        "            print(\"  Loaded models:\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            for name in loaded:\n",
        "                load_time = self.load_times.get(name, 0.0)\n",
        "                print(f\"    ✅ {name:10s} ({load_time:.1f}s)\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"  Loaded models: none\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "    # Unload\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "\n",
        "    def unload(self, name):\n",
        "        instance = self.instances.pop(name, None)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if instance is None:\n",
        "            return False\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            if isinstance(instance, dict):\n",
        "                if \"model\" in instance:\n",
        "                    del instance[\"model\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "                if \"processor\" in instance:\n",
        "                    del instance[\"processor\"]\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                if hasattr(instance, \"model\"):\n",
        "                    del instance.model\n",
        "\n",
        "                if hasattr(instance, \"processor\"):\n",
        "                    del instance.processor\n",
        "\n",
        "            del instance\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Model unload cleanup warning [{name}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        clear_gpu_cache()\n",
        "\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"Model unloaded: {name}\",\n",
        "            level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    def unload_all(self, exclude=None):\n",
        "        exclude = set(exclude or [])\n",
        "\n",
        "        for name in list(self.instances.keys()):\n",
        "            if name not in exclude:\n",
        "                self.unload(name)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "    # RT-DETR\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "\n",
        "    def ensure_rtdetr(self, force=False):\n",
        "        if \"rtdetr\" in self.instances and not force:\n",
        "            return self.instances[\"rtdetr\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        start = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            from transformers import (\n",
        "                RTDetrV2ForObjectDetection,\n",
        "                AutoProcessor,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            model_id = self.config[\"detection\"][\"rtdetr_model_id\"]\n",
        "            cache_dir = str(RTDETR_DIR)\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"  📥 Loading RT-DETR: {model_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            model = RTDetrV2ForObjectDetection.from_pretrained(\n",
        "                model_id,\n",
        "                cache_dir=cache_dir,\n",
        "            ).to(DEVICE).eval()\n",
        "\n",
        "            processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(\n",
        "                model_id,\n",
        "                cache_dir=cache_dir,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            instance = {\n",
        "                \"model\": model,\n",
        "                \"processor\": processor,\n",
        "                \"model_id\": model_id,\n",
        "                \"device\": DEVICE,\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "            self.instances[\"rtdetr\"] = instance\n",
        "            self.load_times[\"rtdetr\"] = time.time() - start\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"RT-DETR loaded in {self.load_times['rtdetr']:.1f}s\",\n",
        "                level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"  ✅ RT-DETR loaded in {self.load_times['rtdetr']:.1f}s\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            return instance\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            self.errors[\"rtdetr\"] = str(exc)[:300]\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"RT-DETR load failed: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            raise\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "    # CTD\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "\n",
        "    def ensure_ctd(self, force=False):\n",
        "        if \"ctd\" in self.instances and not force:\n",
        "            return self.instances[\"ctd\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        start = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            # Weight download if missing\n",
        "            if not (\n",
        "                CTD_WEIGHT_PATH.exists()\n",
        "                and CTD_WEIGHT_PATH.stat().st_size > 1024\n",
        "            ):\n",
        "                ok = download_url_to_file(\n",
        "                    CTD_WEIGHT_URL,\n",
        "                    CTD_WEIGHT_PATH,\n",
        "                    \"CTD weight\",\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                if not ok:\n",
        "                    raise RuntimeError(\"CTD weight download failed\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            from manga_translator.detection.ctd import ComicTextDetector\n",
        "\n",
        "            class MangaBDCTD(ComicTextDetector):\n",
        "                \"\"\"\n",
        "                CTD with MangaBD model directory override।\n",
        "                \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "                def _get_file_path(self, *args):\n",
        "                    return os.path.join(self.model_dir, *args)\n",
        "\n",
        "            MangaBDCTD._MODEL_DIR = str(MODELS_DIR)\n",
        "            MangaBDCTD._MODEL_SUB_DIR = \"ctd\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"  📥 Loading CTD\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            detector = MangaBDCTD()\n",
        "            _run_async(detector.load(DEVICE))\n",
        "\n",
        "            self.instances[\"ctd\"] = detector\n",
        "            self.load_times[\"ctd\"] = time.time() - start\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"CTD loaded in {self.load_times['ctd']:.1f}s\",\n",
        "                level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"  ✅ CTD loaded in {self.load_times['ctd']:.1f}s\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            return detector\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            self.errors[\"ctd\"] = str(exc)[:300]\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"CTD load failed: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            raise\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "    # LaMa\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "\n",
        "    def ensure_lama(self, force=False):\n",
        "        if \"lama\" in self.instances and not force:\n",
        "            return self.instances[\"lama\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        start = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            from manga_translator.inpainting.inpainting_lama_mpe import (\n",
        "                LamaLargeInpainter,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            class MangaBDLaMa(LamaLargeInpainter):\n",
        "                \"\"\"\n",
        "                LaMa Large with MangaBD model directory override।\n",
        "                \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            MangaBDLaMa._MODEL_DIR = str(MODELS_DIR)\n",
        "            MangaBDLaMa._MODEL_SUB_DIR = \"lama_large\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\"  📥 Loading LaMa Large\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            inpainter = MangaBDLaMa()\n",
        "            _run_async(inpainter.load(DEVICE))\n",
        "\n",
        "            self.instances[\"lama\"] = inpainter\n",
        "            self.load_times[\"lama\"] = time.time() - start\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"LaMa loaded in {self.load_times['lama']:.1f}s\",\n",
        "                level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"  ✅ LaMa loaded in {self.load_times['lama']:.1f}s\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            return inpainter\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            self.errors[\"lama\"] = str(exc)[:300]\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"LaMa load failed: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            raise\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "    # Qwen VL\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "\n",
        "    def ensure_qwen(self, force=False, unload_others=False):\n",
        "        if \"qwen\" in self.instances and not force:\n",
        "            return self.instances[\"qwen\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if unload_others:\n",
        "            self.unload_all(exclude=[\"qwen\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        start = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            from transformers import AutoProcessor\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                from transformers import (\n",
        "                    Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration as QwenVLModel,\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                print(\"  ℹ️ Using Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            except ImportError:\n",
        "                from transformers import (\n",
        "                    Qwen2VLForConditionalGeneration as QwenVLModel,\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                print(\"  ℹ️ Using Qwen2VLForConditionalGeneration\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            model_id = self.config[\"ocr\"][\"qwen_model_id\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "            hf_token = None\n",
        "\n",
        "            if \"MANGABD_SECRETS\" in globals():\n",
        "                hf_token = MANGABD_SECRETS.get(\"hf_token\") or None\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"  📥 Loading Qwen VL: {model_id}\")\n",
        "            print(\"     Using local HF cache to avoid Drive I/O errors\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            model = QwenVLModel.from_pretrained(\n",
        "                model_id,\n",
        "                cache_dir=str(QWEN_LOCAL_CACHE),\n",
        "                torch_dtype=TORCH_DTYPE,\n",
        "                trust_remote_code=True,\n",
        "                low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n",
        "                device_map=\"auto\" if DEVICE == \"cuda\" else None,\n",
        "                token=hf_token,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            model.eval()\n",
        "\n",
        "            processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(\n",
        "                model_id,\n",
        "                cache_dir=str(QWEN_DRIVE_CACHE),\n",
        "                trust_remote_code=True,\n",
        "                token=hf_token,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            wrapper = QwenVLWrapper(\n",
        "                model=model,\n",
        "                processor=processor,\n",
        "                device=DEVICE,\n",
        "                torch_dtype=TORCH_DTYPE,\n",
        "                config=self.config,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            self.instances[\"qwen\"] = wrapper\n",
        "            self.load_times[\"qwen\"] = time.time() - start\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Qwen VL loaded in {self.load_times['qwen']:.1f}s\",\n",
        "                level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"  ✅ Qwen VL loaded in {self.load_times['qwen']:.1f}s\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            return wrapper\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            self.errors[\"qwen\"] = str(exc)[:300]\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Qwen VL load failed: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            raise\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "    # Future OCR placeholder\n",
        "    # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "\n",
        "    def ensure_ovis(self, force=False):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        Future OvisOCR2 loader placeholder।\n",
        "        এখনো implement করা হয়নি।\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        raise NotImplementedError(\n",
        "            \"OvisOCR2 support এখনো যোগ করা হয়নি। \"\n",
        "            \"ভবিষ্যতে OCR engine plugin হিসেবে যোগ হবে।\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Global model manager\n",
        "model_manager = ModelManager(MANGABD_CONFIG)\n",
        "MANGABD_MODEL_MANAGER = model_manager\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ ModelManager ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. Optional auto-load controls\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "# ডিফল্টভাবে বড় model auto-load বন্ধ।\n",
        "# পরবর্তী pipeline stage অনুযায়ী load হবে।\n",
        "\n",
        "AUTO_LOAD_RTDETR = False\n",
        "AUTO_LOAD_CTD = False\n",
        "AUTO_LOAD_LAMA = False\n",
        "AUTO_LOAD_QWEN = False\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  6. Optional auto-load controls\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"  AUTO_LOAD_RTDETR : {AUTO_LOAD_RTDETR}\")\n",
        "print(f\"  AUTO_LOAD_CTD    : {AUTO_LOAD_CTD}\")\n",
        "print(f\"  AUTO_LOAD_LAMA   : {AUTO_LOAD_LAMA}\")\n",
        "print(f\"  AUTO_LOAD_QWEN   : {AUTO_LOAD_QWEN}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "if AUTO_LOAD_RTDETR:\n",
        "    model_manager.ensure_rtdetr()\n",
        "\n",
        "if AUTO_LOAD_CTD:\n",
        "    model_manager.ensure_ctd()\n",
        "\n",
        "if AUTO_LOAD_LAMA:\n",
        "    model_manager.ensure_lama()\n",
        "\n",
        "if AUTO_LOAD_QWEN:\n",
        "    model_manager.ensure_qwen(unload_others=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 7. Self-tests\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  7. Self-tests\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "tests = []\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_test(name, condition, details=\"\"):\n",
        "    status = \"✅\" if condition else \"❌\"\n",
        "    print(f\"  {status} {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if details and not condition:\n",
        "        print(f\"     {details}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    tests.append(bool(condition))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",

        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"].setdefault(\"ctd_mask_threshold\", 60)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"].setdefault(\"mask_clip_to_regions\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"].setdefault(\"mask_base_dilate_radius\", 6)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"].setdefault(\"bubble_containment_ratio\", 0.55)\n",

        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"classification\"].setdefault(\"narrator_top_ratio\", 0.20)\n",
