        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"classification\"].setdefault(\"narrator_aspect_ratio\", 2.5)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"classification\"].setdefault(\"narrator_width_ratio\", 0.40)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"classification\"].setdefault(\"sfx_area_ratio\", 0.02)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"classification\"].setdefault(\"sfx_white_ratio_max\", 0.30)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Detection config ready\")\n",
        "print(f\"     RT-DETR threshold : {MANGABD_CONFIG['detection']['rtdetr_threshold']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     CTD pixel mask    : {MANGABD_CONFIG['detection']['use_ctd_pixel_mask']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Reading direction : {MANGABD_CONFIG.get('language', {}).get('reading_direction', 'ltr')}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 3. Geometry helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def box_xywh_to_xyxy(box):\n",
        "    x, y, w, h = box\n",
        "    return int(x), int(y), int(x + w), int(y + h)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def box_xyxy_to_xywh(box):\n",
        "    x1, y1, x2, y2 = box\n",
        "    return int(x1), int(y1), int(x2 - x1), int(y2 - y1)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def box_area(box):\n",
        "    x, y, w, h = box\n",
        "    return max(0, int(w)) * max(0, int(h))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def box_intersection_area(box_a, box_b):\n",
        "    ax, ay, aw, ah = box_a\n",
        "    bx, by, bw, bh = box_b\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax1, ay1, ax2, ay2 = box_xywh_to_xyxy((ax, ay, aw, ah))\n",
        "    bx1, by1, bx2, by2 = box_xywh_to_xyxy((bx, by, bw, bh))\n",
        "\n",
        "    x1 = max(ax1, bx1)\n",
        "    y1 = max(ay1, by1)\n",
        "    x2 = min(ax2, bx2)\n",
        "    y2 = min(ay2, by2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return max(0, x2 - x1) * max(0, y2 - y1)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def box_iou(box_a, box_b):\n",
        "    inter = box_intersection_area(box_a, box_b)\n",
        "    area_a = box_area(box_a)\n",
        "    area_b = box_area(box_b)\n",
        "    union = area_a + area_b - inter\n",
        "\n",
        "    if union <= 0:\n",
        "        return 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    return inter / union\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def deduplicate_boxes(boxes, iou_threshold=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Overlapping boxes suppress করে।\n",
        "    Score বেশি হলে priority পায়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not boxes:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if iou_threshold is None:\n",
        "        iou_threshold = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"][\"dedupe_iou_threshold\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    sorted_boxes = sorted(\n",
        "        boxes,\n",
        "        key=lambda item: float(item.get(\"score\", 0.0)),\n",
        "        reverse=True,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    keep = []\n",
        "    suppressed = set()\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i, box_i in enumerate(sorted_boxes):\n",
        "        if i in suppressed:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        keep.append(box_i)\n",
        "\n",
        "        box_i_xywh = (\n",
        "            box_i[\"x\"],\n",
        "            box_i[\"y\"],\n",
        "            box_i[\"w\"],\n",
        "            box_i[\"h\"],\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        for j in range(i + 1, len(sorted_boxes)):\n",
        "            if j in suppressed:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            box_j = sorted_boxes[j]\n",
        "\n",
        "            box_j_xywh = (\n",
        "                box_j[\"x\"],\n",
        "                box_j[\"y\"],\n",
        "                box_j[\"w\"],\n",
        "                box_j[\"h\"],\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            iou = box_iou(box_i_xywh, box_j_xywh)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if iou > iou_threshold:\n",
        "                suppressed.add(j)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return keep\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def sort_regions_by_reading_order(regions):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Reading direction অনুযায়ী regions sort করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    direction = MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"language\", {}).get(\n",
        "        \"reading_direction\",\n",
        "        \"ltr\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if direction == \"rtl\":\n",
        "        regions.sort(\n",
        "            key=lambda r: (\n",
        "                int(r[\"y\"]) // 50,\n",
        "                -int(r[\"x\"]),\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        regions.sort(\n",
        "            key=lambda r: (\n",
        "                int(r[\"y\"]) // 50,\n",
        "                int(r[\"x\"]),\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return regions\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Geometry helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 4. Region classification helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_white_ratio(image_np, x, y, w, h):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        img_h, img_w = image_np.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        x1 = max(0, int(x))\n",
        "        y1 = max(0, int(y))\n",
        "        x2 = min(img_w, int(x + w))\n",
        "        y2 = min(img_h, int(y + h))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if x2 <= x1 or y2 <= y1:\n",
        "            return 0.5\n",
        "\n",
        "        region = image_np[y1:y2, x1:x2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if region.size == 0:\n",
        "            return 0.5\n",
        "\n",
        "        gray = cv2.cvtColor(region, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n",
        "        white_pixels = np.sum(gray > 200)\n",
        "        total_pixels = gray.size\n",
        "\n",
        "        if total_pixels == 0:\n",
        "            return 0.5\n",
        "\n",
        "        return float(white_pixels) / float(total_pixels)\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return 0.5\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_border_variance(image_np, x, y, w, h):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        img_h, img_w = image_np.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        x1 = max(0, int(x))\n",
        "        y1 = max(0, int(y))\n",
        "        x2 = min(img_w, int(x + w))\n",
        "        y2 = min(img_h, int(y + h))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if x2 - x1 < 10 or y2 - y1 < 10:\n",
        "            return 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        region = image_np[y1:y2, x1:x2]\n",
        "        gray = cv2.cvtColor(region, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n",
        "\n",
        "        border_width = 3\n",
        "        h_reg, w_reg = gray.shape\n",
        "\n",
        "        top = gray[0:border_width, :].flatten()\n",
        "        bottom = gray[h_reg - border_width:h_reg, :].flatten()\n",
        "        left = gray[:, 0:border_width].flatten()\n",
        "        right = gray[:, w_reg - border_width:w_reg].flatten()\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_border = np.concatenate([top, bottom, left, right])\n",
        "\n",
        "        if len(all_border) == 0:\n",
        "            return 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        return float(np.var(all_border)) / 10000.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def is_narrator_box(image_np, x, y, w, h):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        img_h, img_w = image_np.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"classification\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        in_top = y < img_h * cfg[\"narrator_top_ratio\"]\n",
        "        in_bottom = (y + h) > img_h * cfg[\"narrator_bottom_ratio\"]\n",
        "        near_edge = in_top or in_bottom\n",
        "\n",
        "        if h <= 0:\n",
        "            return False\n",
        "\n",
        "        aspect_ratio = w / h\n",
        "        is_wide = aspect_ratio > cfg[\"narrator_aspect_ratio\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        width_ratio = w / img_w if img_w > 0 else 0.0\n",
        "        is_broad = width_ratio > cfg[\"narrator_width_ratio\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if near_edge and (is_wide or is_broad):\n",
        "            return True\n",
        "\n",
        "        if aspect_ratio > 4.0 and is_broad:\n",
        "            return True\n",
        "\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def classify_region_heuristic(image_np, x, y, w, h):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Pixel heuristic-based region classification।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"classification\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if is_narrator_box(image_np, x, y, w, h):\n",
        "            return \"narrator\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        white_ratio = get_white_ratio(image_np, x, y, w, h)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if white_ratio > cfg[\"white_ratio_bubble\"]:\n",
        "            border_var = get_border_variance(image_np, x, y, w, h)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if border_var > cfg[\"dotted_border_threshold\"]:\n",
        "                return \"thought\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            return \"bubble\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        if white_ratio > cfg[\"white_ratio_thought\"]:\n",
        "            return \"thought\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        img_h, img_w = image_np.shape[:2]\n",
        "        region_area = w * h\n",
        "        image_area = img_h * img_w\n",
        "\n",
        "        area_ratio = region_area / image_area if image_area > 0 else 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        if (\n",
        "            area_ratio > cfg[\"sfx_area_ratio\"]\n",
        "            and white_ratio < cfg[\"sfx_white_ratio_max\"]\n",
        "            and h > 40\n",
        "            and w > 40\n",
        "        ):\n",
        "            return \"sfx\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        return \"overlay\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return \"overlay\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def classify_region_from_detector(detector_class, image_np, x, y, w, h):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Detector class + heuristic combine করে final region type দেয়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if detector_class == \"text_bubble\":\n",
        "        return \"bubble\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if detector_class == \"bubble\":\n",
        "        return \"bubble\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if detector_class == \"text_free\":\n",
        "        heuristic = classify_region_heuristic(image_np, x, y, w, h)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if heuristic in [\"narrator\", \"sfx\", \"overlay\"]:\n",
        "            return heuristic\n",
        "\n",
        "        return \"overlay\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return classify_region_heuristic(image_np, x, y, w, h)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Classification helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. RT-DETR detection\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def rtdetr_detect(image_bgr, rtdetr_instance=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    RT-DETR-v2 detection।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            \"boxes\": [...],\n",
        "            \"bubble_boxes\": [...],\n",
        "            \"text_bubble_boxes\": [...],\n",
        "            \"text_free_boxes\": [...],\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if rtdetr_instance is None:\n",
        "        rtdetr_instance = model_manager.ensure_rtdetr()\n",
        "\n",
        "    model = rtdetr_instance[\"model\"]\n",
        "    processor = rtdetr_instance[\"processor\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    image_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)\n",
        "    pil_img = PILImage.fromarray(image_rgb)\n",
        "\n",
        "    inputs = processor(images=pil_img, return_tensors=\"pt\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        model_device = next(model.parameters()).device\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        model_device = DEVICE\n",
        "\n",
        "    prepared_inputs = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key, value in inputs.items():\n",
        "        if isinstance(value, torch.Tensor):\n",
        "            prepared_inputs[key] = value.to(model_device)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            prepared_inputs[key] = value\n",
        "\n",
        "    outputs = model(**prepared_inputs)\n",
        "\n",
        "    target_sizes = torch.tensor([pil_img.size[::-1]])\n",
        "\n",
        "    threshold = float(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"][\"rtdetr_threshold\"]\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = processor.post_process_object_detection(\n",
        "        outputs,\n",
        "        threshold=threshold,\n",
        "        target_sizes=target_sizes,\n",
        "    )[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    class_names = {\n",
        "        0: \"bubble\",\n",
        "        1: \"text_bubble\",\n",
        "        2: \"text_free\",\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    min_w = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"][\"min_region_width\"])\n",
        "    min_h = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"][\"min_region_height\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    boxes = []\n",
        "    bubble_boxes = []\n",
        "    text_bubble_boxes = []\n",
        "    text_free_boxes = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    scores = results[\"scores\"].cpu().numpy()\n",
        "    labels = results[\"labels\"].cpu().numpy()\n",
        "    raw_boxes = results[\"boxes\"].cpu().numpy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    for score, label, box in zip(scores, labels, raw_boxes):\n",
        "        x1, y1, x2, y2 = box.astype(int)\n",
        "\n",
        "        w = int(x2 - x1)\n",
        "        h = int(y2 - y1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if w < min_w or h < min_h:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        class_name = class_names.get(int(label), \"unknown\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        item = {\n",
        "            \"x\": int(x1),\n",
        "            \"y\": int(y1),\n",
        "            \"w\": int(w),\n",
        "            \"h\": int(h),\n",
        "            \"score\": float(score),\n",
        "            \"detector_class\": class_name,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        boxes.append(item)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if class_name == \"bubble\":\n",
        "            bubble_boxes.append(item)\n",
        "        elif class_name == \"text_bubble\":\n",
        "            text_bubble_boxes.append(item)\n",
        "        elif class_name == \"text_free\":\n",
        "            text_free_boxes.append(item)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"boxes\": boxes,\n",
        "        \"bubble_boxes\": bubble_boxes,\n",
        "        \"text_bubble_boxes\": text_bubble_boxes,\n",
        "        \"text_free_boxes\": text_free_boxes,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ RT-DETR detection function ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. CTD fallback detection\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def ctd_detect_boxes_and_mask(image_bgr):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    CTD fallback detection।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            \"boxes\": [...],\n",
        "            \"raw_mask\": numpy or None,\n",
        "            \"textlines\": list,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    detector = model_manager.ensure_ctd()\n",
        "\n",
        "    detection_cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    detection_size = int(detection_cfg.get(\"ctd_detection_size\", 1024))\n",
        "    text_threshold = float(detection_cfg.get(\"ctd_text_threshold\", 0.30))\n",
        "    box_threshold = float(detection_cfg.get(\"ctd_box_threshold\", 0.60))\n",
        "    unclip_ratio = float(detection_cfg.get(\"ctd_unclip_ratio\", 1.50))\n",
        "\n",
        "    result = _run_async(\n",
        "        detector.detect(\n",
        "            image_bgr,\n",
        "            detection_size,\n",
        "            text_threshold,\n",
        "            box_threshold,\n",
        "            unclip_ratio,\n",
        "            False,  # invert\n",
        "            False,  # gamma_correct\n",
        "            False,  # rotate\n",
        "            False,  # auto_rotate\n",
        "            False,  # verbose\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    textlines = []\n",
        "    raw_mask = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if isinstance(result, tuple):\n",
        "        if len(result) >= 1:\n",
        "            textlines = result[0] or []\n",
        "\n",
        "        if len(result) >= 2:\n",
        "            raw_mask = result[1]\n",
        "\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        textlines = result or []\n",
        "\n",
        "    boxes = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    min_w = int(detection_cfg[\"min_region_width\"])\n",
        "    min_h = int(detection_cfg[\"min_region_height\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    for textline in textlines:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            pts = getattr(textline, \"pts\", None)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if pts is None:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            pts = np.array(pts)\n",
        "\n",
        "            x1 = int(np.min(pts[:, 0]))\n",
        "            y1 = int(np.min(pts[:, 1]))\n",
        "            x2 = int(np.max(pts[:, 0]))\n",
        "            y2 = int(np.max(pts[:, 1]))\n",
        "\n",
        "            w = x2 - x1\n",
        "            h = y2 - y1\n",
        "\n",
        "            if w < min_w or h < min_h:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            boxes.append({\n",
        "                \"x\": x1,\n",
        "                \"y\": y1,\n",
        "                \"w\": w,\n",
        "                \"h\": h,\n",
        "                \"score\": 0.50,\n",
        "                \"detector_class\": \"ctd_textline\",\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"boxes\": boxes,\n",
        "        \"raw_mask\": raw_mask,\n",
        "        \"textlines\": textlines,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ CTD fallback function ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 7. Bubble grouping\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def assign_parent_bubbles(text_boxes, bubble_boxes):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    text box কোন bubble-এর ভেতরে আছে তা assign করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        list of parent_bubble_id or None\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    parents = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    containment_ratio = float(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"][\"bubble_containment_ratio\"]\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    for text_box in text_boxes:\n",
        "        text_xywh = (\n",
        "            text_box[\"x\"],\n",
        "            text_box[\"y\"],\n",
        "            text_box[\"w\"],\n",
        "            text_box[\"h\"],\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        text_area = box_area(text_xywh)\n",
        "\n",
        "        best_parent = None\n",
        "        best_parent_area = None\n",
        "        best_overlap = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        for bubble_index, bubble_box in enumerate(bubble_boxes):\n",
        "            bubble_xywh = (\n",
        "                bubble_box[\"x\"],\n",
        "                bubble_box[\"y\"],\n",
        "                bubble_box[\"w\"],\n",
        "                bubble_box[\"h\"],\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            inter = box_intersection_area(text_xywh, bubble_xywh)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if text_area <= 0:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            overlap_ratio = inter / text_area\n",
        "\n",
        "            if overlap_ratio < containment_ratio:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            bubble_area = box_area(bubble_xywh)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # সবচেয়ে ছোট containing bubble বেছে নিই\n",
        "            if (\n",
        "                best_parent is None\n",
        "                or bubble_area < best_parent_area\n",
        "            ):\n",
        "                best_parent = bubble_index + 1\n",
        "                best_parent_area = bubble_area\n",
        "                best_overlap = overlap_ratio\n",
        "\n",
        "        parents.append(best_parent)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return parents\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Bubble grouping ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 8. Mask helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def clean_binary_mask(mask, min_area=8):\n",
        "    if mask is None or mask.size == 0:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    _, binary = cv2.threshold(mask, 128, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n",
        "\n",
        "    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (3, 3))\n",
        "    closed = cv2.morphologyEx(binary, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel, iterations=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    num_labels, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(closed)\n",
        "\n",
        "    cleaned = np.zeros_like(closed)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if num_labels > 1:\n",
        "        for i in range(1, num_labels):\n",
        "            area = stats[i, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA]\n",
        "\n",
        "            if area >= min_area:\n",
        "                cleaned[labels == i] = 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    return cleaned\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def adaptive_dilate_mask(mask, textlines=None, base_radius=None):\n",
        "    if mask is None or mask.size == 0:\n",
        "        return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "    if base_radius is None:\n",
        "        base_radius = int(\n",
        "            MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"][\"mask_base_dilate_radius\"]\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    radius = base_radius\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        dist = cv2.distanceTransform(mask, cv2.DIST_L2, 5)\n",
        "        max_stroke_half = float(dist.max())\n",
        "        adaptive_radius = max(2, int(max_stroke_half * 0.7))\n",
        "        radius = max(radius, min(adaptive_radius, 15))\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(\n",
        "        cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,\n",
        "        (2 * radius + 1, 2 * radius + 1),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    dilated = cv2.dilate(mask, kernel, iterations=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return dilated\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_mask_from_regions(image_bgr, regions, padding=6):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Bounding box mask fallback।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "    mask = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for region in regions:\n",
        "        x = int(region[\"x\"])\n",
        "        y = int(region[\"y\"])\n",
        "        rw = int(region[\"width\"])\n",
        "        rh = int(region[\"height\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        x1 = max(0, x - padding)\n",
        "        y1 = max(0, y - padding)\n",
        "        x2 = min(w, x + rw + padding)\n",
        "        y2 = min(h, y + rh + padding)\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.rectangle(mask, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), 255, -1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_page_text_mask(\n",
        "    image_bgr,\n",
        "    regions,\n",
        "    raw_mask=None,\n",
        "    textlines=None,\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Final page text mask তৈরি করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Priority:\n",
        "        1. CTD raw pixel mask\n",
        "        2. bounding box mask fallback\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "    detection_cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if (\n",
        "        raw_mask is not None\n",
        "        and raw_mask.size > 0\n",
        "        and np.sum(raw_mask > 0) > 0\n",
        "    ):\n",
        "        threshold = int(detection_cfg.get(\"ctd_mask_threshold\", 60))\n",
        "\n",
        "        mask = (raw_mask > threshold).astype(np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "        kernel_close = cv2.getStructuringElement(\n",
        "            cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,\n",
        "            (5, 5),\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        mask = cv2.morphologyEx(\n",
        "            mask,\n",
        "            cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,\n",
        "            kernel_close,\n",
        "            iterations=2,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        mask = clean_binary_mask(mask, min_area=3)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if mask is None:\n",
        "            mask = build_mask_from_regions(image_bgr, regions)\n",
        "\n",
        "        mask = adaptive_dilate_mask(mask, textlines=textlines)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if detection_cfg.get(\"mask_clip_to_regions\", True):\n",
        "            region_mask = build_mask_from_regions(\n",
        "                image_bgr,\n",
        "                regions,\n",
        "                padding=10,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            clipped = cv2.bitwise_and(mask, region_mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if np.sum(clipped > 0) > 0:\n",
        "                mask = clipped\n",
        "\n",
        "        return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Fallback bbox mask\n",
        "    mask = build_mask_from_regions(image_bgr, regions, padding=6)\n",
        "    mask = clean_binary_mask(mask, min_area=8)\n",
        "    mask = adaptive_dilate_mask(mask, textlines=textlines)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Mask helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 9. Crop helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def crop_region(image_np, region, padding=4):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        x = int(region[\"x\"])\n",
        "        y = int(region[\"y\"])\n",
        "        w = int(region[\"width\"])\n",
        "        h = int(region[\"height\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        img_h, img_w = image_np.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        x1 = max(0, x - padding)\n",
        "        y1 = max(0, y - padding)\n",
        "        x2 = min(img_w, x + w + padding)\n",
        "        y2 = min(img_h, y + h + padding)\n",
        "\n",
        "        return image_np[y1:y2, x1:x2].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return np.zeros((10, 10, 3), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def crop_bubble_with_context(image_np, region, context_ratio=0.15):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        x = int(region[\"x\"])\n",
        "        y = int(region[\"y\"])\n",
        "        w = int(region[\"width\"])\n",
        "        h = int(region[\"height\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        img_h, img_w = image_np.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        expand_x = int(max(8, min(100, w * context_ratio)))\n",
        "        expand_y = int(max(8, min(100, h * context_ratio)))\n",
        "\n",
        "        x1 = max(0, x - expand_x)\n",
        "        y1 = max(0, y - expand_y)\n",
        "        x2 = min(img_w, x + w + expand_x)\n",
        "        y2 = min(img_h, y + h + expand_y)\n",
        "\n",
        "        crop = image_np[y1:y2, x1:x2].copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if crop.shape[0] < 5 or crop.shape[1] < 5:\n",
        "            return crop\n",
        "\n",
        "        border = max(8, min(30, w // 8))\n",
        "\n",
        "        region_type = str(region.get(\"region_type\", \"bubble\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if region_type in [\"sfx\", \"overlay\"]:\n",
        "            crop = cv2.copyMakeBorder(\n",
        "                crop,\n",
        "                border,\n",
        "                border,\n",
        "                border,\n",
        "                border,\n",
        "                cv2.BORDER_REPLICATE,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            crop = cv2.copyMakeBorder(\n",
        "                crop,\n",
        "                border,\n",
        "                border,\n",
        "                border,\n",
        "                border,\n",
        "                cv2.BORDER_CONSTANT,\n",
        "                value=(255, 255, 255),\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return crop\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return crop_region(image_np, region, padding=4)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def crop_region_rotated(image_np, region, padding=4):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        quad = region.get(\"quad\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if quad is None:\n",
        "            return crop_region(image_np, region, padding)\n",
        "\n",
        "        pts = np.array(quad, dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "\n",
        "        width = int(max(\n",
        "            np.linalg.norm(pts[0] - pts[1]),\n",
        "            np.linalg.norm(pts[2] - pts[3]),\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        height = int(max(\n",
        "            np.linalg.norm(pts[0] - pts[3]),\n",
        "            np.linalg.norm(pts[1] - pts[2]),\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if width < 5 or height < 5:\n",
        "            return crop_region(image_np, region, padding)\n",
        "\n",
        "        dst_pts = np.array([\n",
        "            [0, 0],\n",
        "            [width - 1, 0],\n",
        "            [width - 1, height - 1],\n",
        "            [0, height - 1],\n",
        "        ], dtype=np.float32)\n",
        "\n",
        "        matrix = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(pts, dst_pts)\n",
        "        cropped = cv2.warpPerspective(image_np, matrix, (width, height))\n",
        "\n",
        "        return cropped\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return crop_region(image_np, region, padding)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Crop helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 10. Visualization\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def visualize_detections(image_np, regions, max_display_width=1024):\n",
        "    vis = image_np.copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    colors = {\n",
        "        \"bubble\": (0, 255, 0),\n",
        "        \"thought\": (255, 255, 0),\n",
        "        \"narrator\": (0, 165, 255),\n",
        "        \"sfx\": (0, 0, 255),\n",
        "        \"overlay\": (255, 0, 255),\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i, region in enumerate(regions):\n",
        "        region_type = region.get(\"region_type\", \"overlay\")\n",
        "        color = colors.get(region_type, (200, 200, 200))\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = int(region[\"x\"])\n",
        "        y = int(region[\"y\"])\n",
        "        w = int(region[\"width\"])\n",
        "        h = int(region[\"height\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.rectangle(vis, (x, y), (x + w, y + h), color, 2)\n",
        "\n",
        "        score = float(region.get(\"detector_score\", 0.0))\n",
        "        label = f\"#{i + 1} {region_type} {score:.2f}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.rectangle(\n",
        "            vis,\n",
        "            (x, max(0, y - 18)),\n",
        "            (x + len(label) * 7, y),\n",
        "            color,\n",
        "            -1,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.putText(\n",
        "            vis,\n",
        "            label,\n",
        "            (x + 2, max(12, y - 4)),\n",
        "            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,\n",
        "            0.4,\n",
        "            (255, 255, 255),\n",
        "            1,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    h, w = vis.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if w > max_display_width:\n",
        "        scale = max_display_width / w\n",
        "        new_w = int(w * scale)\n",
        "        new_h = int(h * scale)\n",
        "        vis = cv2.resize(vis, (new_w, new_h))\n",
        "\n",
        "    return vis\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Visualization helper ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 11. Main detection service\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def detect_page(page_id, image_bgr, force=False, return_mask=True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Production detection pipeline for one page.\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        regions, mask\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Cache check\n",
        "    if is_stage_done(page_id, \"detect\") and not force:\n",
        "        print(f\"    ✅ Detection cached: {page_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        regions = load_json_artifact(page_id, \"detections\", default=[])\n",
        "\n",
        "        mask = None\n",
        "\n",
        "        if return_mask:\n",
        "            mask = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"mask\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        return regions, mask\n",
        "\n",
        "    img_h, img_w = image_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "    update_page_meta(\n",
        "        page_id,\n",
        "        width=img_w,\n",
        "        height=img_h,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    🔎 RT-DETR detection...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    rtdetr_result = rtdetr_detect(image_bgr)\n",
        "\n",
        "    text_bubble_boxes = rtdetr_result[\"text_bubble_boxes\"]\n",
        "    text_free_boxes = rtdetr_result[\"text_free_boxes\"]\n",
        "    bubble_boxes = rtdetr_result[\"bubble_boxes\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"    🎯 RT-DETR: \"\n",
        "        f\"text_bubble={len(text_bubble_boxes)}, \"\n",
        "        f\"text_free={len(text_free_boxes)}, \"\n",
        "        f\"bubble={len(bubble_boxes)}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    candidate_boxes = list(text_bubble_boxes) + list(text_free_boxes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ctd_result = None\n",
        "    ctd_raw_mask = None\n",
        "    ctd_textlines = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    # CTD fallback যদি RT-DETR কিছু না পায়\n",
        "    if not candidate_boxes:\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ RT-DETR found nothing — CTD fallback...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            ctd_result = ctd_detect_boxes_and_mask(image_bgr)\n",
        "            candidate_boxes = ctd_result[\"boxes\"]\n",
        "            ctd_raw_mask = ctd_result[\"raw_mask\"]\n",
        "            ctd_textlines = ctd_result[\"textlines\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"    ✅ CTD fallback: {len(candidate_boxes)} boxes\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"CTD fallback failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Optional CTD pixel mask, যদি RT-DETR boxes থাকে\n",
        "    if (\n",
        "        candidate_boxes\n",
        "        and MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"].get(\"use_ctd_pixel_mask\", True)\n",
        "        and ctd_raw_mask is None\n",
        "    ):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            print(\"    🔎 CTD pixel mask generation...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            ctd_result = ctd_detect_boxes_and_mask(image_bgr)\n",
        "            ctd_raw_mask = ctd_result[\"raw_mask\"]\n",
        "            ctd_textlines = ctd_result[\"textlines\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "            if ctd_raw_mask is not None and np.sum(ctd_raw_mask > 0) > 0:\n",
        "                print(\n",
        "                    f\"    ✅ CTD pixel mask: \"\n",
        "                    f\"{np.sum(ctd_raw_mask > 0):,} text pixels\"\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                print(\"    ⚠️ CTD pixel mask empty\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"CTD pixel mask failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not candidate_boxes:\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ No text regions detected\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        regions = []\n",
        "        mask = np.zeros((img_h, img_w), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "        save_json_artifact(page_id, \"detections\", regions)\n",
        "        save_image_artifact(page_id, \"mask\", mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "        mark_stage_done(\n",
        "            page_id,\n",
        "            \"detect\",\n",
        "            meta={\n",
        "                \"region_count\": 0,\n",
        "                \"detector\": \"none\",\n",
        "            },\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if return_mask:\n",
        "            return regions, mask\n",
        "\n",
        "        return regions, None\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Deduplicate\n",
        "    candidate_boxes = deduplicate_boxes(candidate_boxes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Parent bubble assignment\n",
        "    parents = assign_parent_bubbles(candidate_boxes, bubble_boxes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Build regions\n",
        "    regions = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i, box in enumerate(candidate_boxes):\n",
        "        x = int(box[\"x\"])\n",
        "        y = int(box[\"y\"])\n",
        "        w = int(box[\"w\"])\n",
        "        h = int(box[\"h\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        detector_class = str(box.get(\"detector_class\", \"unknown\"))\n",
        "        detector_score = float(box.get(\"score\", 0.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        region_type = classify_region_from_detector(\n",
        "            detector_class,\n",
        "            image_bgr,\n",
        "            x,\n",
        "            y,\n",
        "            w,\n",
        "            h,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        quad = np.array([\n",
        "            [x, y],\n",
        "            [x + w, y],\n",
        "            [x + w, y + h],\n",
        "            [x, y + h],\n",
        "        ], dtype=np.int32)\n",
        "\n",
        "        angle = 0.0\n",
        "\n",
        "        regions.append({\n",
        "            \"id\": i + 1,\n",
        "            \"page_id\": page_id,\n",
        "            \"x\": x,\n",
        "            \"y\": y,\n",
        "            \"width\": w,\n",
        "            \"height\": h,\n",
        "            \"quad\": quad.tolist(),\n",
        "            \"angle\": angle,\n",
        "            \"region_type\": region_type,\n",
        "            \"detector_class\": detector_class,\n",
        "            \"detector_score\": detector_score,\n",
        "            \"parent_bubble_id\": parents[i] if i < len(parents) else None,\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Sort\n",
        "    regions = sort_regions_by_reading_order(regions)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Reassign IDs after sorting\n",
        "    for new_id, region in enumerate(regions, start=1):\n",
        "        region[\"id\"] = new_id\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Mask\n",
        "    mask = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if return_mask:\n",
        "        mask = build_page_text_mask(\n",
        "            image_bgr,\n",
        "            regions,\n",
        "            raw_mask=ctd_raw_mask,\n",
        "            textlines=ctd_textlines,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        save_image_artifact(page_id, \"mask\", mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Save detections\n",
        "    save_json_artifact(page_id, \"detections\", regions)\n",
        "\n",
        "    mark_stage_done(\n",
        "        page_id,\n",
        "        \"detect\",\n",
        "        meta={\n",
        "            \"region_count\": len(regions),\n",
        "            \"rtdetr_text_bubble\": len(text_bubble_boxes),\n",
        "            \"rtdetr_text_free\": len(text_free_boxes),\n",
        "            \"rtdetr_bubble\": len(bubble_boxes),\n",
        "            \"ctd_fallback_used\": ctd_result is not None and not text_bubble_boxes and not text_free_boxes,\n",
        "            \"ctd_mask_used\": ctd_raw_mask is not None,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Detection completed [{page_id}]: {len(regions)} regions\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        regions=len(regions),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    ✅ {len(regions)} regions prepared\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if return_mask:\n",
        "        return regions, mask\n",
        "\n",
        "    return regions, None\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Main detection service ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 12. Self-tests\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  12. Self-tests\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "tests = []\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_test(name, condition, details=\"\"):\n",
        "    status = \"✅\" if condition else \"❌\"\n",
        "    print(f\"  {status} {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if details and not condition:\n",
        "        print(f\"     {details}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    tests.append(bool(condition))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 1: deduplicate boxes\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    boxes = [\n",
        "        {\"x\": 10, \"y\": 10, \"w\": 100, \"h\": 50, \"score\": 0.9, \"detector_class\": \"text_bubble\"},\n",
        "        {\"x\": 12, \"y\": 12, \"w\": 100, \"h\": 50, \"score\": 0.7, \"detector_class\": \"text_bubble\"},\n",
        "        {\"x\": 300, \"y\": 300, \"w\": 80, \"h\": 40, \"score\": 0.8, \"detector_class\": \"text_free\"},\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    deduped = deduplicate_boxes(boxes, iou_threshold=0.5)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"deduplicate_boxes\",\n",
        "        len(deduped) == 2,\n",
        "        f\"got {len(deduped)} boxes\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"deduplicate_boxes\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 2: classification white bubble\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    white_img = np.ones((400, 400, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "    result = classify_region_heuristic(white_img, 50, 50, 150, 80)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"classify white region as bubble\",\n",
        "        result == \"bubble\",\n",
        "        result,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"classify white region as bubble\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 3: narrator box\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    page_img = np.ones((1000, 700, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 220\n",
        "    result = classify_region_heuristic(page_img, 10, 5, 600, 50)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"classify top wide region as narrator\",\n",
        "        result == \"narrator\",\n",
        "        result,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"classify top wide region as narrator\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 4: crop region\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    img = np.random.randint(0, 255, (200, 200, 3), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "    region = {\n",
        "        \"x\": 50,\n",
        "        \"y\": 50,\n",
        "        \"width\": 100,\n",
        "        \"height\": 100,\n",
        "        \"region_type\": \"bubble\",\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    crop = crop_region(img, region)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"crop_region\",\n",
        "        crop.shape[0] > 0 and crop.shape[1] > 0,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"crop_region\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 5: reading order LTR\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    regions = [\n",
        "        {\"x\": 500, \"y\": 10, \"width\": 10, \"height\": 10},\n",
        "        {\"x\": 100, \"y\": 10, \"width\": 10, \"height\": 10},\n",
        "        {\"x\": 300, \"y\": 10, \"width\": 10, \"height\": 10},\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    old_direction = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"language\"][\"reading_direction\"]\n",
        "    MANGABD_CONFIG[\"language\"][\"reading_direction\"] = \"ltr\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    sorted_regions = sort_regions_by_reading_order(regions)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = sorted_regions[0][\"x\"] == 100\n",
        "\n",
        "    MANGABD_CONFIG[\"language\"][\"reading_direction\"] = old_direction\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"reading order LTR\",\n",
        "        ok,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"reading order LTR\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 6: mask from regions\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    img = np.ones((300, 300, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    regions = [\n",
        "        {\"x\": 50, \"y\": 50, \"width\": 100, \"height\": 50},\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask = build_mask_from_regions(img, regions)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"mask from regions\",\n",
        "        mask.shape == (300, 300) and np.sum(mask > 0) > 0,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"mask from regions\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 7: parent bubble assignment\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    text_boxes = [\n",
        "        {\"x\": 110, \"y\": 110, \"w\": 80, \"h\": 30},\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    bubble_boxes = [\n",
        "        {\"x\": 100, \"y\": 100, \"w\": 100, \"h\": 50},\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    parents = assign_parent_bubbles(text_boxes, bubble_boxes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"parent bubble assignment\",\n",
        "        parents[0] == 1,\n",
        "        str(parents),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"parent bubble assignment\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 13. Optional live detection test\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "# এটা True করলে RT-DETR/CTD load হবে এবং synthetic image-এ test হবে।\n",
        "# ডিফল্ট False, কারণ model download/load সময় নিতে পারে।\n",
        "\n",
        "RUN_LIVE_DETECTION_TEST = False\n",
        "\n",
        "if RUN_LIVE_DETECTION_TEST:\n",
        "    print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "    print(\"  13. Optional live detection test\")\n",
        "    print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        test_page_id = \"__cell6_live_test__.jpg\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        test_image = np.ones((600, 800, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.putText(\n",
        "            test_image,\n",
        "            \"Hello MangaBD\",\n",
        "            (80, 150),\n",
        "            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,\n",
        "            1.5,\n",
        "            (0, 0, 0),\n",
        "            4,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.putText(\n",
        "            test_image,\n",
        "            \"V12 Production\",\n",
        "            (80, 350),\n",
        "            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,\n",
        "            1.5,\n",
        "            (0, 0, 0),\n",
        "            4,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Test page init\n",
        "        if \"init_page\" in globals():\n",
        "            init_page(\n",
        "                test_page_id,\n",
        "                width=800,\n",
        "                height=600,\n",
        "                image_np=test_image,\n",
        "                overwrite=True,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        regions, mask = detect_page(\n",
        "            test_page_id,\n",
        "            test_image,\n",
        "            force=True,\n",
        "            return_mask=True,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  Live test regions: {len(regions)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if mask is not None:\n",
        "            print(f\"  Live test mask pixels: {np.sum(mask > 0)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Cleanup\n",
        "        if \"reset_page\" in globals():\n",
        "            reset_page(test_page_id, delete_artifacts=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        run_test(\n",
        "            \"Live detection test\",\n",
        "            isinstance(regions, list),\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        run_test(\n",
        "            \"Live detection test\",\n",
        "            False,\n",
        "            str(exc)[:200],\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# Final status\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "all_tests_passed = all(tests)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "if all_tests_passed:\n",
        "    print(\"  Cell 6 result: SUCCESS\")\n",
        "    print(\"  Next: Cell 7 — OCR Service (Qwen deterministic + page OCR)\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"  Cell 6 result: NEEDS FIX\")\n",
        "    print(\"  কিছু self-test fail করেছে। উপরের output দেখুন।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "log_event(\n",
        "    \"Cell 6 completed\",\n",
        "    level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "    tests_passed=sum(tests),\n",
        "    tests_total=len(tests),\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "if not all_tests_passed:\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"Cell 6 self-tests failed. Fix errors before moving to Cell 7.\")"
      ]
