        "# MangaBD V12 — Cell 7: OCR Service\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# Features:\n",
        "# - Qwen deterministic OCR\n",
        "# - OCR preprocessing\n",
        "# - English language scoring\n",
        "# - confidence heuristic\n",
        "# - OCR artifact cache\n",
        "# - translation_df row generation\n",
        "# - checkpoint-aware resume\n",
        "# - low confidence / non-English flagging\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import cv2\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  MangaBD V12 — Cell 7: OCR Service\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 1. Dependency check\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"MANGABD_CONFIG\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"MANGABD_CONFIG not found. আগে Cell 2 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"log_event\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"log_event not found. আগে Cell 2 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"model_manager\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"model_manager not found. আগে Cell 5 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"get_page\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"Storage functions not found. আগে Cell 3 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"crop_bubble_with_context\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"Detection crop helpers not found. আগে Cell 6 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  1. Dependency check\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ MANGABD_CONFIG found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ log_event found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ model_manager found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ storage functions found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ detection crop helpers found\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 2. OCR config defaults\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  2. OCR config defaults\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG.setdefault(\"ocr\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].setdefault(\"engine\", \"qwen\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].setdefault(\"min_confidence\", 0.50)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].setdefault(\"confidence_threshold\", 0.65)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].setdefault(\"min_crop_height\", 40)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].setdefault(\"min_crop_width\", 10)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].setdefault(\"skip_empty_regions\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].setdefault(\"english_score_threshold\", 0.50)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].setdefault(\n",
        "    \"default_prompt\",\n",
        "    (\n",
        "        \"Extract all English text visible in this image exactly as written. \"\n",
        "        \"Do not translate. \"\n",
        "        \"Do not explain. \"\n",
        "        \"Preserve readable punctuation. \"\n",
        "        \"Return only the raw text content. \"\n",
        "        \"If there is no readable text, return empty string.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].setdefault(\"preprocess\", {})\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"][\"preprocess\"].setdefault(\"upscale_small\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"][\"preprocess\"].setdefault(\"denoise\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"][\"preprocess\"].setdefault(\"clahe\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"][\"preprocess\"].setdefault(\"pad\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"][\"preprocess\"].setdefault(\"pad_color\", [255, 255, 255])\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ OCR config ready\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Engine              : {MANGABD_CONFIG['ocr']['engine']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Min confidence      : {MANGABD_CONFIG['ocr']['min_confidence']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Confidence threshold: {MANGABD_CONFIG['ocr']['confidence_threshold']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     English threshold   : {MANGABD_CONFIG['ocr']['english_score_threshold']}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 3. OCR text helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def clean_ocr_output(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Qwen OCR output conservative clean করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = str(text).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"[OCR_FAILED\" in text:\n",
        "        return text\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Remove common model prefixes\n",
        "    text = re.sub(\n",
        "        r\"^(Extracted text|Text|OCR|Raw text|Output)\\s*[:\\-]?\\s*\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        text,\n",
        "        flags=re.IGNORECASE,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Remove surrounding quotes/backticks\n",
        "    text = text.strip(\"`\")\n",
        "    text = text.strip('\"')\n",
        "    text = text.strip(\"'\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Collapse spaces/tabs but preserve meaningful text\n",
        "    text = re.sub(r\"[ \\t]+\", \" \", text)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return text.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def is_english_text(text, threshold=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Text মূলত English কিনা check করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if threshold is None:\n",
        "        threshold = float(\n",
        "            MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].get(\"english_score_threshold\", 0.50)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"[OCR_FAILED\" in text:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"_language_scores\" in globals():\n",
        "        scores = _language_scores(text)\n",
        "        return float(scores.get(\"en\", 0.0)) >= threshold\n",
        "\n",
        "    latin = sum(1 for ch in text if ch.isascii() and ch.isalpha())\n",
        "    bengali = sum(1 for ch in text if \"\\u0980\" <= ch <= \"\\u09FF\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    total = latin + bengali\n",
        "\n",
        "    if total == 0:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    return (latin / total) >= threshold\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_ocr_status(text, confidence, is_english):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    OCR row status decide করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return \"empty\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"[OCR_FAILED\" in text:\n",
        "        return \"failed\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"[EMPTY]\" in text:\n",
        "        return \"empty\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    min_conf = float(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].get(\"min_confidence\", 0.50))\n",
        "    conf_threshold = float(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].get(\"confidence_threshold\", 0.65)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if confidence < min_conf:\n",
        "        return \"failed\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if confidence < conf_threshold:\n",
        "        return \"low_confidence\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not is_english:\n",
        "        return \"non_english\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return \"ok\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ OCR text helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 4. OCR preprocessing\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def preprocess_for_ocr(image_bgr, region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    OCR-এর আগে image preprocess করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Input:\n",
        "        image_bgr: BGR numpy array\n",
        "\n",
        "    Output:\n",
        "        RGB numpy array\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if image_bgr is None or image_bgr.size == 0:\n",
        "            return np.zeros((40, 100, 3), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "        cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"][\"preprocess\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # BGR → RGB\n",
        "        if len(image_bgr.shape) == 3 and image_bgr.shape[2] == 3:\n",
        "            img = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            img = image_bgr.copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "        h, w = img.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Upscale small crops\n",
        "        min_h = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].get(\"min_crop_height\", 40))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cfg.get(\"upscale_small\", True) and h < min_h and h > 0:\n",
        "            scale = max(2.0, float(min_h) / float(h))\n",
        "            new_w = max(1, int(w * scale))\n",
        "            new_h = max(1, int(h * scale))\n",
        "\n",
        "            img = cv2.resize(\n",
        "                img,\n",
        "                (new_w, new_h),\n",
        "                interpolation=cv2.INTER_CUBIC,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Light denoise\n",
        "        if cfg.get(\"denoise\", True):\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                img = cv2.bilateralFilter(\n",
        "                    img,\n",
        "                    d=5,\n",
        "                    sigmaColor=50,\n",
        "                    sigmaSpace=50,\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Contrast boost\n",
        "        if cfg.get(\"clahe\", True):\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                lab = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_RGB2LAB)\n",
        "                clahe = cv2.createCLAHE(\n",
        "                    clipLimit=2.0,\n",
        "                    tileGridSize=(8, 8),\n",
        "                )\n",
        "                lab[:, :, 0] = clahe.apply(lab[:, :, 0])\n",
        "                img = cv2.cvtColor(lab, cv2.COLOR_LAB2RGB)\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Padding\n",
        "        if cfg.get(\"pad\", True):\n",
        "            h, w = img.shape[:2]\n",
        "            pad = max(4, min(h, w) // 10)\n",
        "\n",
        "            pad_color = tuple(cfg.get(\"pad_color\", [255, 255, 255]))\n",
        "\n",
        "            if region_type in [\"sfx\", \"overlay\"]:\n",
        "                img = cv2.copyMakeBorder(\n",
        "                    img,\n",
        "                    pad,\n",
        "                    pad,\n",
        "                    pad,\n",
        "                    pad,\n",
        "                    cv2.BORDER_REPLICATE,\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                img = cv2.copyMakeBorder(\n",
        "                    img,\n",
        "                    pad,\n",
        "                    pad,\n",
        "                    pad,\n",
        "                    pad,\n",
        "                    cv2.BORDER_CONSTANT,\n",
        "                    value=pad_color,\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return img\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"OCR preprocess failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if image_bgr is not None and image_bgr.size > 0:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                return cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                return image_bgr\n",
        "\n",
        "        return np.zeros((40, 100, 3), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ OCR preprocessing ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. Single region OCR\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def ocr_region(image_bgr, region, page_id=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা region-এর উপর OCR চালায়।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        dict:\n",
        "            {\n",
        "                \"text\": str,\n",
        "                \"confidence\": float,\n",
        "                \"engine\": str,\n",
        "                \"language_score\": dict,\n",
        "                \"warnings\": list,\n",
        "                \"is_english\": bool,\n",
        "                \"status\": str,\n",
        "            }\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    region_type = str(region.get(\"region_type\", \"bubble\"))\n",
        "    angle = float(region.get(\"angle\", 0.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Crop\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if abs(angle) > 5.0 and \"crop_region_rotated\" in globals():\n",
        "            crop = crop_region_rotated(image_bgr, region)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            crop = crop_bubble_with_context(\n",
        "                image_bgr,\n",
        "                region,\n",
        "                context_ratio=0.15,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        crop = np.zeros((10, 10, 3), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"OCR crop failed [{page_id}:{region.get('id', '?')}]: {str(exc)[:100]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    min_h = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].get(\"min_crop_height\", 40))\n",
        "    min_w = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].get(\"min_crop_width\", 10))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if (\n",
        "        crop is None\n",
        "        or crop.size == 0\n",
        "        or crop.shape[0] < 5\n",
        "        or crop.shape[1] < 5\n",
        "    ):\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"text\": \"[EMPTY]\",\n",
        "            \"confidence\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"engine\": \"none\",\n",
        "            \"language_score\": {\n",
        "                \"en\": 0.0,\n",
        "                \"bn\": 0.0,\n",
        "                \"unknown\": 1.0,\n",
        "            },\n",
        "            \"warnings\": [\"crop too small\"],\n",
        "            \"is_english\": False,\n",
        "            \"status\": \"empty\",\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Preprocess\n",
        "    preprocessed = preprocess_for_ocr(crop, region_type=region_type)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Load Qwen\n",
        "    qwen = model_manager.ensure_qwen()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Recognize\n",
        "    prompt = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].get(\"default_prompt\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    result = qwen.recognize(preprocessed, prompt=prompt)\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = clean_ocr_output(result.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n",
        "    confidence = float(result.get(\"confidence\", 0.0))\n",
        "    language_score = result.get(\n",
        "        \"language_score\",\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            \"en\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"bn\": 0.0,\n",
        "            \"unknown\": 1.0,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    warnings_list = list(result.get(\"warnings\", []))\n",
        "\n",
        "    is_english = is_english_text(text)\n",
        "\n",
        "    status = get_ocr_status(text, confidence, is_english)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if status == \"low_confidence\":\n",
        "        warnings_list.append(\"low OCR confidence\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if status == \"non_english\":\n",
        "        warnings_list.append(\"OCR output does not look English\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"text\": text,\n",
        "        \"confidence\": confidence,\n",
        "        \"engine\": str(result.get(\"engine\", \"qwen\")),\n",
        "        \"language_score\": language_score,\n",
        "        \"warnings\": warnings_list,\n",
        "        \"is_english\": bool(is_english),\n",
        "        \"status\": status,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Single region OCR ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. OCR row builder\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_ocr_row(page_id, region, ocr_result):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    translation_df-এর জন্য production-ready row তৈরি করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    original_filename = page_id\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page is not None:\n",
        "        original_filename = page.get(\"original_filename\", page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    status = str(ocr_result.get(\"status\", \"ok\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "    row = {\n",
        "        \"page_id\": page_id,\n",
        "        \"page\": original_filename,\n",
        "        \"id\": int(region.get(\"id\", 0)),\n",
        "        \"region_type\": str(region.get(\"region_type\", \"overlay\")),\n",
        "        \"detector_class\": str(region.get(\"detector_class\", \"\")),\n",
        "        \"detector_score\": float(region.get(\"detector_score\", 0.0)),\n",
        "        \"parent_bubble_id\": region.get(\"parent_bubble_id\", \"\"),\n",
        "        \"x\": int(region.get(\"x\", 0)),\n",
        "        \"y\": int(region.get(\"y\", 0)),\n",
        "        \"width\": int(region.get(\"width\", 0)),\n",
        "        \"height\": int(region.get(\"height\", 0)),\n",
        "        \"angle\": float(region.get(\"angle\", 0.0)),\n",
        "        \"original_text\": str(ocr_result.get(\"text\", \"\")),\n",
        "        \"translated_text\": \"\",\n",
        "        \"confidence\": float(ocr_result.get(\"confidence\", 0.0)),\n",
        "        \"ocr_engine\": str(ocr_result.get(\"engine\", \"qwen\")),\n",
        "        \"translator_engine\": \"\",\n",
        "        \"quality_score\": \"\",\n",
        "        \"is_english\": bool(ocr_result.get(\"is_english\", False)),\n",
        "        \"bengali_valid\": False,\n",
        "        \"status\": status,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    return row\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ OCR row builder ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 7. Page OCR pipeline\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def ocr_page(page_id, image_bgr=None, force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর সব region OCR করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        ocr_results list\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Cache check\n",
        "    if is_stage_done(page_id, \"ocr\") and not force:\n",
        "        print(f\"    ✅ OCR cached: {page_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        ocr_data = load_json_artifact(page_id, \"ocr\", default=[])\n",
        "\n",
        "        # যদি translation_df-এ এই page-এর rows না থাকে, rebuild\n",
        "        if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None:\n",
        "            page_exists = False\n",
        "\n",
        "            if len(translation_df) > 0 and \"page_id\" in translation_df.columns:\n",
        "                page_exists = any(translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not page_exists and ocr_data:\n",
        "                rows = []\n",
        "\n",
        "                for item in ocr_data:\n",
        "                    region = item.get(\"region\", {})\n",
        "                    ocr_result = item.get(\"ocr\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "                    rows.append(\n",
        "                        build_ocr_row(page_id, region, ocr_result)\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "\n",
        "                if rows:\n",
        "                    upsert_translation_rows(page_id, rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "        return ocr_data\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Image load\n",
        "    if image_bgr is None:\n",
        "        image_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if image_bgr is None:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "            f\"Original image not found for page: {page_id}. \"\n",
        "            f\"Upload/original artifact দরকার।\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Regions load\n",
        "    regions = load_json_artifact(page_id, \"detections\", default=[])\n",
        "\n",
        "    # যদি detection না হয়ে থাকে, detection service call\n",
        "    if not regions and \"detect_page\" in globals():\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ No detections found — running detection first\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        regions, _ = detect_page(\n",
        "            page_id,\n",
        "            image_bgr,\n",
        "            force=False,\n",
        "            return_mask=False,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not regions:\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ No regions to OCR\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        save_json_artifact(page_id, \"ocr\", [])\n",
        "\n",
        "        mark_stage_done(\n",
        "            page_id,\n",
        "            \"ocr\",\n",
        "            meta={\n",
        "                \"ocr_region_count\": 0,\n",
        "                \"ocr_engine\": MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].get(\"engine\", \"qwen\"),\n",
        "            },\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    📖 OCR processing {len(regions)} regions...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    ocr_results = []\n",
        "    rows = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    status_counts = {\n",
        "        \"ok\": 0,\n",
        "        \"low_confidence\": 0,\n",
        "        \"non_english\": 0,\n",
        "        \"failed\": 0,\n",
        "        \"empty\": 0,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    for index, region in enumerate(regions, start=1):\n",
        "        region_id = region.get(\"id\", index)\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            ocr_result = ocr_region(\n",
        "                image_bgr,\n",
        "                region,\n",
        "                page_id=page_id,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            ocr_results.append({\n",
        "                \"region\": region,\n",
        "                \"ocr\": ocr_result,\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "            row = build_ocr_row(page_id, region, ocr_result)\n",
        "            rows.append(row)\n",
        "\n",
        "            status = str(ocr_result.get(\"status\", \"ok\"))\n",
        "            status_counts[status] = status_counts.get(status, 0) + 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            text_preview = str(ocr_result.get(\"text\", \"\"))[:35]\n",
        "\n",
        "            confidence = float(ocr_result.get(\"confidence\", 0.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "            status_icon = \"✅\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            if status == \"low_confidence\":\n",
        "                status_icon = \"🟡\"\n",
        "            elif status == \"non_english\":\n",
        "                status_icon = \"🟠\"\n",
        "            elif status == \"failed\":\n",
        "                status_icon = \"❌\"\n",
        "            elif status == \"empty\":\n",
        "                status_icon = \"⚪\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\n",
        "                f\"      [{index:2d}/{len(regions)}] \"\n",
        "                f\"{str(region.get('region_type', 'overlay')):8s} \"\n",
        "                f\"conf={confidence:.2f} \"\n",
        "                f\"{status_icon} \"\n",
        "                f\"'{text_preview}'\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"OCR region failed [{page_id}:{region_id}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            failed_result = {\n",
        "                \"text\": f\"[OCR_FAILED: {str(exc)[:30]}]\",\n",
        "                \"confidence\": 0.0,\n",
        "                \"engine\": \"qwen\",\n",
        "                \"language_score\": {\n",
        "                    \"en\": 0.0,\n",
        "                    \"bn\": 0.0,\n",
        "                    \"unknown\": 1.0,\n",
        "                },\n",
        "                \"warnings\": [str(exc)[:200]],\n",
        "                \"is_english\": False,\n",
        "                \"status\": \"failed\",\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "            ocr_results.append({\n",
        "                \"region\": region,\n",
        "                \"ocr\": failed_result,\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "            rows.append(\n",
        "                build_ocr_row(page_id, region, failed_result)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            status_counts[\"failed\"] += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Save OCR artifact\n",
        "    save_json_artifact(page_id, \"ocr\", ocr_results)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Update translation_df\n",
        "    if rows:\n",
        "        upsert_translation_rows(page_id, rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Mark stage done\n",
        "    mark_stage_done(\n",
        "        page_id,\n",
        "        \"ocr\",\n",
        "        meta={\n",
        "            \"ocr_region_count\": len(rows),\n",
        "            \"ocr_engine\": MANGABD_CONFIG[\"ocr\"].get(\"engine\", \"qwen\"),\n",
        "            \"ocr_status_counts\": status_counts,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"OCR completed [{page_id}]: {len(rows)} rows\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        rows=len(rows),\n",
        "        status_counts=status_counts,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"    ✅ OCR done: \"\n",
        "        f\"ok={status_counts.get('ok', 0)}, \"\n",
        "        f\"low={status_counts.get('low_confidence', 0)}, \"\n",
        "        f\"non_en={status_counts.get('non_english', 0)}, \"\n",
        "        f\"failed={status_counts.get('failed', 0)}, \"\n",
        "        f\"empty={status_counts.get('empty', 0)}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return ocr_results\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def ocr_all_pages(force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Manifest-এর সব detected page OCR করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"MANGABD_MANIFEST\" not in globals():\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"MANGABD_MANIFEST not found. আগে Cell 3 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    detected_pages = [\n",
        "        page for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"detect\", False)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not detected_pages:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No detected pages found. আগে detection চালান।\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📂 {len(detected_pages)} pages OCR হবে\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page_index, page in enumerate(detected_pages, start=1):\n",
        "        page_id = page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╔═══════════════════════════════════════════╗\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ║  [{page_index}/{len(detected_pages)}] {page_id}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╚═══════════════════════════════════════════╝\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            page_results = ocr_page(page_id, force=force)\n",
        "            results.append(page_results)\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"OCR page failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            add_page_error(page_id, \"ocr\", str(exc)[:300])\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return results\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Page OCR pipeline ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",

        "def run_test(name, condition, details=\"\"):\n",
        "    status = \"✅\" if condition else \"❌\"\n",
        "    print(f\"  {status} {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if details and not condition:\n",
        "        print(f\"     {details}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    tests.append(bool(condition))\n",
        "\n",

        "# Test 1: clean OCR output\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    cleaned = clean_ocr_output(\"  Text: Hello World  \")\n",

        "        cleaned == \"Hello World\",\n",

        "    )\n",

        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 2: English detection\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    ok_en = is_english_text(\"Hello World\")\n",
        "    ok_bn = is_english_text(\"হ্যালো বিশ্ব\")\n",

        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",

        "\n",

        "# Test 3: OCR status\n",
        "try:\n",

        "\n",

        "        and status_failed == \"failed\"\n",

        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"get_ocr_status\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
