        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 2. Translation config defaults\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  2. Translation config defaults\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG.setdefault(\"translation\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"engine\", \"manual\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"source_lang\", \"en\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"target_lang\", \"bn\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"max_text_length\", 2000)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"save_json_sidecar\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"manual_export_format\", \"ai_text\")\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"gemini_model\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"openai_model\", \"gpt-4o-mini\")\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"retry\", {})\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"retry\"].setdefault(\"enabled\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"retry\"].setdefault(\"max_retries\", 3)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"retry\"].setdefault(\"backoff_seconds\", 2)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"prompt\", {})\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"prompt\"].setdefault(\n",
        "    \"role\",\n",
        "    \"professional English-to-Bengali manga translator\",\n",
        ")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"prompt\"].setdefault(\"return_only_translation\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"prompt\"].setdefault(\"no_quotes\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"prompt\"].setdefault(\"no_explanation\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"prompt\"].setdefault(\"region_type_aware\", True)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"nllb\", {})\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"nllb\"].setdefault(\n",
        "    \"model_id\",\n",
        "    \"facebook/nllb-200-distilled-600M\",\n",
        ")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"nllb\"].setdefault(\"source_lang_code\", \"eng_Latn\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"nllb\"].setdefault(\"target_lang_code\", \"ben_Beng\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"nllb\"].setdefault(\"num_beams\", 4)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"nllb\"].setdefault(\"max_length\", 512)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Glossary\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"use_glossary\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"use_sfx_glossary\", True)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\"glossary\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].setdefault(\n",
        "    \"sfx_glossary\",\n",
        "    {\n",
        "        \"BOOM\": \"বুম!\",\n",
        "        \"BAM\": \"ব্যাম!\",\n",
        "        \"BANG\": \"ব্যাং!\",\n",
        "        \"CRASH\": \"ক্র্যাশ!\",\n",
        "        \"THUD\": \"ধপাস!\",\n",
        "        \"SLAM\": \"স্ল্যাম!\",\n",
        "        \"WHOOSH\": \"উশ!\",\n",
        "        \"CLICK\": \"ক্লিক!\",\n",
        "        \"RING\": \"রিং!\",\n",
        "        \"ROAR\": \"গর্জন!\",\n",
        "        \"GASP\": \"হাঁপ!\",\n",
        "        \"SIGH\": \"দীর্ঘশ্বাস!\",\n",
        "    },\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Translation config ready\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Engine       : {MANGABD_CONFIG['translation']['engine']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Source       : {MANGABD_CONFIG['translation']['source_lang']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Target       : {MANGABD_CONFIG['translation']['target_lang']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Gemini model : {MANGABD_CONFIG['translation']['gemini_model']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     OpenAI model : {MANGABD_CONFIG['translation']['openai_model']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     NLLB model   : {MANGABD_CONFIG['translation']['nllb']['model_id']}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 3. Text helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def validate_bengali(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    অন্তত একটি Bengali character আছে কিনা check করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    return any(\"\\u0980\" <= ch <= \"\\u09FF\" for ch in str(text))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def clean_translation(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Translation output clean করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = str(text).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Remove surrounding quotes\n",
        "    if len(text) >= 2:\n",
        "        if (text.startswith('\"') and text.endswith('\"')) or \\\n",
        "           (text.startswith(\"'\") and text.endswith(\"'\")):\n",
        "            text = text[1:-1].strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Remove common prefixes\n",
        "    text = re.sub(\n",
        "        r\"^(Translation|Bengali|Bangla|Translated text|Output)\\s*[:\\-]?\\s*\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        text,\n",
        "        flags=re.IGNORECASE,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Remove surrounding quotes again after prefix removal\n",
        "    if len(text) >= 2:\n",
        "        if (text.startswith('\"') and text.endswith('\"')) or \\\n",
        "           (text.startswith(\"'\") and text.endswith(\"'\")):\n",
        "            text = text[1:-1].strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Normalize whitespace\n",
        "    text = re.sub(r\"[ \\t]+\", \" \", text)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return text.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def is_special_marker(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    OCR/translation special marker কিনা।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    markers = {\n",
        "        \"[EMPTY]\",\n",
        "        \"[OCR_FAILED]\",\n",
        "        \"[SFX]\",\n",
        "        \"[SKIP:OCR_FAILED]\",\n",
        "        \"[TRANS_FAILED]\",\n",
        "        \"[UNTRANSLATED]\",\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(text).strip() in markers\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_translation_status(translated_text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Translation status decide করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not translated_text:\n",
        "        return \"untranslated\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if is_special_marker(translated_text):\n",
        "        return \"marker\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if validate_bengali(translated_text):\n",
        "        return \"translated\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return \"non_bengali\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Text helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 4. Prompt builder\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "REGION_TYPE_INSTRUCTIONS = {\n",
        "    \"bubble\": \"This is spoken dialogue. Use natural, conversational Bengali.\",\n",
        "    \"thought\": \"This is an inner thought. Use natural monologue-style Bengali.\",\n",
        "    \"narrator\": \"This is narration. Use clear, slightly literary Bengali.\",\n",
        "    \"sfx\": \"This is a sound effect. Use a short, impactful Bengali sound expression.\",\n",
        "    \"overlay\": \"This is a sign/label/overlay. Use concise Bengali.\",\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_translation_prompt(text, region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Region-type-aware translation prompt তৈরি করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"]\n",
        "    prompt_cfg = cfg.get(\"prompt\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    role = prompt_cfg.get(\n",
        "        \"role\",\n",
        "        \"professional English-to-Bengali manga translator\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    region_instruction = REGION_TYPE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(\n",
        "        region_type,\n",
        "        REGION_TYPE_INSTRUCTIONS[\"bubble\"],\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    rules = [\n",
        "        \"Translate the following English manga text into natural Bengali.\",\n",
        "        \"Return ONLY the Bengali translation.\",\n",
        "        \"No explanation.\",\n",
        "        \"No quotes.\",\n",
        "        \"No prefix.\",\n",
        "        \"Do not transliterate unnecessarily.\",\n",
        "        \"Keep the tone suitable for manga.\",\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if prompt_cfg.get(\"region_type_aware\", True):\n",
        "        rules.append(region_instruction)\n",
        "\n",
        "    glossary_lines = build_glossary_prompt_lines(region_type)\n",
        "\n",
        "    prompt_parts = [\n",
        "        f\"You are a {role}.\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        f\"Region type: {region_type}\",\n",
        "        \"\",\n",
        "        \"Rules:\",\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    for rule in rules:\n",
        "        prompt_parts.append(f\"- {rule}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if glossary_lines:\n",
        "        prompt_parts.append(\"\")\n",
        "        prompt_parts.append(\"Glossary:\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        for line in glossary_lines:\n",
        "            prompt_parts.append(f\"- {line}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    prompt_parts.append(\"\")\n",
        "    prompt_parts.append(\"English text:\")\n",
        "    prompt_parts.append(str(text))\n",
        "    prompt_parts.append(\"\")\n",
        "    prompt_parts.append(\"Bengali translation:\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return \"\\n\".join(prompt_parts).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_glossary_prompt_lines(region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Glossary কে prompt line-এ convert করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if cfg.get(\"use_glossary\", True):\n",
        "        glossary = cfg.get(\"glossary\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "        for english, bengali in glossary.items():\n",
        "            if english and bengali:\n",
        "                lines.append(f\"{english} → {bengali}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if region_type == \"sfx\" and cfg.get(\"use_sfx_glossary\", True):\n",
        "        sfx_glossary = cfg.get(\"sfx_glossary\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "        for english, bengali in sfx_glossary.items():\n",
        "            if english and bengali:\n",
        "                lines.append(f\"{english} → {bengali}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return lines\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def apply_sfx_glossary(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Exact SFX glossary match থাকলে সরাসরি return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not cfg.get(\"use_sfx_glossary\", True):\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    sfx_glossary = cfg.get(\"sfx_glossary\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    cleaned = str(text).strip()\n",
        "    upper = cleaned.upper()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if cleaned in sfx_glossary:\n",
        "        return sfx_glossary[cleaned]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if upper in sfx_glossary:\n",
        "        return sfx_glossary[upper]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Punctuation strip match\n",
        "    stripped = re.sub(r\"[^A-Za-z]\", \"\", cleaned).upper()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if stripped in sfx_glossary:\n",
        "        return sfx_glossary[stripped]\n",
        "\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Prompt builder ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. Retry helper\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def translate_with_retry(translate_func, text, region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Retry/backoff সহ translation call করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"]\n",
        "    retry_cfg = cfg.get(\"retry\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    enabled = retry_cfg.get(\"enabled\", True)\n",
        "    max_retries = int(retry_cfg.get(\"max_retries\", 3))\n",
        "    backoff = float(retry_cfg.get(\"backoff_seconds\", 2))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not enabled:\n",
        "        max_retries = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    last_error = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    for attempt in range(max_retries + 1):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            result = translate_func(text, region_type)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if result and str(result).strip():\n",
        "                return clean_translation(result)\n",
        "\n",
        "            last_error = \"empty translation\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            last_error = str(exc)[:200]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if attempt < max_retries:\n",
        "            sleep_time = backoff * (attempt + 1)\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Translation retry {attempt + 1}/{max_retries} \"\n",
        "                f\"after {sleep_time:.1f}s — {last_error[:80]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            time.sleep(sleep_time)\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Translation failed after retries: {last_error[:120]}\",\n",
        "        level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Retry helper ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. Gemini translator\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def translate_with_gemini(text, region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Gemini API দিয়ে translate করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    api_key = MANGABD_SECRETS.get(\"gemini_api_key\", \"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not api_key:\n",
        "        log_event(\"Gemini API key not set\", level=\"WARN\")\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from google import genai\n",
        "\n",
        "        client = genai.Client(api_key=api_key)\n",
        "\n",
        "        model_id = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].get(\n",
        "            \"gemini_model\",\n",
        "            \"gemini-2.5-flash\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        prompt = build_translation_prompt(text, region_type=region_type)\n",
        "\n",
        "        response = client.models.generate_content(\n",
        "            model=model_id,\n",
        "            contents=prompt,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        translated = response.text.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        return clean_translation(translated)\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"Gemini translation failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Gemini translator ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 7. ChatGPT/OpenAI translator\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def translate_with_chatgpt(text, region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    OpenAI ChatGPT API দিয়ে translate করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    api_key = MANGABD_SECRETS.get(\"openai_api_key\", \"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not api_key:\n",
        "        log_event(\"OpenAI API key not set\", level=\"WARN\")\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from openai import OpenAI\n",
        "\n",
        "        client = OpenAI(api_key=api_key)\n",
        "\n",
        "        model_id = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].get(\n",
        "            \"openai_model\",\n",
        "            \"gpt-4o-mini\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        prompt = build_translation_prompt(text, region_type=region_type)\n",
        "\n",
        "        response = client.chat.completions.create(\n",
        "            model=model_id,\n",
        "            messages=[\n",
        "                {\n",
        "                    \"role\": \"system\",\n",
        "                    \"content\": (\n",
        "                        \"You are a professional English-to-Bengali manga translator. \"\n",
        "                        \"Return only the Bengali translation.\"\n",
        "                    ),\n",
        "                },\n",
        "                {\n",
        "                    \"role\": \"user\",\n",
        "                    \"content\": prompt,\n",
        "                },\n",
        "            ],\n",
        "            temperature=0.3,\n",
        "            max_tokens=512,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        translated = response.choices[0].message.content.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        return clean_translation(translated)\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"ChatGPT translation failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ ChatGPT translator ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 8. NLLB translator\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_nllb_bengali_bos_id(tokenizer):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    NLLB Bengali forced BOS token ID বের করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    target_lang_code = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"nllb\"].get(\n",
        "        \"target_lang_code\",\n",
        "        \"ben_Beng\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if hasattr(tokenizer, \"lang_code_to_id\"):\n",
        "            bos_id = tokenizer.lang_code_to_id.get(target_lang_code)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if bos_id is not None:\n",
        "                return int(bos_id)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        bos_id = tokenizer.convert_tokens_to_ids(target_lang_code)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if bos_id is not None and bos_id != tokenizer.unk_token_id:\n",
        "            return int(bos_id)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Known fallback for NLLB-200 ben_Beng\n",
        "    return 100362\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def ensure_nllb():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    NLLB model load করে।\n",
        "    ModelManager থাকলে সেখানে cache করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"model_manager\" in globals():\n",
        "        existing = model_manager.instances.get(\"nllb\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if existing is not None:\n",
        "            return existing\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForSeq2SeqLM\n",
        "\n",
        "        nllb_cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"nllb\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        model_id = nllb_cfg.get(\n",
        "            \"model_id\",\n",
        "            \"facebook/nllb-200-distilled-600M\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        cache_dir = str(\n",
        "            Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"models\"]) / \"nllb\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        Path(cache_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # GPU memory খালি করার জন্য অন্য model unload\n",
        "        if \"model_manager\" in globals():\n",
        "            model_manager.unload_all(exclude=[\"nllb\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  📥 Loading NLLB: {model_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\n",
        "            model_id,\n",
        "            cache_dir=cache_dir,\n",
        "            src_lang=nllb_cfg.get(\"source_lang_code\", \"eng_Latn\"),\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        model = AutoModelForSeq2SeqLM.from_pretrained(\n",
        "            model_id,\n",
        "            cache_dir=cache_dir,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        device = MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"runtime\", {}).get(\"device\", \"cpu\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        model = model.to(device).eval()\n",
        "\n",
        "        instance = {\n",
        "            \"model\": model,\n",
        "            \"tokenizer\": tokenizer,\n",
        "            \"model_id\": model_id,\n",
        "            \"device\": device,\n",
        "            \"bengali_bos_id\": get_nllb_bengali_bos_id(tokenizer),\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"model_manager\" in globals():\n",
        "            model_manager.instances[\"nllb\"] = instance\n",
        "\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"NLLB loaded: {model_id}\",\n",
        "            level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  ✅ NLLB loaded on {device}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        return instance\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"NLLB load failed: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        raise\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def translate_with_nllb(text, region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    NLLB দিয়ে translate করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        import torch\n",
        "\n",
        "        instance = ensure_nllb()\n",
        "\n",
        "        model = instance[\"model\"]\n",
        "        tokenizer = instance[\"tokenizer\"]\n",
        "        device = instance[\"device\"]\n",
        "        forced_bos_id = instance[\"bengali_bos_id\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        nllb_cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"nllb\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        inputs = tokenizer(\n",
        "            str(text),\n",
        "            return_tensors=\"pt\",\n",
        "            max_length=int(nllb_cfg.get(\"max_length\", 512)),\n",
        "            truncation=True,\n",
        "            src_lang=nllb_cfg.get(\"source_lang_code\", \"eng_Latn\"),\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if device == \"cuda\":\n",
        "            inputs = {\n",
        "                key: value.to(device)\n",
        "                for key, value in inputs.items()\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "        with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "            output = model.generate(\n",
        "                **inputs,\n",
        "                forced_bos_token_id=forced_bos_id,\n",
        "                max_length=int(nllb_cfg.get(\"max_length\", 512)),\n",
        "                num_beams=int(nllb_cfg.get(\"num_beams\", 4)),\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        translated = tokenizer.batch_decode(\n",
        "            output,\n",
        "            skip_special_tokens=True,\n",
        "        )[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        return clean_translation(translated)\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"NLLB translation failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ NLLB translator ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 9. Unified translation dispatcher\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def translate_text(text, engine=None, region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Unified translation dispatcher।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        (translated_text, engine_used, warnings)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    warnings_list = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if engine is None:\n",
        "        engine = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].get(\"engine\", \"manual\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text or not str(text).strip():\n",
        "        return \"\", engine, [\"empty text\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = str(text).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if is_special_marker(text):\n",
        "        return text, engine, [\"special marker\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Manual engine এখানে auto translation করে না\n",
        "    if engine == \"manual\":\n",
        "        return \"\", \"manual\", [\"manual translation required\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # SFX glossary exact match\n",
        "    if region_type == \"sfx\":\n",
        "        glossary_result = apply_sfx_glossary(text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if glossary_result:\n",
        "            return glossary_result, \"sfx_glossary\", []\n",
        "\n",
        "    max_text_length = int(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].get(\"max_text_length\", 2000)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(text) > max_text_length:\n",
        "        text = text[:max_text_length]\n",
        "        warnings_list.append(\"text truncated\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if engine == \"gemini\":\n",
        "        result = translate_with_retry(\n",
        "            translate_with_gemini,\n",
        "            text,\n",
        "            region_type=region_type,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    elif engine == \"chatgpt\":\n",
        "        result = translate_with_retry(\n",
        "            translate_with_chatgpt,\n",
        "            text,\n",
        "            region_type=region_type,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    elif engine == \"nllb\":\n",
        "        result = translate_with_retry(\n",
        "            translate_with_nllb,\n",
        "            text,\n",
        "            region_type=region_type,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        warnings_list.append(f\"unknown engine: {engine}\")\n",
        "        result = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not result:\n",
        "        return \"\", engine, warnings_list + [\"translation failed\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    return result, engine, warnings_list\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def switch_translator(engine):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Translator engine switch করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    valid_engines = [\"manual\", \"gemini\", \"chatgpt\", \"nllb\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if engine not in valid_engines:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"Invalid translator engine: {engine}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"engine\"] = engine\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Translator engine switched to: {engine}\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Translator engine: {engine}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return True\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Translation dispatcher ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 10. Manual ai.Text parser/exporter\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def parse_ai_text(content):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    ai.Text content parse করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Format:\n",
        "        [page.jpg:1] English text → Bengali translation\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        dict:\n",
        "            {\n",
        "                (page, region_id): {\n",
        "                    \"original_text\": str,\n",
        "                    \"translated_text\": str,\n",
        "                }\n",
        "            }\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    translations = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not content:\n",
        "        return translations\n",
        "\n",
        "    for line in str(content).splitlines():\n",
        "        line = line.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not line:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if line.startswith(\"#\"):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        match = re.match(r\"^\\[([^:\\]]+):(\\d+)\\]\\s*(.*)$\", line)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not match:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        page = match.group(1).strip()\n",
        "        region_id = int(match.group(2))\n",
        "        rest = match.group(3).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        original_text = \"\"\n",
        "        translated_text = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"→\" in rest:\n",
        "            # শেষ arrow ব্যবহার করি, যাতে original text-এ arrow থাকলেও সমস্যা কম হয়\n",
        "            original_text, translated_text = rest.rsplit(\"→\", 1)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            original_text = rest\n",
        "            translated_text = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        translations[(page, region_id)] = {\n",
        "            \"original_text\": original_text.strip(),\n",
        "            \"translated_text\": translated_text.strip(),\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    return translations\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_ai_text(df=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    translation_df থেকে ai.Text export তৈরি করে।\n",
        "    JSON sidecarও save করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        (txt_path, json_path)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if df is not None:\n",
        "        translation_df = df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        if \"load_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "            load_translation_df()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            translation_df = pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(translation_df) == 0:\n",
        "        log_event(\"export_ai_text: translation_df empty\", level=\"WARN\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir = Path(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"].get(\n",
        "            \"session_export_dir\",\n",
        "            MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"session_dir\"],\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    txt_path = export_dir / \"ai_text_export.txt\"\n",
        "    json_path = export_dir / \"translation_export.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"# MangaBD V12 — ai.Text Export\")\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"# Session: {MANGABD_CONFIG.get('session_id', 'unknown')}\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"#\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"# Format:\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"#   [PAGE_FILENAME:REGION_ID] English text → Bengali translation\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"#\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"# Translate each line by writing Bengali after the arrow.\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    records = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(translation_df) > 0:\n",
        "        pages = translation_df[\"page\"].unique()\n",
        "\n",
        "        for page in pages:\n",
        "            page_rows = translation_df[\n",
        "                translation_df[\"page\"] == page\n",
        "            ].sort_values(\"id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"# ═══════════════════════════════════════════\")\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"# Page: {page} ({len(page_rows)} regions)\")\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"# ═══════════════════════════════════════════\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            for _, row in page_rows.iterrows():\n",
        "                region_id = int(row[\"id\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "                original_text = str(row.get(\"original_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "                translated_text = str(row.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "                original_text = \" \".join(original_text.split())\n",
        "\n",
        "                if not original_text or is_special_marker(original_text):\n",
        "                    line = f\"[{page}:{region_id}] [SKIP:OCR_FAILED] →\"\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    line = f\"[{page}:{region_id}] {original_text} → {translated_text}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "                lines.append(line)\n",
        "\n",
        "                records.append({\n",
        "                    \"page\": page,\n",
        "                    \"page_id\": str(row.get(\"page_id\", page)),\n",
        "                    \"id\": region_id,\n",
        "                    \"region_type\": str(row.get(\"region_type\", \"overlay\")),\n",
        "                    \"original_text\": original_text,\n",
        "                    \"translated_text\": translated_text,\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "            lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    txt_content = \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "    txt_path.write_text(txt_content, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    json_path.write_text(\n",
        "        json.dumps(records, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),\n",
        "        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"ai.Text exported: {txt_path.name} ({len(records)} rows)\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ ai.Text exported: {txt_path}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ JSON sidecar  : {json_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(txt_path), str(json_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def translate_manual_upload():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    User থেকে ai.Text file upload নিয়ে parse করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from google.colab import files as colab_files\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"  📤 Upload your translated ai.Text file\")\n",
        "        print(\"     Format: [page.jpg:1] English text → Bengali translation\")\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "        uploaded = colab_files.upload()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not uploaded:\n",
        "            print(\"  ⚠️ No file uploaded\")\n",
        "            return {}\n",
        "\n",
        "        translations = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "        for filename, content_bytes in uploaded.items():\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                content = content_bytes.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")\n",
        "                parsed = parse_ai_text(content)\n",
        "\n",
        "                translations.update(parsed)\n",
        "\n",
        "                print(f\"  ✅ Parsed {filename}: {len(parsed)} entries\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as exc:\n",
        "                log_event(\n",
        "                    f\"Manual upload parse failed [{filename}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                    level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  ✅ Total translations parsed: {len(translations)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        return translations\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"Manual upload failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        return {}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def apply_manual_translation(translations_dict):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Parsed manual translations translation_df-এ apply করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Supports keys:\n",
        "        (page_filename, region_id)\n",
        "        (page_id, region_id)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        if \"load_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "            load_translation_df()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            translation_df = pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(translation_df) == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ translation_df empty\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    applied = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for idx, row in translation_df.iterrows():\n",
        "        page = str(row.get(\"page\", \"\"))\n",
        "        page_id = str(row.get(\"page_id\", page))\n",
        "        region_id = int(row.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        key_page = (page, region_id)\n",
        "        key_page_id = (page_id, region_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "        entry = None\n",
        "\n",
        "        if key_page in translations_dict:\n",
        "            entry = translations_dict[key_page]\n",
        "        elif key_page_id in translations_dict:\n",
        "            entry = translations_dict[key_page_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if entry is None:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if isinstance(entry, dict):\n",
        "            translated_text = entry.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            translated_text = str(entry)\n",
        "\n",
        "        translated_text = clean_translation(translated_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not translated_text:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"translated_text\"] = translated_text\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"translator_engine\"] = \"manual\"\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"bengali_valid\"] = validate_bengali(translated_text)\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"status\"] = get_translation_status(translated_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        applied += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    if applied > 0:\n",
        "        if \"save_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "            save_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Manual translations applied: {applied}\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Manual translations applied: {applied}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return applied\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Manual ai.Text tools ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 11. Translation records/artifacts\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_translation_records_from_df(page_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    translation_df থেকে একটা page-এর translation records তৈরি করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        if \"load_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "            load_translation_df()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            translation_df = pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(translation_df) == 0:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_rows = translation_df[\n",
        "        translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id\n",
        "    ].sort_values(\"id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    records = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for _, row in page_rows.iterrows():\n",
        "        translated_text = str(row.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        records.append({\n",
        "            \"id\": int(row.get(\"id\", 0)),\n",
        "            \"region_type\": str(row.get(\"region_type\", \"overlay\")),\n",
        "            \"original_text\": str(row.get(\"original_text\", \"\")).strip(),\n",
        "            \"translated_text\": translated_text,\n",
        "            \"translator_engine\": str(row.get(\"translator_engine\", \"\")).strip(),\n",
        "            \"bengali_valid\": bool(row.get(\"bengali_valid\", False)),\n",
        "            \"status\": get_translation_status(translated_text),\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    return records\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_translation_artifact(page_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Page translation artifact save করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    records = build_translation_records_from_df(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    save_json_artifact(page_id, \"translation\", records)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return records\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Translation artifact tools ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 12. Page translation pipeline\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def translate_page(page_id, engine=None, force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর সব OCR text translate করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Manual engine হলে auto translation করে না।\n",
        "    Manual translation apply করতে translate_manual_upload() ব্যবহার করুন।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if engine is None:\n",
        "        engine = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].get(\"engine\", \"manual\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Cache check\n",
        "    if is_stage_done(page_id, \"translate\") and not force:\n",
        "        print(f\"    ✅ Translation cached: {page_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        records = load_json_artifact(page_id, \"translation\", default=[])\n",
        "\n",
        "        # যদি translation_df-এ translated text না থাকে, records থেকে apply\n",
        "        if records and \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None:\n",
        "            page_rows = translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "            needs_rebuild = False\n",
        "\n",
        "            if len(page_rows) == 0:\n",
        "                needs_rebuild = True\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                translated_count = page_rows[\"translated_text\"].astype(str).str.strip().ne(\"\").sum()\n",
        "\n",
        "                if translated_count == 0:\n",
        "                    needs_rebuild = True\n",
        "\n",
        "            if needs_rebuild:\n",
        "                translations_dict = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "                for record in records:\n",
        "                    key = (page_id, int(record.get(\"id\", 0)))\n",
        "\n",
        "                    translations_dict[key] = {\n",
        "                        \"original_text\": record.get(\"original_text\", \"\"),\n",
        "                        \"translated_text\": record.get(\"translated_text\", \"\"),\n",
        "                    }\n",
        "\n",
        "                apply_manual_translation(translations_dict)\n",
        "\n",
        "        return records\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        if \"load_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "            load_translation_df()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            translation_df = pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(translation_df) == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ translation_df empty — no OCR rows found\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        save_json_artifact(page_id, \"translation\", [])\n",
        "\n",
        "        mark_stage_done(\n",
        "            page_id,\n",
        "            \"translate\",\n",
        "            meta={\n",
        "                \"translator_engine\": engine,\n",
        "                \"translated_count\": 0,\n",
        "            },\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_mask = translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id\n",
        "    page_indices = translation_df.index[page_mask].tolist()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not page_indices:\n",
        "        print(f\"    ⚠️ No translation rows for page: {page_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        save_json_artifact(page_id, \"translation\", [])\n",
        "\n",
        "        mark_stage_done(\n",
        "            page_id,\n",
        "            \"translate\",\n",
        "            meta={\n",
        "                \"translator_engine\": engine,\n",
        "                \"translated_count\": 0,\n",
        "            },\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    🌐 Translating {len(page_indices)} regions with: {engine}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if engine == \"manual\":\n",
        "        print(\"    ℹ️ Manual engine selected — auto translation skipped\")\n",
        "        print(\"    ℹ️ Use export_ai_text() + translate_manual_upload()\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    translated_count = 0\n",
        "    failed_count = 0\n",
        "    bengali_count = 0\n",
        "    marker_count = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for idx in page_indices:\n",
        "        original_text = str(translation_df.at[idx, \"original_text\"]).strip()\n",
        "        region_type = str(translation_df.at[idx, \"region_type\"]).strip()\n",
        "        region_type = region_type or \"bubble\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not original_text or is_special_marker(original_text):\n",
        "            translation_df.at[idx, \"translated_text\"] = original_text\n",
        "            translation_df.at[idx, \"translator_engine\"] = engine\n",
        "            translation_df.at[idx, \"status\"] = get_translation_status(original_text)\n",
        "            marker_count += 1\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        # যদি আগেই valid Bengali translation থাকে এবং force না হয়\n",
        "        existing_translation = str(\n",
        "            translation_df.at[idx, \"translated_text\"]\n",
        "        ).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if (\n",
        "            not force\n",
        "            and existing_translation\n",
        "            and validate_bengali(existing_translation)\n",
        "        ):\n",
        "            translated_count += 1\n",
        "            bengali_count += 1\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if engine == \"manual\":\n",
        "            translated_text = existing_translation\n",
        "            engine_used = \"manual\"\n",
        "            warnings_list = []\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            translated_text, engine_used, warnings_list = translate_text(\n",
        "                original_text,\n",
        "                engine=engine,\n",
        "                region_type=region_type,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        translated_text = clean_translation(translated_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"translated_text\"] = translated_text\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"translator_engine\"] = engine_used\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"bengali_valid\"] = validate_bengali(translated_text)\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"status\"] = get_translation_status(translated_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        status = get_translation_status(translated_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if status == \"translated\":\n",
        "            translated_count += 1\n",
        "            bengali_count += 1\n",
        "        elif status == \"non_bengali\":\n",
        "            failed_count += 1\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            failed_count += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "        preview = translated_text[:35]\n",
        "\n",
        "        status_icon = \"✅\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        if status == \"non_bengali\":\n",
        "            status_icon = \"🟠\"\n",
        "        elif status == \"untranslated\":\n",
        "            status_icon = \"🔴\"\n",
        "        elif status == \"marker\":\n",
        "            status_icon = \"⚪\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"      [{region_type:8s}] \"\n",
        "            f\"{status_icon} \"\n",
        "            f\"'{preview}'\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if warnings_list:\n",
        "            for warning in warnings_list[:1]:\n",
        "                print(f\"                 ⚠️ {warning}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Save translation_df\n",
        "    if \"save_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "        save_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Save page translation artifact\n",
        "    records = save_translation_artifact(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    mark_stage_done(\n",
        "        page_id,\n",
        "        \"translate\",\n",
        "        meta={\n",
        "            \"translator_engine\": engine,\n",
        "            \"translated_count\": translated_count,\n",
        "            \"bengali_count\": bengali_count,\n",
        "            \"failed_count\": failed_count,\n",
        "            \"marker_count\": marker_count,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Translation completed [{page_id}]: \"\n",
        "        f\"translated={translated_count}, failed={failed_count}\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "        engine=engine,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"    ✅ Translation done: \"\n",
        "        f\"translated={translated_count}, \"\n",
        "        f\"bengali={bengali_count}, \"\n",
        "        f\"failed={failed_count}, \"\n",
        "        f\"marker={marker_count}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return records\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def translate_all_pages(engine=None, force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Manifest-এর সব OCR-done page translate করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if engine is None:\n",
        "        engine = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"].get(\"engine\", \"manual\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Manual workflow\n",
        "    if engine == \"manual\":\n",
        "        print(\"  📝 Manual translation workflow\")\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "        txt_path, json_path = export_ai_text()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "        print(\"  এখন আপনি ai.Text file-এ অনুবাদ করে upload করুন।\")\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "        translations_dict = translate_manual_upload()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not translations_dict:\n",
        "            print(\"  ⚠️ No manual translations received\")\n",
        "            return []\n",
        "\n",
        "        apply_manual_translation(translations_dict)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # যাদের translation apply হয়েছে তাদের page mark done\n",
        "        if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None:\n",
        "            translated_pages = translation_df[\n",
        "                translation_df[\"translated_text\"].astype(str).str.strip().ne(\"\")\n",
        "                & translation_df[\"bengali_valid\"].astype(bool)\n",
        "            ][\"page_id\"].unique()\n",
        "\n",
        "            for page_id in translated_pages:\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    save_translation_artifact(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "                    mark_stage_done(\n",
        "                        page_id,\n",
        "                        \"translate\",\n",
        "                        meta={\n",
        "                            \"translator_engine\": \"manual\",\n",
        "                        },\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                except Exception as exc:\n",
        "                    log_event(\n",
        "                        f\"Manual translate mark failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                        level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Auto engines\n",
        "    if \"MANGABD_MANIFEST\" in globals():\n",
        "        pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "        target_pages = [\n",
        "            page for page in pages.values()\n",
        "            if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"ocr\", False)\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        target_pages = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Fallback: translation_df থেকে page_id\n",
        "    if not target_pages:\n",
        "        if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None and len(translation_df) > 0:\n",
        "            page_ids = translation_df[\"page_id\"].unique()\n",
        "\n",
        "            target_pages = [\n",
        "                {\"page_id\": page_id}\n",
        "                for page_id in page_ids\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not target_pages:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No OCR-done pages found. আগে OCR চালান।\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📂 {len(target_pages)} pages translate হবে\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  🌐 Engine: {engine}\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page_index, page in enumerate(target_pages, start=1):\n",
        "        page_id = page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╔═══════════════════════════════════════════╗\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ║  [{page_index}/{len(target_pages)}] {page_id}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╚═══════════════════════════════════════════╝\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            records = translate_page(page_id, engine=engine, force=force)\n",
        "            all_results.append(records)\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Translation page failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            if \"add_page_error\" in globals():\n",
        "                add_page_error(page_id, \"translate\", str(exc)[:300])\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return all_results\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Page translation pipeline ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",

        "    print(f\"  {status} {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if details and not condition:\n",
        "        print(f\"     {details}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    tests.append(bool(condition))\n",

        "        and validate_bengali(\"Hello\") is False\n",
        "        and validate_bengali(\"\") is False\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"validate_bengali\", ok)\n",

        "        and parsed[(\"page2.jpg\", 1)][\"translated_text\"] == \"\"\n",

        "except Exception as exc:\n",

        "\n",
