        "# - page-wise inpaint pipeline\n",
        "# - GPU memory-safe model handling\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "import time\n",
        "import cv2\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  MangaBD V12 — Cell 9: Inpainting Service\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 1. Dependency check\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"MANGABD_CONFIG\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"MANGABD_CONFIG not found. আগে Cell 2 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"log_event\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"log_event not found. আগে Cell 2 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"model_manager\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"model_manager not found. আগে Cell 5 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"_run_async\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"_run_async not found. আগে Cell 5 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"get_page\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"Storage functions not found. আগে Cell 3 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  1. Dependency check\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ MANGABD_CONFIG found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ log_event found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ model_manager found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ _run_async found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ storage functions found\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 2. Inpainting config defaults\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  2. Inpainting config defaults\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG.setdefault(\"inpainting\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"engine\", \"lama\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"fallback_engine\", \"opencv\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"lama_size\", 1024)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"unload_others\", True)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"clean_mask\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"redilate_artifact_mask\", False)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"mask_base_dilate_radius\", 6)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"mask_clip_to_regions\", True)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"mask_clip_padding\", 10)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"min_mask_pixels\", 10)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"opencv_radius\", 5)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].setdefault(\"save_preview\", False)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Inpainting config ready\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Engine          : {MANGABD_CONFIG['inpainting']['engine']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Fallback        : {MANGABD_CONFIG['inpainting']['fallback_engine']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     LaMa size       : {MANGABD_CONFIG['inpainting']['lama_size']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Unload others   : {MANGABD_CONFIG['inpainting']['unload_others']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Clip mask       : {MANGABD_CONFIG['inpainting']['mask_clip_to_regions']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Redilate mask   : {MANGABD_CONFIG['inpainting']['redilate_artifact_mask']}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 3. Mask helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def _ensure_uint8_mask(mask):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Mask কে uint8 binary mask-এ convert করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask.size == 0:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask.dtype != np.uint8:\n",
        "        mask = mask.astype(np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(mask.shape) == 3:\n",
        "        mask = cv2.cvtColor(mask, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n",
        "\n",
        "    _, mask = cv2.threshold(mask, 127, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def inpaint_clean_mask(mask, min_area=8):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Mask clean করে:\n",
        "    - binary threshold\n",
        "    - morphological close\n",
        "    - tiny component remove\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask = _ensure_uint8_mask(mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (3, 3))\n",
        "\n",
        "    closed = cv2.morphologyEx(\n",
        "        mask,\n",
        "        cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,\n",
        "        kernel,\n",
        "        iterations=1,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    num_labels, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(closed)\n",
        "\n",
        "    cleaned = np.zeros_like(closed)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if num_labels > 1:\n",
        "        for i in range(1, num_labels):\n",
        "            area = stats[i, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA]\n",
        "\n",
        "            if area >= min_area:\n",
        "                cleaned[labels == i] = 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    return cleaned\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def inpaint_adaptive_dilate_mask(mask, base_radius=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Adaptive dilation।\n",
        "    Distance transform ব্যবহার করে stroke width estimate করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask = _ensure_uint8_mask(mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if np.sum(mask > 0) == 0:\n",
        "        return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "    if base_radius is None:\n",
        "        base_radius = int(\n",
        "            MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].get(\"mask_base_dilate_radius\", 6)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    radius = base_radius\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        dist = cv2.distanceTransform(mask, cv2.DIST_L2, 5)\n",
        "        max_stroke_half = float(dist.max())\n",
        "        adaptive_radius = max(2, int(max_stroke_half * 0.7))\n",
        "        radius = max(radius, min(adaptive_radius, 15))\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(\n",
        "        cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE,\n",
        "        (2 * radius + 1, 2 * radius + 1),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    dilated = cv2.dilate(mask, kernel, iterations=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return dilated\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def inpaint_bbox_mask_from_regions(image_bgr, regions, padding=6):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Region bounding box থেকে fallback mask তৈরি করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "    mask = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for region in regions:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            x = int(region.get(\"x\", 0))\n",
        "            y = int(region.get(\"y\", 0))\n",
        "            rw = int(region.get(\"width\", 0))\n",
        "            rh = int(region.get(\"height\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "            x1 = max(0, x - padding)\n",
        "            y1 = max(0, y - padding)\n",
        "            x2 = min(w, x + rw + padding)\n",
        "            y2 = min(h, y + rh + padding)\n",
        "\n",
        "            cv2.rectangle(mask, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), 255, -1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def inpaint_clip_mask_to_regions(mask, regions, padding=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Mask কে detected region boxes-এর ভেতরে clip করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask = _ensure_uint8_mask(mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not regions:\n",
        "        return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "    if padding is None:\n",
        "        padding = int(\n",
        "            MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].get(\"mask_clip_padding\", 10)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    h, w = mask.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "    region_mask = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for region in regions:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            x = int(region.get(\"x\", 0))\n",
        "            y = int(region.get(\"y\", 0))\n",
        "            rw = int(region.get(\"width\", 0))\n",
        "            rh = int(region.get(\"height\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "            x1 = max(0, x - padding)\n",
        "            y1 = max(0, y - padding)\n",
        "            x2 = min(w, x + rw + padding)\n",
        "            y2 = min(h, y + rh + padding)\n",
        "\n",
        "            cv2.rectangle(region_mask, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), 255, -1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    clipped = cv2.bitwise_and(mask, region_mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if np.sum(clipped > 0) == 0:\n",
        "        return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "    return clipped\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def prepare_mask_for_inpaint(\n",
        "    image_bgr,\n",
        "    mask,\n",
        "    regions=None,\n",
        "    source=\"artifact\",\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Inpainting-এর আগে mask prepare করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    source:\n",
        "        \"artifact\"  → cached text_mask.png\n",
        "        \"generated\" → fallback bbox mask / newly generated mask\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask = _ensure_uint8_mask(mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask is None or np.sum(mask > 0) == 0:\n",
        "        if regions:\n",
        "            mask = inpaint_bbox_mask_from_regions(image_bgr, regions)\n",
        "            source = \"generated\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "            mask = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if cfg.get(\"clean_mask\", True):\n",
        "        cleaned = inpaint_clean_mask(mask, min_area=3)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cleaned is not None and np.sum(cleaned > 0) > 0:\n",
        "            mask = cleaned\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Artifact mask সাধারণত detection stage-এ already dilated।\n",
        "    # তাই defaultভাবে আবার dilate করি না।\n",
        "    if source != \"artifact\" or cfg.get(\"redilate_artifact_mask\", False):\n",
        "        mask = inpaint_adaptive_dilate_mask(mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if cfg.get(\"mask_clip_to_regions\", True) and regions:\n",
        "        clipped = inpaint_clip_mask_to_regions(mask, regions)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if clipped is not None and np.sum(clipped > 0) > 0:\n",
        "            mask = clipped\n",
        "\n",
        "    return mask\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Mask helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 4. OpenCV fallback inpainter\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def opencv_inpaint(image_bgr, mask, radius=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    OpenCV TELEA inpainting fallback।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if radius is None:\n",
        "        radius = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].get(\"opencv_radius\", 5))\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask = _ensure_uint8_mask(mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask is None:\n",
        "        return image_bgr.copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        result = cv2.inpaint(\n",
        "            image_bgr,\n",
        "            mask,\n",
        "            radius,\n",
        "            cv2.INPAINT_TELEA,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return result\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"OpenCV inpaint failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return image_bgr.copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ OpenCV fallback ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. LaMa inpainter\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def lama_inpaint_full(image_bgr, mask):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    LaMa Large দিয়ে full page inpaint করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # GPU memory বাঁচাতে অন্য model unload\n",
        "    if cfg.get(\"unload_others\", True):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            model_manager.unload_all(exclude=[\"lama\"])\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Model unload before LaMa warning: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    lama = model_manager.ensure_lama()\n",
        "\n",
        "    from manga_translator.inpainting.common import InpainterConfig\n",
        "\n",
        "    inpaint_config = InpainterConfig()\n",
        "\n",
        "    lama_size = int(cfg.get(\"lama_size\", 1024))\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask = _ensure_uint8_mask(mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    result = _run_async(\n",
        "        lama.inpaint(\n",
        "            image_bgr,\n",
        "            mask,\n",
        "            inpaint_config,\n",
        "            lama_size,\n",
        "            False,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if result is None or result.size == 0:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"LaMa returned empty result\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if result.shape[:2] != image_bgr.shape[:2]:\n",
        "        result = cv2.resize(\n",
        "            result,\n",
        "            (image_bgr.shape[1], image_bgr.shape[0]),\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return result\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ LaMa inpainter ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. Page inpainting pipeline\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def inpaint_page(page_id, image_bgr=None, force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page inpaint করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        inpainted_bgr\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Cache check\n",
        "    if is_stage_done(page_id, \"inpaint\") and not force:\n",
        "        cached = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cached is not None:\n",
        "            print(f\"    ✅ Inpaint cached: {page_id}\")\n",
        "            return cached\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"    ⚠️ Inpaint checkpoint আছে কিন্তু file নেই — re-inpaint হবে\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"reset_page\" in globals():\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                reset_page(page_id, from_stage=\"inpaint\")\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Image load\n",
        "    if image_bgr is None:\n",
        "        image_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if image_bgr is None:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "            f\"Original image not found for page: {page_id}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Regions load\n",
        "    regions = load_json_artifact(page_id, \"detections\", default=[])\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Mask load\n",
        "    mask = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"mask\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask_source = \"artifact\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask is None or np.sum(mask > 0) == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ Cached mask empty — generating fallback mask\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if regions:\n",
        "            mask = inpaint_bbox_mask_from_regions(image_bgr, regions)\n",
        "            mask_source = \"generated\"\n",
        "        elif \"detect_page\" in globals():\n",
        "            print(\"    🔎 Running detection to get mask\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            regions, detected_mask = detect_page(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                image_bgr,\n",
        "                force=False,\n",
        "                return_mask=True,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            if detected_mask is not None and np.sum(detected_mask > 0) > 0:\n",
        "                mask = detected_mask\n",
        "                mask_source = \"artifact\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask is None or np.sum(mask > 0) == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ No text mask found — original image kept as inpainted\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        save_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\", image_bgr, quality=95)\n",
        "\n",
        "        mark_stage_done(\n",
        "            page_id,\n",
        "            \"inpaint\",\n",
        "            meta={\n",
        "                \"inpaint_engine\": \"none\",\n",
        "                \"mask_pixels\": 0,\n",
        "                \"fallback_used\": False,\n",
        "            },\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return image_bgr\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Prepare mask\n",
        "    prepared_mask = prepare_mask_for_inpaint(\n",
        "        image_bgr,\n",
        "        mask,\n",
        "        regions=regions,\n",
        "        source=mask_source,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask_pixels = int(np.sum(prepared_mask > 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "    min_mask_pixels = int(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"].get(\"min_mask_pixels\", 10)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mask_pixels < min_mask_pixels:\n",
        "        print(f\"    ⚠️ Mask too small ({mask_pixels}px) — original kept\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        save_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\", image_bgr, quality=95)\n",
        "\n",
        "        mark_stage_done(\n",
        "            page_id,\n",
        "            \"inpaint\",\n",
        "            meta={\n",
        "                \"inpaint_engine\": \"none\",\n",
        "                \"mask_pixels\": mask_pixels,\n",
        "                \"fallback_used\": False,\n",
        "            },\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return image_bgr\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    🎨 Inpainting with LaMa ({mask_pixels:,} mask pixels)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_start = time.time()\n",
        "\n",
        "    inpainted = None\n",
        "    engine_used = \"lama\"\n",
        "    fallback_used = False\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        inpainted = lama_inpaint_full(image_bgr, prepared_mask)\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"LaMa inpaint failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ LaMa failed — OpenCV fallback\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            inpainted = opencv_inpaint(image_bgr, prepared_mask)\n",
        "            engine_used = \"opencv\"\n",
        "            fallback_used = True\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc2:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"OpenCV inpaint failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc2)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            inpainted = image_bgr.copy()\n",
        "            engine_used = \"none\"\n",
        "            fallback_used = True\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_time = time.time() - page_start\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Save inpainted artifact\n",
        "    save_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\", inpainted, quality=95)\n",
        "\n",
        "    mark_stage_done(\n",
        "        page_id,\n",
        "        \"inpaint\",\n",
        "        meta={\n",
        "            \"inpaint_engine\": engine_used,\n",
        "            \"fallback_used\": fallback_used,\n",
        "            \"mask_pixels\": mask_pixels,\n",
        "            \"mask_source\": mask_source,\n",
        "            \"inpaint_time_sec\": round(page_time, 2),\n",
        "        },\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Inpaint completed [{page_id}]: engine={engine_used}, \"\n",
        "        f\"mask_pixels={mask_pixels}, time={page_time:.1f}s\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"    ✅ Inpaint done: \"\n",
        "        f\"engine={engine_used}, \"\n",
        "        f\"fallback={fallback_used}, \"\n",
        "        f\"time={page_time:.1f}s\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return inpainted\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def inpaint_all_pages(force=False, require_translation=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Manifest-এর সব detected page inpaint করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    require_translation=True হলে শুধু translated pages inpaint হবে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"MANGABD_MANIFEST\" not in globals():\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"MANGABD_MANIFEST not found. আগে Cell 3 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    target_pages = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page in pages.values():\n",
        "        stages = page.get(\"stages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not stages.get(\"detect\", False):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if require_translation and not stages.get(\"translate\", False):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        target_pages.append(page)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not target_pages:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No eligible pages found for inpainting\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📂 {len(target_pages)} pages inpaint হবে\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page_index, page in enumerate(target_pages, start=1):\n",
        "        page_id = page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╔═══════════════════════════════════════════╗\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ║  [{page_index}/{len(target_pages)}] {page_id}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╚═══════════════════════════════════════════╝\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            inpainted = inpaint_page(page_id, force=force)\n",
        "            results.append(inpainted)\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Inpaint page failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            if \"add_page_error\" in globals():\n",
        "                add_page_error(page_id, \"inpaint\", str(exc)[:300])\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return results\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Page inpainting pipeline ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",

        "tests = []\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_test(name, condition, details=\"\"):\n",
        "    status = \"✅\" if condition else \"❌\"\n",
        "    print(f\"  {status} {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if details and not condition:\n",
        "        print(f\"     {details}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    tests.append(bool(condition))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 1: mask clean\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_mask = np.zeros((100, 100), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "    test_mask[40:60, 40:60] = 255\n",
        "    test_mask[10:12, 10:12] = 255  # tiny noise\n",
        "\n",
        "    cleaned = inpaint_clean_mask(test_mask, min_area=8)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = (\n",
        "        cleaned is not None\n",
        "        and np.sum(cleaned[40:60, 40:60] > 0) > 0\n",
        "        and np.sum(cleaned[10:12, 10:12] > 0) == 0\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"inpaint_clean_mask\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"inpaint_clean_mask\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 2: adaptive dilation\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_mask = np.zeros((100, 100), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "    test_mask[45:55, 45:55] = 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    dilated = inpaint_adaptive_dilate_mask(test_mask, base_radius=4)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = np.sum(dilated > 0) > np.sum(test_mask > 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"inpaint_adaptive_dilate_mask\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"inpaint_adaptive_dilate_mask\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 3: bbox mask from regions\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_image = np.ones((300, 300, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    regions = [\n",
        "        {\"x\": 50, \"y\": 50, \"width\": 100, \"height\": 50},\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask = inpaint_bbox_mask_from_regions(test_image, regions)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = mask.shape == (300, 300) and np.sum(mask > 0) > 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"inpaint_bbox_mask_from_regions\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"inpaint_bbox_mask_from_regions\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 4: mask clipping\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_mask = np.ones((300, 300), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    regions = [\n",
        "        {\"x\": 100, \"y\": 100, \"width\": 100, \"height\": 100},\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    clipped = inpaint_clip_mask_to_regions(test_mask, regions, padding=0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = (\n",
        "        np.sum(clipped > 0) < np.sum(test_mask > 0)\n",
        "        and np.sum(clipped[100:200, 100:200] > 0) > 0\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"inpaint_clip_mask_to_regions\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"inpaint_clip_mask_to_regions\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 5: prepare mask\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_image = np.ones((300, 300, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    test_mask = np.zeros((300, 300), dtype=np.uint8)\n",
        "    test_mask[100:150, 100:200] = 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    regions = [\n",
        "        {\"x\": 100, \"y\": 100, \"width\": 100, \"height\": 50},\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    prepared = prepare_mask_for_inpaint(\n",
        "        test_image,\n",
        "        test_mask,\n",
        "        regions=regions,\n",
        "        source=\"artifact\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = prepared is not None and np.sum(prepared > 0) > 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"prepare_mask_for_inpaint\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"prepare_mask_for_inpaint\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 6: OpenCV fallback\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_image = np.ones((120, 200, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 220\n",
        "    test_image[50:70, 50:150] = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    test_mask = np.zeros((120, 200), dtype=np.uint8)\n",

        "    ok = result.shape == test_image.shape and center_brightness > 100\n",

        "except ImportError:\n",
        "    def grapheme_clusters(word):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        Fallback character split।\n",
        "        grapheme-safe না, কিন্তু regex ছাড়া চলবে।\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        return list(str(word))\n",

        "def local_validate_bengali(text):\n",

        "    অন্তত একটি Bengali character আছে কিনা।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    return any(\"\\u0980\" <= ch <= \"\\u09FF\" for ch in str(text))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",

        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Special OCR/translation marker কিনা।\n",

        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return False\n",

        "    markers = {\n",
        "        \"[EMPTY]\",\n",
        "        \"[OCR_FAILED]\",\n",
        "        \"[SFX]\",\n",
        "        \"[SKIP:OCR_FAILED]\",\n",
        "        \"[TRANS_FAILED]\",\n",
        "        \"[UNTRANSLATED]\",\n",
        "        \"nan\",\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(text).strip() in markers\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"validate_bengali\" in globals():\n",

        "                path = bold_path\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            path = regular_path\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        font = ImageFont.truetype(path, size)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            font = ImageFont.truetype(regular_path, size)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            font = ImageFont.load_default()\n",
        "\n",
        "    _FONT_CACHE[key] = font\n",
        "\n",
        "    return font\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def measure_text(text, font, draw=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Text width/height measure করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return 0, 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if draw is None:\n",
        "        dummy = PILImage.new(\"RGB\", (1, 1))\n",
        "        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(dummy)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), str(text), font=font)\n",
        "        width = max(0, bbox[2] - bbox[0])\n",
        "        height = max(0, bbox[3] - bbox[1])\n",
        "        return width, height\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            width = int(font.getlength(str(text)))\n",
        "            height = int(getattr(font, \"size\", 12) * 1.2)\n",
        "            return width, height\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            return len(str(text)) * 8, 16\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_line_height(font, factor=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Actual font metrics থেকে line height।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if factor is None:\n",
        "        factor = float(\n",
        "            MANGABD_CONFIG[\"rendering\"].get(\"line_spacing_factor\", 1.18)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        ascent, descent = font.getmetrics()\n",
        "        return int((ascent + descent) * factor)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return int(getattr(font, \"size\", 12) * factor)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Font helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. Text wrapping\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def split_long_word(word, font, draw, max_width):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    খুব লম্বা word কে grapheme-safeভাবে ভাগ করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    word = str(word)\n",
        "\n",
        "    word_width, _ = measure_text(word, font, draw)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if word_width <= max_width:\n",
        "        return [word]\n",
        "\n",
        "    clusters = grapheme_clusters(word)\n",
        "\n",
        "    parts = []\n",
        "    current = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    for cluster in clusters:\n",
        "        test = current + cluster\n",
        "        test_width, _ = measure_text(test, font, draw)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if test_width <= max_width or not current:\n",
        "            current = test\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            parts.append(current)\n",
        "            current = cluster\n",
        "\n",
        "    if current:\n",
        "        parts.append(current)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return parts\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def split_text_to_lines(text, font, draw, max_width):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Word-level + grapheme-safe text wrapping।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = str(text).strip()\n",
        "    words = text.split()\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = []\n",
        "    current_line = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    for word in words:\n",
        "        if not word:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        word_width, _ = measure_text(word, font, draw)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # যদি একটা word নিজেই max_width-এর বেশি হয়\n",
        "        if word_width > max_width:\n",
        "            if current_line:\n",
        "                lines.append(current_line)\n",
        "                current_line = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            word_parts = split_long_word(word, font, draw, max_width)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if len(word_parts) > 1:\n",
        "                lines.extend(word_parts[:-1])\n",
        "                current_line = word_parts[-1]\n",
        "            elif word_parts:\n",
        "                current_line = word_parts[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if current_line:\n",
        "            test_line = current_line + \" \" + word\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            test_line = word\n",
        "\n",
        "        test_width, _ = measure_text(test_line, font, draw)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if test_width <= max_width:\n",
        "            current_line = test_line\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            if current_line:\n",
        "                lines.append(current_line)\n",
        "            current_line = word\n",
        "\n",
        "    if current_line:\n",
        "        lines.append(current_line)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return lines\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Text wrapping ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. Font fitting\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def fit_font_size(\n",
        "    text,\n",
        "    target_width,\n",
        "    target_height,\n",
        "    bold=False,\n",
        "    min_size=None,\n",
        "    max_size=None,\n",
        "    padding=None,\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Target box-এর ভেতরে fit করার জন্য best font size খোঁজে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        (font_size, lines, total_height)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"rendering\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return int(cfg.get(\"font_size_default\", 24)), [], 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if min_size is None:\n",
        "        min_size = int(cfg.get(\"font_size_min\", 8))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if max_size is None:\n",
        "        max_size = int(cfg.get(\"font_size_max\", 48))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if padding is None:\n",
        "        padding_ratio = float(cfg.get(\"padding_ratio\", 0.08))\n",
        "        padding = max(\n",
        "            4,\n",
        "            int(min(target_width, target_height) * padding_ratio),\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    available_width = max(10, int(target_width) - 2 * padding)\n",
        "    available_height = max(10, int(target_height) - 2 * padding)\n",
        "\n",
        "    dummy = PILImage.new(\"RGB\", (1, 1))\n",
        "    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(dummy)\n",
        "\n",
        "    best_size = min_size\n",
        "    best_lines = [str(text)]\n",
        "    best_height = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    low = min_size\n",
        "    high = max_size\n",
        "\n",
        "    while low <= high:\n",
        "        mid = (low + high) // 2\n",
        "\n",
        "        font = get_font(mid, bold=bold)\n",
        "        lines = split_text_to_lines(text, font, draw, available_width)\n",
        "\n",
        "        line_height = get_line_height(font)\n",
        "        total_height = line_height * len(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "        max_line_width = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        for line in lines:\n",
        "            line_width, _ = measure_text(line, font, draw)\n",
        "            max_line_width = max(max_line_width, line_width)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if total_height <= available_height and max_line_width <= available_width:\n",
        "            best_size = mid\n",
        "            best_lines = lines\n",
        "            best_height = total_height\n",
        "            low = mid + 1\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            high = mid - 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    # যদি কোনো size fit না করে, min_size-এ render করব\n",
        "    # Truncation করব না — dialogue কাটা যাবে না\n",
        "    if best_height == 0:\n",
        "        font = get_font(min_size, bold=bold)\n",
        "        best_lines = split_text_to_lines(text, font, draw, available_width)\n",
        "        best_height = get_line_height(font) * len(best_lines)\n",
        "        best_size = min_size\n",
        "\n",
        "    return best_size, best_lines, best_height\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Font fitting ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 7. Background-aware text color\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def choose_text_color(image_bgr, x, y, w, h, region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Inpainted background brightness দেখে text color choose করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        RGB tuple\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"rendering\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    brightness = 255.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if cfg.get(\"use_background_aware_color\", True):\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            img_h, img_w = image_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "            x1 = max(0, int(x))\n",
        "            y1 = max(0, int(y))\n",
        "            x2 = min(img_w, int(x + w))\n",
        "            y2 = min(img_h, int(y + h))\n",
        "\n",
        "            if x2 > x1 and y2 > y1:\n",
        "                roi = image_bgr[y1:y2, x1:x2]\n",
        "\n",
        "                if roi.size > 0:\n",
        "                    gray = cv2.cvtColor(roi, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n",
        "                    brightness = float(np.mean(gray))\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            brightness = 255.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if region_type in [\"bubble\", \"thought\"]:\n",
        "        if brightness > 160:\n",
        "            return tuple(cfg.get(\"default_bubble_color\", [0, 0, 0]))\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            return (255, 255, 255)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if region_type == \"narrator\":\n",
        "        if brightness > 140:\n",
        "            return tuple(cfg.get(\"default_narrator_color\", [0, 0, 0]))\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            return (255, 255, 255)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # sfx / overlay\n",
        "    if brightness < 160:\n",
        "        return tuple(cfg.get(\"default_sfx_color\", [255, 255, 255]))\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        return tuple(cfg.get(\"default_overlay_color\", [0, 0, 0]))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def choose_stroke_width(region_type):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Region type অনুযায়ী stroke width।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"rendering\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if region_type == \"bubble\":\n",
        "        return int(cfg.get(\"stroke_width_bubble\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if region_type == \"thought\":\n",
        "        return int(cfg.get(\"stroke_width_thought\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if region_type == \"narrator\":\n",
        "        return int(cfg.get(\"stroke_width_narrator\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if region_type == \"sfx\":\n",
        "        return int(cfg.get(\"stroke_width_sfx\", 1))\n",
        "\n",
        "    return int(cfg.get(\"stroke_width_overlay\", 1))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def choose_stroke_color(text_color):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Text color-এর বিপরীতে stroke color।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text_color:\n",
        "        return (0, 0, 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    r, g, b = text_color[:3]\n",
        "\n",
        "    brightness = 0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b\n",
        "\n",
        "    if brightness > 150:\n",
        "        return (0, 0, 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return (255, 255, 255)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Background-aware color ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 8. Main renderer\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def render_bengali_text(\n",
        "    image_pil,\n",
        "    text,\n",
        "    x,\n",
        "    y,\n",
        "    w,\n",
        "    h,\n",
        "    angle=0.0,\n",
        "    region_type=\"bubble\",\n",
        "    bold=None,\n",
        "    stroke_width=None,\n",
        "    text_color=None,\n",
        "    padding=None,\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    PIL image-এ Bengali text render করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text or not str(text).strip():\n",
        "        return image_pil\n",
        "\n",
        "    cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"rendering\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if bold is None:\n",
        "        bold = region_type in [\"sfx\", \"narrator\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if stroke_width is None:\n",
        "        stroke_width = choose_stroke_width(region_type)\n",
        "\n",
        "    font_size, lines, _ = fit_font_size(\n",
        "        text,\n",
        "        w,\n",
        "        h,\n",
        "        bold=bold,\n",
        "        padding=padding,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    font = get_font(font_size, bold=bold)\n",
        "    line_height = get_line_height(font)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if text_color is None:\n",
        "        text_color = (0, 0, 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    stroke_color = choose_stroke_color(text_color)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Rotated rendering\n",
        "    if abs(float(angle)) > 1.0:\n",
        "        return _render_rotated_text(\n",
        "            image_pil,\n",
        "            lines,\n",
        "            font,\n",
        "            x,\n",
        "            y,\n",
        "            w,\n",
        "            h,\n",
        "            float(angle),\n",
        "            text_color,\n",
        "            stroke_color,\n",
        "            stroke_width,\n",
        "            line_height,\n",
        "            padding,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image_pil)\n",
        "\n",
        "    total_text_height = line_height * len(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if padding is None:\n",
        "        padding_ratio = float(cfg.get(\"padding_ratio\", 0.08))\n",
        "        padding = max(4, int(min(w, h) * padding_ratio))\n",
        "\n",
        "    vertical_center = int(y + (h - total_text_height) // 2)\n",
        "    start_y = max(int(y) + padding, vertical_center)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n",
        "        line_width, _ = measure_text(line, font, draw)\n",
        "\n",
        "        line_x = int(x + (w - line_width) // 2)\n",
        "        line_y = int(start_y + i * line_height)\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            if stroke_width and stroke_width > 0:\n",
        "                draw.text(\n",
        "                    (line_x, line_y),\n",
        "                    line,\n",
        "                    font=font,\n",
        "                    fill=text_color,\n",
        "                    stroke_width=int(stroke_width),\n",
        "                    stroke_fill=stroke_color,\n",
        "                )\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                draw.text(\n",
        "                    (line_x, line_y),\n",
        "                    line,\n",
        "                    font=font,\n",
        "                    fill=text_color,\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        except TypeError:\n",
        "            # Older Pillow fallback\n",
        "            if stroke_width and stroke_width > 0:\n",
        "                for dx in range(-stroke_width, stroke_width + 1):\n",
        "                    for dy in range(-stroke_width, stroke_width + 1):\n",
        "                        draw.text(\n",
        "                            (line_x + dx, line_y + dy),\n",
        "                            line,\n",
        "                            font=font,\n",
        "                            fill=stroke_color,\n",
        "                        )\n",
        "\n",
        "            draw.text(\n",
        "                (line_x, line_y),\n",
        "                line,\n",
        "                font=font,\n",
        "                fill=text_color,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return image_pil\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _render_rotated_text(\n",
        "    image_pil,\n",
        "    lines,\n",
        "    font,\n",
        "    x,\n",
        "    y,\n",
        "    w,\n",
        "    h,\n",
        "    angle,\n",
        "    text_color,\n",
        "    stroke_color,\n",
        "    stroke_width,\n",
        "    line_height,\n",
        "    padding,\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Rotated text render করে transparent canvas ব্যবহার করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if padding is None:\n",
        "            padding = 8\n",
        "\n",
        "        canvas_width = max(int(w) + 4 * padding, 200)\n",
        "        canvas_height = max(int(h) + 4 * padding, 100)\n",
        "\n",
        "        text_canvas = PILImage.new(\n",
        "            \"RGBA\",\n",
        "            (canvas_width, canvas_height),\n",
        "            (0, 0, 0, 0),\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(text_canvas)\n",
        "\n",
        "        total_text_height = line_height * len(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "        start_y = padding + (canvas_height - 2 * padding - total_text_height) // 2\n",
        "        start_y = max(padding, int(start_y))\n",
        "\n",
        "        for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n",
        "            line_width, _ = measure_text(line, font, draw)\n",
        "\n",
        "            line_x = int((canvas_width - line_width) // 2)\n",
        "            line_y = int(start_y + i * line_height)\n",
        "\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if stroke_width and stroke_width > 0:\n",
        "                    draw.text(\n",
        "                        (line_x, line_y),\n",
        "                        line,\n",
        "                        font=font,\n",
        "                        fill=text_color + (255,),\n",
        "                        stroke_width=int(stroke_width),\n",
        "                        stroke_fill=stroke_color + (255,),\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    draw.text(\n",
        "                        (line_x, line_y),\n",
        "                        line,\n",
        "                        font=font,\n",
        "                        fill=text_color + (255,),\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "\n",
        "            except TypeError:\n",
        "                draw.text(\n",
        "                    (line_x, line_y),\n",
        "                    line,\n",
        "                    font=font,\n",
        "                    fill=text_color + (255,),\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        rotated = text_canvas.rotate(\n",
        "            angle,\n",
        "            expand=True,\n",
        "            resample=PILImage.BICUBIC,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        center_x = int(x + w // 2)\n",
        "        center_y = int(y + h // 2)\n",
        "\n",
        "        paste_x = center_x - rotated.width // 2\n",
        "        paste_y = center_y - rotated.height // 2\n",
        "\n",
        "        image_pil.paste(rotated, (paste_x, paste_y), rotated)\n",
        "\n",
        "        return image_pil\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"Rotated render failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Fallback axis-aligned\n",
        "        return render_bengali_text(\n",
        "            image_pil,\n",
        "            \" \".join(lines),\n",
        "            x,\n",
        "            y,\n",
        "            w,\n",
        "            h,\n",
        "            angle=0.0,\n",
        "            region_type=\"overlay\",\n",
        "            bold=False,\n",
        "            stroke_width=stroke_width,\n",
        "            text_color=text_color,\n",
        "            padding=padding,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Main renderer ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 9. Page rendering pipeline\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def render_page(page_id, force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর final Bengali image render করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        final_bgr numpy array\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Cache check\n",
        "    if is_stage_done(page_id, \"render\") and not force:\n",
        "        cached = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"final\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cached is not None:\n",
        "            print(f\"    ✅ Render cached: {page_id}\")\n",
        "            return cached\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"    ⚠️ Render checkpoint আছে কিন্তু file নেই — re-render হবে\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"reset_page\" in globals():\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                reset_page(page_id, from_stage=\"render\")\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Inpainted image load\n",
        "    inpainted_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if inpainted_bgr is None:\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ Inpainted image নেই — original ব্যবহার হবে\")\n",
        "        inpainted_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if inpainted_bgr is None:\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\n",
        "            f\"No inpainted/original image found for page: {page_id}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Translation data load\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        if \"load_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "            load_translation_df()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            translation_df = pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(translation_df) == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"    ⚠️ translation_df empty — blank final image save হবে\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Canvas\n",
        "    inpainted_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(inpainted_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)\n",
        "    canvas_pil = PILImage.fromarray(inpainted_rgb).copy()\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_rows = translation_df[\n",
        "        translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id\n",
        "    ].sort_values(\"id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    rendered_count = 0\n",
        "    skipped_count = 0\n",
        "    warning_count = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    font_sizes_used = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for idx, row in page_rows.iterrows():\n",
        "        region_id = int(row.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = int(row.get(\"x\", 0))\n",
        "        y = int(row.get(\"y\", 0))\n",
        "        w = int(row.get(\"width\", 0))\n",
        "        h = int(row.get(\"height\", 0))\n",
        "        angle = float(row.get(\"angle\", 0.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        region_type = str(row.get(\"region_type\", \"overlay\")).strip()\n",
        "        translated_text = str(row.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not translated_text:\n",
        "            skipped_count += 1\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if render_is_special_marker(translated_text):\n",
        "            skipped_count += 1\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not render_validate_bengali(translated_text):\n",
        "            skipped_count += 1\n",
        "            warning_count += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Render skipped non-Bengali [{page_id}:{region_id}]\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if w < 5 or h < 5:\n",
        "            skipped_count += 1\n",
        "            warning_count += 1\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            text_color = choose_text_color(\n",
        "                inpainted_bgr,\n",
        "                x,\n",
        "                y,\n",
        "                w,\n",
        "                h,\n",
        "                region_type=region_type,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            bold = region_type in [\"sfx\", \"narrator\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "            stroke_width = choose_stroke_width(region_type)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # Font size tracking\n",
        "            estimated_size, _, _ = fit_font_size(\n",
        "                translated_text,\n",
        "                w,\n",
        "                h,\n",
        "                bold=bold,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            font_sizes_used.append(estimated_size)\n",
        "\n",
        "            canvas_pil = render_bengali_text(\n",
        "                canvas_pil,\n",
        "                translated_text,\n",
        "                x,\n",
        "                y,\n",
        "                w,\n",
        "                h,\n",
        "                angle=angle,\n",
        "                region_type=region_type,\n",
        "                bold=bold,\n",
        "                stroke_width=stroke_width,\n",
        "                text_color=text_color,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            # DataFrame update\n",
        "            translation_df.at[idx, \"bengali_valid\"] = True\n",
        "            translation_df.at[idx, \"status\"] = \"rendered\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            rendered_count += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            warning_count += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Render failed [{page_id}:{region_id}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Save final artifact\n",
        "    save_image_artifact(page_id, \"final\", canvas_pil, quality=95)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Save translation_df\n",
        "    if \"save_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "        save_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Mark done\n",
        "    meta = {\n",
        "        \"rendered_regions\": rendered_count,\n",
        "        \"skipped_regions\": skipped_count,\n",
        "        \"render_warnings\": warning_count,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    if font_sizes_used:\n",
        "        meta[\"font_size_min_used\"] = int(min(font_sizes_used))\n",
        "        meta[\"font_size_max_used\"] = int(max(font_sizes_used))\n",
        "        meta[\"font_size_mean_used\"] = round(float(np.mean(font_sizes_used)), 1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    mark_stage_done(page_id, \"render\", meta=meta)\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Render completed [{page_id}]: rendered={rendered_count}, \"\n",
        "        f\"skipped={skipped_count}, warnings={warning_count}\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"    ✅ Render done: \"\n",
        "        f\"rendered={rendered_count}, \"\n",
        "        f\"skipped={skipped_count}, \"\n",
        "        f\"warnings={warning_count}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    final_bgr = cv2.cvtColor(np.array(canvas_pil), cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return final_bgr\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def render_all_pages(force=False, require_translation=True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Manifest-এর সব inpainted page render করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",

        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    target_pages = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page in pages.values():\n",
        "        stages = page.get(\"stages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not stages.get(\"inpaint\", False):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if require_translation and not stages.get(\"translate\", False):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        target_pages.append(page)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not target_pages:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No eligible pages found for rendering\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📂 {len(target_pages)} pages render হবে\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page_index, page in enumerate(target_pages, start=1):\n",
        "        page_id = page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╔═══════════════════════════════════════════╗\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ║  [{page_index}/{len(target_pages)}] {page_id}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╚═══════════════════════════════════════════╝\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            final_bgr = render_page(page_id, force=force)\n",
        "            results.append(final_bgr)\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Render page failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",

        "                add_page_error(page_id, \"render\", str(exc)[:300])\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return results\n",
        "\n",

        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 🎯 QUALITY CORE FINAL (single, permanent, color-safe, scalable)\n",
        "# Place: after Cell 10, before Cell 11. Delete all old FIX/QC cells.\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "import cv2, numpy as np\n",
        "import urllib.request\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "from PIL import Image as PILImage, ImageDraw, ImageFont\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── 1) Safe precise config ──\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"][\"ctd_mask_threshold\"] = 55\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"detection\"][\"mask_base_dilate_radius\"] = 5\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"][\"redilate_artifact_mask\"] = False\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"inpainting\"][\"mask_base_dilate_radius\"] = 5\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── 2) Fallback font ──\n",
        "_fb_dir = Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"].get(\"fonts\", str(Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"base\"]) / \"fonts\")))\n",
        "_fb_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "_fb_path = _fb_dir / \"NotoSansJP.ttf\"\n",
        "if not _fb_path.exists() or _fb_path.stat().st_size < 1024:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        urllib.request.urlretrieve(\n",
        "            \"https://github.com/google/fonts/raw/main/ofl/notosansjp/NotoSansJP%5Bwght%5D.ttf\", _fb_path)\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(f\"⚠️ fallback font failed: {str(e)[:80]}\")\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"rendering\"][\"font_fallback\"] = str(_fb_path)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"rendering\"].setdefault(\"vertical_rotate\", -90)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── 3) Recovery: coordinate ফিরিয়ে আনো (detection = আসল সত্য) ──\n",
        "def recover_coordinates():\n",
        "    fixed = 0\n",
        "    for pid in translation_df[\"page_id\"].unique():\n",
        "        regions = load_json_artifact(pid, \"detections\", default=[])\n",
        "        by_id = {int(r[\"id\"]): r for r in regions}\n",
        "        for idx, r in translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == pid].iterrows():\n",
        "            d = by_id.get(int(r[\"id\"]))\n",
        "            if d:\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"x\"] = int(d[\"x\"]); translation_df.at[idx, \"y\"] = int(d[\"y\"])\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"width\"] = int(d[\"width\"]); translation_df.at[idx, \"height\"] = int(d[\"height\"])\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"region_type\"] = d.get(\"region_type\", \"bubble\")\n",
        "                fixed += 1\n",
        "    save_translation_df()\n",
        "    return fixed\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── 4) SAFE box snap (guarded, no close) ──\n",
        "def _snap_white_box(gray, x, y, w, h):\n",
        "    H, W = gray.shape\n",
        "    _, white = cv2.threshold(gray, 200, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n",
        "    n, labels, stats, cent = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(white, 8)\n",
        "    for (px, py) in [(x+w//2, y+h//2), (x+w//2, y+h//3), (x+w//2, y+2*h//3),\n",
        "                     (x+w//3, y+h//2), (x+2*w//3, y+h//2)]:\n",
        "        px = max(0, min(W-1, int(px))); py = max(0, min(H-1, int(py)))\n",
        "        lab = labels[py, px]\n",
        "        if lab == 0: continue\n",
        "        bx, by, bw, bh, area = stats[lab]\n",
        "        if (bx <= 2) or (by <= 2) or (bx+bw >= W-2) or (by+bh >= H-2): continue\n",
        "        if bw*bh > 4*(w*h): continue\n",
        "        fill = area/(bw*bh) if bw*bh else 0\n",
        "        if fill > 0.85 and bw >= 30 and bh >= 30:\n",
        "            return (int(bx), int(by), int(bw), int(bh))\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "def apply_container_types():\n",
        "    changed = 0\n",
        "    for pid in translation_df[\"page_id\"].unique():\n",
        "        orig = load_image_artifact(pid, \"original\")\n",
        "        if orig is None: continue\n",
        "        gray = cv2.cvtColor(orig, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n",
        "        for idx, r in translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == pid].iterrows():\n",
        "            x, y, w, h = int(r[\"x\"]), int(r[\"y\"]), int(r[\"width\"]), int(r[\"height\"])\n",
        "            if w < 90 and h > 2*max(1, w): continue\n",
        "            box = _snap_white_box(gray, x, y, w, h)\n",
        "            if box:\n",
        "                bx, by, bw, bh = box\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"x\"] = bx; translation_df.at[idx, \"y\"] = by\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"width\"] = bw; translation_df.at[idx, \"height\"] = bh\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"region_type\"] = \"narrator\"\n",
        "                changed += 1\n",
        "    save_translation_df()\n",
        "    print(f\"✅ Container types applied ({changed} box snapped)\")\n",
        "    return changed\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── 5) translated-check + mask filter + residual killer ──\n",
        "def _has_valid_trans(row):\n",
        "    t = str(row.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "    return bool(t) and not t.startswith(\"[\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def mask_only_translated(page_id, pad=10):\n",
        "    mask = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"mask\")\n",
        "    if mask is None: return 0\n",
        "    keep = np.zeros_like(mask)\n",
        "    for _, r in translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id].iterrows():\n",
        "        if not _has_valid_trans(r): continue\n",
        "        x, y, w, h = int(r[\"x\"]), int(r[\"y\"]), int(r[\"width\"]), int(r[\"height\"])\n",
        "        cv2.rectangle(keep, (max(0, x-pad), max(0, y-pad)), (x+w+pad, y+h+pad), 255, -1)\n",
        "    save_image_artifact(page_id, \"mask\", cv2.bitwise_and(mask, keep))\n",
        "    return 1\n",
        "\n",
        "def kill_residual(page_id, dilate=13):\n",
        "    orig = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "    inp = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\")\n",
        "    mask = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"mask\")\n",
        "    if orig is None or inp is None or mask is None: return 0\n",
        "    fixed = inp.copy()\n",
        "    k = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (dilate, dilate))\n",
        "    cnt = 0\n",
        "    for _, r in translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id].iterrows():\n",
        "        if not _has_valid_trans(r): continue\n",
        "        x, y, w, h = int(r[\"x\"]), int(r[\"y\"]), int(r[\"width\"]), int(r[\"height\"])\n",
        "        m = mask[y:y+h, x:x+w]\n",
        "        if m.max() == 0: continue\n",
        "        dil = cv2.dilate(m, k)\n",
        "        ring = (dil > 0) & (m == 0)\n",
        "        bg = np.median(orig[y:y+h, x:x+w][ring], axis=0).astype(np.uint8) if ring.sum() else np.array([255,255,255], np.uint8)\n",
        "        fixed[y:y+h, x:x+w][dil > 0] = bg\n",
        "        cnt += 1\n",
        "    if cnt: save_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\", fixed, quality=95)\n",
        "    return cnt\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── 6) clean box restore ──\n",
        "def restore_boxes(page_id):\n",
        "    inp = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\")\n",
        "    if inp is None: return 0\n",
        "    fixed = inp.copy(); cnt = 0\n",
        "    for _, r in translation_df[(translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id) & (translation_df[\"region_type\"] == \"narrator\")].iterrows():\n",
        "        x, y, w, h = int(r[\"x\"]), int(r[\"y\"]), int(r[\"width\"]), int(r[\"height\"])\n",
        "        cv2.rectangle(fixed, (x, y), (x+w, y+h), (255,255,255), -1)\n",
        "        cv2.rectangle(fixed, (x, y), (x+w, y+h), (0,0,0), 2)\n",
        "        cnt += 1\n",
        "    if cnt: save_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\", fixed, quality=95)\n",
        "    return cnt\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── 7) vertical note render ──\n",
        "def _main_font_ok(ch):\n",
        "    o = ord(ch)\n",
        "    return (0x0980 <= o <= 0x09FF) or (0x0000 <= o <= 0x00FF) \\\n",
        "        or (0x2000 <= o <= 0x206F) or (0x20B0 <= o <= 0x20BF) or ch in \"→⇒➔➡—–…\"\n",
        "def _split_runs(text):\n",
        "    runs, cur, cur_main = [], \"\", None\n",
        "    for ch in text:\n",
        "        m = _main_font_ok(ch)\n",
        "        if cur and m == cur_main: cur += ch\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            if cur: runs.append((cur, cur_main))\n",
        "            cur, cur_main = ch, m\n",
        "    if cur: runs.append((cur, cur_main))\n",
        "    return runs\n",
        "_FB_CACHE = {}\n",
        "def get_font_fb(size, bold=False):\n",
        "    key = (int(size), bool(bold))\n",
        "    if key in _FB_CACHE: return _FB_CACHE[key]\n",
        "    try: f = ImageFont.truetype(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"rendering\"][\"font_fallback\"], int(size))\n",
        "    except Exception: f = get_font(size, bold)\n",
        "    _FB_CACHE[key] = f\n",
        "    return f\n",
        "def _qc_render_vertical(image_pil, text, x, y, w, h, bold, text_color, padding):\n",
        "    padding = padding or 4\n",
        "    size, lines, th = fit_font_size(text, h, w, bold=bold, padding=padding)\n",
        "    lh = get_line_height(get_font(size, bold))\n",
        "    tc = PILImage.new(\"RGBA\", (int(h), int(w)), (0,0,0,0))\n",
        "    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(tc)\n",
        "    sy = max(padding, (int(w) - lh*len(lines))//2)\n",
        "    for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n",
        "        runs = _split_runs(line); widths, total = [], 0\n",
        "        for t2, im in runs:\n",
        "            f = get_font(size, bold) if im else get_font_fb(size, bold)\n",
        "            widths.append(draw.textlength(t2, font=f)); total += widths[-1]\n",
        "        lx = (int(h)-total)//2; ly = sy + i*lh\n",
        "        for (t2, im), ww in zip(runs, widths):\n",
        "            f = get_font(size, bold) if im else get_font_fb(size, bold)\n",
        "            draw.text((lx, ly), t2, font=f, fill=text_color+(255,)); lx += ww\n",
        "    rot = tc.rotate(int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"rendering\"][\"vertical_rotate\"]), expand=True, resample=PILImage.BICUBIC)\n",
        "    image_pil.paste(rot, (x+w//2-rot.width//2, y+h//2-rot.height//2), rot)\n",
        "    return image_pil\n",
        "\n",
        "if not globals().get(\"_QC_FINAL_RENDER_PATCHED\"):\n",
        "    _base_render = globals().get(\"_qc_orig_render\", render_bengali_text)\n",
        "    def render_bengali_text(image_pil, text, x, y, w, h, angle=0.0,\n",
        "                            region_type=\"bubble\", bold=None, stroke_width=None,\n",
        "                            text_color=None, padding=None):\n",
        "        if text and str(text).strip() and w < 90 and h > 2*max(1, w) and abs(float(angle)) <= 1.0:\n",
        "            b = bold if bold is not None else (region_type in [\"sfx\", \"narrator\"])\n",
        "            return _qc_render_vertical(image_pil, text, x, y, w, h, b, (0,0,0), padding)\n",
        "        return _base_render(image_pil, text, x, y, w, h, angle, region_type,\n",
        "                            bold, stroke_width, text_color, padding)\n",
        "    globals()[\"render_bengali_text\"] = render_bengali_text\n",
        "    globals()[\"_QC_FINAL_RENDER_PATCHED\"] = True\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── 8) pipeline wrap ──\n",
        "def run_inpaint_render_all(force=False, require_translation=False):\n",
        "    apply_container_types()\n",
        "    for pid in MANGABD_MANIFEST[\"pages\"]:\n",
        "        mask_only_translated(pid)\n",
        "    run_inpaint_all(force=force, require_translation=False)\n",
        "    for pid, pg in MANGABD_MANIFEST[\"pages\"].items():\n",
        "        if pg[\"stages\"].get(\"inpaint\"):\n",
        "            kill_residual(pid)\n",
        "            restore_boxes(pid)\n",
        "    run_render_all(force=force, require_translation=require_translation)\n",
        "globals()[\"run_inpaint_render_all\"] = run_inpaint_render_all\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── একবার coordinate recovery (আগের corruption কাটাতে) ──\n",
        "print(f\"✅ Recovered {recover_coordinates()} region coords\")\n",
        "print(\"✅ QUALITY CORE FINAL active\")"
      ]

        "def _snap_white_box(gray, x, y, w, h):\n",
        "    H, W = gray.shape\n",
        "    _, white = cv2.threshold(gray, 200, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n",
        "    n, labels, stats, cent = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(white, 8)\n",
        "    cx, cy = min(W-1, x+w//2), min(H-1, y+h//2)\n",
        "    lab = labels[cy, cx]\n",
        "    if lab == 0:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "    bx, by, bw, bh, area = stats[lab]\n",
        "    fill = area/(bw*bh) if bw*bh else 0\n",
        "    if fill > 0.85 and bw >= 30 and bh >= 30:\n",
        "        return (int(bx), int(by), int(bw), int(bh))\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "def apply_container_types():\n",
        "    \"\"\"আয়তক্ষেত্র সাদা box গুলোকে narrator + সঠিক coordinate দেয়\"\"\"\n",
        "    changed = 0\n",
        "    for pid in translation_df[\"page_id\"].unique():\n",
        "        orig = load_image_artifact(pid, \"original\")\n",
        "        if orig is None:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        gray = cv2.cvtColor(orig, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n",
        "        rows = translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == pid]\n",
        "        for idx, r in rows.iterrows():\n",
        "            x, y, w, h = int(r[\"x\"]), int(r[\"y\"]), int(r[\"width\"]), int(r[\"height\"])\n",
        "            if w < 90 and h > 2*max(1, w):      # vertical note — skip\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "            box = _snap_white_box(gray, x, y, w, h)\n",
        "            if box:\n",
        "                bx, by, bw, bh = box\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"x\"] = bx\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"y\"] = by\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"width\"] = bw\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"height\"] = bh\n",
        "                translation_df.at[idx, \"region_type\"] = \"narrator\"\n",
        "                changed += 1\n",
        "    save_translation_df()\n",
        "    print(f\"✅ Container types applied ({changed} box snapped)\")\n",
        "    return changed\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── 4) Inpaint-এর ক্ষতি ঢাকতে clean box আঁকা ──\n",

        "        cv2.rectangle(fixed, (x, y), (x+w, y+h), (255, 255, 255), -1)\n",
