        "# ═══ RESIDUAL KILLER: translated region-এর original লেখা guaranteed মুছবে ═══\n",
        "import cv2, numpy as np\n",
        "\n",
        "def kill_residual(page_id, dilate=13):\n",
        "    orig = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "    inp  = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\")\n",
        "    mask = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"mask\")\n",
        "    if orig is None or inp is None or mask is None:\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "    fixed = inp.copy()\n",
        "    k = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (dilate, dilate))\n",
        "    cnt = 0\n",
        "    rows = translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id]\n",
        "    for _, r in rows.iterrows():\n",
        "        if not _has_valid_trans(r):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        x, y, w, h = int(r[\"x\"]), int(r[\"y\"]), int(r[\"width\"]), int(r[\"height\"])\n",
        "        m = mask[y:y+h, x:x+w]\n",
        "        if m.max() == 0:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        dil = cv2.dilate(m, k)\n",
        "        # background রং = original-এর ring pixel-এর median (color-agnostic)\n",
        "        ring = (dil > 0) & (m == 0)\n",
        "        if ring.sum() > 0:\n",
        "            bg = np.median(orig[y:y+h, x:x+w][ring], axis=0).astype(np.uint8)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            bg = np.array([255, 255, 255], np.uint8)\n",
        "        fixed[y:y+h, x:x+w][dil > 0] = bg\n",
        "        cnt += 1\n",
        "    if cnt:\n",
        "        save_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\", fixed, quality=95)\n",
        "    return cnt\n",
        "\n",
        "# pipeline wrap: inpaint → kill_residual → restore_boxes → render\n",
        "def run_inpaint_render_all(force=False, require_translation=False):\n",
        "    apply_container_types()\n",
        "    for pid in MANGABD_MANIFEST[\"pages\"]:\n",
        "        mask_only_translated(pid)\n",
        "    run_inpaint_all(force=force, require_translation=False)\n",
        "    for pid, pg in MANGABD_MANIFEST[\"pages\"].items():\n",

        "            kill_residual(pid)\n",
        "            restore_boxes(pid)\n",
        "    run_render_all(force=force, require_translation=require_translation)\n",
        "globals()[\"run_inpaint_render_all\"] = run_inpaint_render_all\n",
        "print(\"✅ RESIDUAL KILLER active: original লেখা আর কোনো page-এ থেকে যাবে না\")"

        "# ═══ kill_residual v3: শুধু solid box-এ, pixel-level, border/face-safe ═══\n",
        "import cv2, numpy as np\n",
        "\n",
        "def kill_residual(page_id, dilate=3, margin=5):\n",
        "    orig = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "    inp  = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\")\n",
        "    if orig is None or inp is None:\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "    fixed = inp.copy()\n",
        "    k = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (dilate, dilate))\n",
        "    cnt = 0\n",
        "    for _, r in translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id].iterrows():\n",
        "        if not _has_valid_trans(r):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        x, y, w, h = int(r[\"x\"]), int(r[\"y\"]), int(r[\"width\"]), int(r[\"height\"])\n",
        "        crop = orig[y:y+h, x:x+w]\n",
        "        if crop.size == 0:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        bg = np.median(crop.reshape(-1, 3), axis=0)\n",
        "        dist = np.abs(crop.astype(int) - bg.astype(int)).sum(axis=2)\n",
        "        uniform_frac = float((dist < 60).mean())\n",
        "        # ★ solid box না হলে (bubble/art/মুখ) → হাতই দিই না\n",
        "        if uniform_frac <= 0.8:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        # solid box → শুধু লেখার pixel (pixel-level)\n",
        "        textm = (dist >= 60).astype(np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "        # ★ border বাঁচাতে চারপাশের margin বাদ\n",
        "        textm[:margin, :] = 0; textm[-margin:, :] = 0\n",
        "        textm[:, :margin] = 0; textm[:, -margin:] = 0\n",
        "        dil = cv2.dilate(textm, k)\n",
        "        fixed[y:y+h, x:x+w][dil > 0] = bg.astype(np.uint8)\n",
        "        cnt += 1\n",
        "    if cnt:\n",
        "        save_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\", fixed, quality=95)\n",
        "    return cnt\n",
        "\n",
        "globals()[\"kill_residual\"] = kill_residual\n",
        "print(\"✅ kill_residual v3 active: pixel-level, box-only, face/border-safe\")\n",
        "\n",
        "run_inpaint_render_all(force=True)"
      ]
    },

        "\n",
        "def sanitize_filename(filename):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Filename safe করে।\n",
        "    Spaces, brackets, invalid characters remove/clean করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    base = Path(str(filename)).name.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not base:\n",
        "        base = \"page.jpg\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Space to underscore\n",
        "    base = base.replace(\" \", \"_\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Invalid characters to underscore\n",
        "    base = re.sub(r\"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]\", \"_\", base)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Multiple underscores collapse\n",
        "    base = re.sub(r\"_+\", \"_\", base)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Split stem and extension\n",
        "    p = Path(base)\n",
        "\n",
        "    stem = p.stem.strip(\"_\")\n",
        "    ext = p.suffix.lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not ext:\n",
        "        ext = \".jpg\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not stem:\n",
        "        stem = \"page\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Remove trailing underscore before extension\n",
        "    stem = re.sub(r\"_+$\", \"\", stem)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return stem + ext\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def decode_image_bytes(content):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Uploaded bytes থেকে BGR image decode করে।\n",
        "    EXIF rotation handle করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        pil_img = PILImage.open(io.BytesIO(content))\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            pil_img = ImageOps.exif_transpose(pil_img)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        if pil_img.mode in (\"RGBA\", \"P\", \"LA\"):\n",
        "            pil_img = pil_img.convert(\"RGB\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        img_rgb = np.array(pil_img)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if len(img_rgb.shape) == 2:\n",
        "            img_bgr = cv2.cvtColor(img_rgb, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            img_bgr = cv2.cvtColor(img_rgb, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)\n",
        "\n",
        "        return img_bgr\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            nparr = np.frombuffer(content, np.uint8)\n",
        "            img_bgr = cv2.imdecode(nparr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)\n",
        "            return img_bgr\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            return None\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def is_valid_image_size(image_bgr):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Image minimum size requirement check করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if image_bgr is None or image_bgr.size == 0:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "    min_w = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"input\"].get(\"min_image_width\", 50))\n",
        "    min_h = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"input\"].get(\"min_image_height\", 50))\n",
        "\n",
        "    return w >= min_w and h >= min_h\n",
        "\n",

        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return page_id\n",
        "\n",

        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return page_ids\n",

        "\n",
        "def add_images_from_directory(directory, pattern=\"*\", overwrite=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Drive/local directory থেকে images add করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Example:\n",
        "        add_images_from_directory(\n",
        "            \"/content/drive/MyDrive/MangaBD/input/chapter_01\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    directory = Path(directory)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not directory.exists():\n",
        "        raise FileNotFoundError(f\"Directory not found: {directory}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    supported = set(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"input\"].get(\n",
        "            \"supported_extensions\",\n",
        "            [\".jpg\", \".jpeg\", \".png\", \".webp\", \".bmp\"],\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    files = sorted(directory.glob(pattern))\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_ids = []\n",
        "    success_count = 0\n",
        "    failed_count = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📂 Scanning: {directory}\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    for file_path in files:\n",
        "        if file_path.suffix.lower() not in supported:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            image_bgr = cv2.imread(str(file_path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if image_bgr is None:\n",
        "                print(f\"  ❌ {file_path.name}: decode failed\")\n",
        "                failed_count += 1\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not is_valid_image_size(image_bgr):\n",
        "                h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "                print(f\"  ⚠️ {file_path.name}: too small ({w}x{h}) — skipped\")\n",
        "                failed_count += 1\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            page_id = register_image_array(\n",
        "                file_path.name,\n",
        "                image_bgr,\n",
        "                overwrite=overwrite,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            page_ids.append(page_id)\n",
        "            success_count += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"  ✅ {page_id}: {w}x{h}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            failed_count += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Directory add failed [{file_path.name}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"  ❌ {file_path.name}: {str(exc)[:120]}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Added: {success_count}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  ❌ Failed/skipped: {failed_count}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return page_ids\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Page registration ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",

        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. Pipeline stage helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "PIPELINE_STAGES = [\n",
        "    \"upload\",\n",
        "    \"detect\",\n",
        "    \"ocr\",\n",
        "    \"translate\",\n",
        "    \"inpaint\",\n",
        "    \"render\",\n",
        "    \"export\",\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "STAGE_DEPENDENCIES = {\n",
        "    \"detect\": \"upload\",\n",
        "    \"ocr\": \"detect\",\n",
        "    \"translate\": \"ocr\",\n",
        "    \"inpaint\": \"detect\",\n",
        "    \"render\": \"inpaint\",\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def can_run_stage(page_id, stage, require_translation_for_render=True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা stage চালানোর জন্য dependency ready কিনা check করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if stage not in PIPELINE_STAGES:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    if stage == \"upload\":\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page is None:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    stages = page.get(\"stages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    dependency = STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(stage)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if dependency is None:\n",
        "        return True\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not stages.get(dependency, False):\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    if stage == \"render\" and require_translation_for_render:\n",
        "        if not stages.get(\"translate\", False):\n",
        "            return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    return True\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_page_stage_summary(page_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর stage icons return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page is None:\n",
        "        return \"❌ page missing\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    stages = page.get(\"stages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    icons = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for stage in PIPELINE_STAGES:\n",
        "        if stage == \"export\":\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if stages.get(stage, False):\n",
        "            icons.append(\"✅\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            icons.append(\"⬜\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return \" \".join(icons)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Pipeline stage helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. Pipeline runners\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_uploaded_page_ids():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Manifest থেকে uploaded page IDs return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    return [\n",
        "        page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "        for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"upload\", False)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_detection_all(force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    সব uploaded page-এ detection চালায়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"detect_page\" not in globals():\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"detect_page not found. আগে Cell 6 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_ids = get_uploaded_page_ids()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not page_ids:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No uploaded pages found. আগে upload_images() চালান।\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  🔎 Detection: {len(page_ids)} pages\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for index, page_id in enumerate(page_ids, start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"  [{index}/{len(page_ids)}] {page_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            if is_stage_done(page_id, \"detect\") and not force:\n",
        "                print(\"    ✅ Detection cached\")\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                image_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                if image_bgr is None:\n",
        "                    raise RuntimeError(\"Original image artifact missing\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                detect_page(page_id, image_bgr, force=force)\n",
        "\n",
        "            results.append(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Detection failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            if \"add_page_error\" in globals():\n",
        "                add_page_error(page_id, \"detect\", str(exc)[:300])\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return results\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_ocr_all(force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    সব detected page-এ OCR চালায়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"ocr_page\" not in globals():\n",
        "        raise RuntimeError(\"ocr_page not found. আগে Cell 7 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    target_pages = [\n",
        "        page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "        for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"detect\", False)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not target_pages:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No detected pages found. আগে detection চালান।\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📖 OCR: {len(target_pages)} pages\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for index, page_id in enumerate(target_pages, start=1):\n",
        "        print(f\"  [{index}/{len(target_pages)}] {page_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            if is_stage_done(page_id, \"ocr\") and not force:\n",
        "                print(\"    ✅ OCR cached\")\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                ocr_page(page_id, force=force)\n",
        "\n",
        "            results.append(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"OCR failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            if \"add_page_error\" in globals():\n",
        "                add_page_error(page_id, \"ocr\", str(exc)[:300])\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return results\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_detection_ocr_all(force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Detection + OCR একসাথে চালায়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",

        "\n",

        "    )\n",

        "\n",
        "def run_full_pipeline(engine=None, force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Full pipeline:\n",
        "\n",
        "        upload → detect → OCR → translate → inpaint → render\n",

        "def run_test(name, condition, details=\"\"):\n",
        "    status = \"✅\" if condition else \"❌\"\n",
        "    print(f\"  {status} {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if details and not condition:\n",
        "        print(f\"     {details}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    tests.append(bool(condition))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 1: sanitize filename\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    safe = sanitize_filename(\"my page (1).jpg\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"sanitize_filename\",\n",
        "        safe == \"my_page_1.jpg\",\n",
        "        safe,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"sanitize_filename\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 2: decode image bytes\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_img = np.ones((120, 160, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok_encode, buffer = cv2.imencode(\".png\", test_img)\n",
        "\n",
        "    decoded = decode_image_bytes(buffer.tobytes())\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"decode_image_bytes\",\n",
        "        decoded is not None and decoded.shape[:2] == (120, 160),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"decode_image_bytes\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 3: register synthetic page\n",
        "test_page_id = None\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_img = np.ones((200, 300, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "    cv2.putText(\n",
        "        test_img,\n",
        "        \"TEST\",\n",
        "        (50, 100),\n",
        "        cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,\n",
        "        1.5,\n",
        "        (0, 0, 0),\n",
        "        3,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    test_page_id = register_image_array(\n",
        "        \"__cell11_test__.jpg\",\n",
        "        test_img,\n",
        "        overwrite=True,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(test_page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"register_image_array\",\n",
        "        page is not None and page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"upload\", False),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"register_image_array\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 4: stage dependency\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    if test_page_id is not None:\n",
        "        ok = (\n",
        "            can_run_stage(test_page_id, \"detect\") is True\n",
        "            and can_run_stage(test_page_id, \"ocr\") is False\n",
        "            and can_run_stage(test_page_id, \"render\") is False\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        ok = False\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"stage dependency check\",\n",
        "        ok,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"stage dependency check\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 5: stage summary\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    if test_page_id is not None:\n",
        "        summary = get_page_stage_summary(test_page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "        run_test(\n",
        "            \"page stage summary\",\n",
        "            isinstance(summary, str) and len(summary) > 0,\n",
        "            summary,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        run_test(\"page stage summary\", False, \"test page missing\")\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"page stage summary\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 6: pipeline status print\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    print_pipeline_status()\n",
        "    run_test(\"print_pipeline_status\", True)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"print_pipeline_status\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Cleanup test page\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    if test_page_id is not None and \"reset_page\" in globals():\n",
        "        reset_page(test_page_id, delete_artifacts=True)\n",
        "except Exception:\n",
        "    pass\n",
        "\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
