        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 1. Dependency check\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"MANGABD_CONFIG\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"MANGABD_CONFIG not found. আগে Cell 2 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"log_event\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"log_event not found. আগে Cell 2 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"MANGABD_MANIFEST\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"MANGABD_MANIFEST not found. আগে Cell 3 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"get_page\" not in globals():\n",
        "    raise RuntimeError(\"Storage functions not found. আগে Cell 3 চালান।\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  1. Dependency check\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ MANGABD_CONFIG found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ log_event found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ MANGABD_MANIFEST found\")\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ storage functions found\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 2. QA config defaults\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  2. QA config defaults\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG.setdefault(\"qa\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].setdefault(\"low_confidence_threshold\", 0.65)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].setdefault(\"critical_confidence_threshold\", 0.35)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].setdefault(\"max_translation_length_ratio\", 5.0)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].setdefault(\"visual_qa\", False)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].setdefault(\"visual_qa_sample_ratio\", 0.25)\n",
        "MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].setdefault(\"visual_qa_min_confidence\", 0.55)\n",
        "\n",
        "SEVERITY_WEIGHTS = {\n",
        "    \"critical\": 20,\n",
        "    \"warning\": 7,\n",
        "    \"info\": 1,\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ QA config ready\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Low confidence threshold : \"\n",
        "      f\"{MANGABD_CONFIG['qa']['low_confidence_threshold']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Critical confidence      : \"\n",
        "      f\"{MANGABD_CONFIG['qa']['critical_confidence_threshold']}\")\n",
        "print(f\"     Visual QA default        : \"\n",
        "      f\"{MANGABD_CONFIG['qa']['visual_qa']}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 3. QA globals\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"quality_reports\" not in globals():\n",
        "    quality_reports = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"quality_warnings\" not in globals():\n",
        "    quality_warnings = []\n",
        "\n",
        "if \"manual_review_flags_dict\" not in globals():\n",
        "    manual_review_flags_dict = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "# Compatibility with older V11-style code\n",
        "if \"manual_review_flags\" not in globals():\n",
        "    manual_review_flags = []\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def clear_page_qa(page_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর পুরনো QA data মুছে ফেলে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global quality_warnings, manual_review_flags\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page_id in quality_reports:\n",
        "        del quality_reports[page_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    quality_warnings = [\n",
        "        item for item in quality_warnings\n",
        "        if item.get(\"page_id\") != page_id and item.get(\"page\") != page_id\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    keys_to_remove = [\n",
        "        key for key in manual_review_flags_dict.keys()\n",
        "        if key[0] == page_id\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key in keys_to_remove:\n",
        "        del manual_review_flags_dict[key]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Rebuild legacy list\n",
        "    manual_review_flags = list(manual_review_flags_dict.values())\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ QA globals ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 4. QA issue helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def add_quality_issue(\n",
        "    page_id,\n",
        "    region_id,\n",
        "    category,\n",
        "    issue,\n",
        "    severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "    suggestion=\"\",\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    QA issue add করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    category:\n",
        "        ocr | translation | render | inpaint | general\n",
        "    severity:\n",
        "        critical | warning | info\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    entry = {\n",
        "        \"page_id\": page_id,\n",
        "        \"page\": page_id,\n",
        "        \"region_id\": int(region_id) if region_id is not None else 0,\n",
        "        \"category\": category,\n",
        "        \"issue\": issue,\n",
        "        \"severity\": severity,\n",
        "        \"suggestion\": suggestion,\n",
        "        \"timestamp\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    quality_warnings.append(entry)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if severity in [\"critical\", \"warning\"]:\n",
        "        key = (page_id, int(region_id) if region_id is not None else 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        manual_review_flags_dict[key] = {\n",
        "            \"page\": page_id,\n",
        "            \"page_id\": page_id,\n",
        "            \"region_id\": int(region_id) if region_id is not None else 0,\n",
        "            \"issue\": f\"[{category}] {issue}\",\n",
        "            \"severity\": \"🔴\" if severity == \"critical\" else \"🟡\",\n",
        "            \"suggestion\": suggestion,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "        global manual_review_flags\n",
        "        manual_review_flags = list(manual_review_flags_dict.values())\n",
        "\n",
        "    return entry\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_page_issues(page_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর সব QA issue return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return [\n",
        "        item for item in quality_warnings\n",
        "        if item.get(\"page_id\") == page_id or item.get(\"page\") == page_id\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ QA issue helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. Text helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def qa_validate_bengali(text):\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    return any(\"\\u0980\" <= ch <= \"\\u09FF\" for ch in str(text))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def qa_is_special_marker(text):\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    markers = {\n",
        "        \"[EMPTY]\",\n",
        "        \"[OCR_FAILED]\",\n",
        "        \"[SFX]\",\n",
        "        \"[SKIP:OCR_FAILED]\",\n",
        "        \"[TRANS_FAILED]\",\n",
        "        \"[UNTRANSLATED]\",\n",
        "        \"nan\",\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(text).strip() in markers\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def qa_clean_text(text):\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = str(text).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"clean_translation\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            return clean_translation(text)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    return text\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ QA text helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. OCR quality inspection\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def inspect_ocr_quality(page_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    OCR quality inspect করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Checks:\n",
        "    - empty OCR\n",
        "    - failed OCR\n",
        "    - low confidence\n",
        "    - non-English output\n",
        "    - suspicious very short text\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    issues = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    low_conf = float(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].get(\n",
        "            \"low_confidence_threshold\",\n",
        "            0.65,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    critical_conf = float(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].get(\n",
        "            \"critical_confidence_threshold\",\n",
        "            0.35,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Try OCR artifact first\n",
        "    ocr_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"load_json_artifact\" in globals():\n",
        "        ocr_data = load_json_artifact(page_id, \"ocr\", default=[])\n",
        "\n",
        "    if ocr_data:\n",
        "        for item in ocr_data:\n",
        "            region = item.get(\"region\", {})\n",
        "            ocr = item.get(\"ocr\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "            region_id = int(region.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "            text = str(ocr.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "            confidence = float(ocr.get(\"confidence\", 0.0))\n",
        "            is_english = bool(ocr.get(\"is_english\", False))\n",
        "            status = str(ocr.get(\"status\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not text or qa_is_special_marker(text):\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"ocr\",\n",
        "                    \"OCR empty or failed\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"Re-run OCR or manually check region crop\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            if status == \"failed\" or confidence <= 0.0:\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"ocr\",\n",
        "                    \"OCR failed\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"critical\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"Re-run OCR with better crop/preprocessing\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "            elif confidence < critical_conf:\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"ocr\",\n",
        "                    f\"Very low OCR confidence ({confidence:.2f})\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"critical\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"Manual OCR correction recommended\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "            elif confidence < low_conf:\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"ocr\",\n",
        "                    f\"Low OCR confidence ({confidence:.2f})\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"Check OCR text manually\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "            if text and not qa_is_special_marker(text) and not is_english:\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"ocr\",\n",
        "                    \"OCR output does not look English\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"Verify source text or OCR prompt\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "            if (\n",
        "                text\n",
        "                and not qa_is_special_marker(text)\n",
        "                and len(text) <= 1\n",
        "                and confidence > 0.6\n",
        "            ):\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"ocr\",\n",
        "                    \"Suspiciously short OCR output\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"info\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"May be valid single character or OCR miss\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Fallback: translation_df\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_rows = translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    for _, row in page_rows.iterrows():\n",
        "        region_id = int(row.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "        text = str(row.get(\"original_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "        confidence = float(row.get(\"confidence\", 0.0))\n",
        "        is_english = bool(row.get(\"is_english\", False))\n",
        "        status = str(row.get(\"status\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not text or qa_is_special_marker(text):\n",
        "            issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                region_id,\n",
        "                \"ocr\",\n",
        "                \"OCR empty or failed\",\n",
        "                severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                suggestion=\"Re-run OCR or manually check region crop\",\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"failed\" in status or confidence <= 0.0:\n",
        "            issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                region_id,\n",
        "                \"ocr\",\n",
        "                \"OCR failed\",\n",
        "                severity=\"critical\",\n",
        "                suggestion=\"Re-run OCR with better crop/preprocessing\",\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        elif confidence < critical_conf:\n",
        "            issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                region_id,\n",
        "                \"ocr\",\n",
        "                f\"Very low OCR confidence ({confidence:.2f})\",\n",
        "                severity=\"critical\",\n",
        "                suggestion=\"Manual OCR correction recommended\",\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        elif confidence < low_conf:\n",
        "            issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                region_id,\n",
        "                \"ocr\",\n",
        "                f\"Low OCR confidence ({confidence:.2f})\",\n",
        "                severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                suggestion=\"Check OCR text manually\",\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if text and not qa_is_special_marker(text) and not is_english:\n",
        "            issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                region_id,\n",
        "                \"ocr\",\n",
        "                \"OCR output does not look English\",\n",
        "                severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                suggestion=\"Verify source text or OCR prompt\",\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ OCR quality inspector ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 7. Translation quality inspection\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def inspect_translation_quality(page_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Translation quality inspect করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Checks:\n",
        "    - missing translation\n",
        "    - non-Bengali translation\n",
        "    - translation same as original\n",
        "    - excessively long translation\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    issues = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_rows = translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    max_ratio = float(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].get(\"max_translation_length_ratio\", 5.0)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    for _, row in page_rows.iterrows():\n",
        "        region_id = int(row.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        original_text = str(row.get(\"original_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "        translated_text = str(row.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # OCR marker rows\n",
        "        if qa_is_special_marker(original_text):\n",
        "            if translated_text and not qa_is_special_marker(translated_text):\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"translation\",\n",
        "                    \"Translated text present for OCR-skipped region\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"info\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"Okay if manually added\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Missing translation\n",
        "        if not translated_text or qa_is_special_marker(translated_text):\n",
        "            issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                region_id,\n",
        "                \"translation\",\n",
        "                \"Missing translation\",\n",
        "                severity=\"critical\",\n",
        "                suggestion=\"Translate manually or re-run translator\",\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Non-Bengali translation\n",
        "        if not qa_validate_bengali(translated_text):\n",
        "            issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                region_id,\n",
        "                \"translation\",\n",
        "                \"Translation is not Bengali\",\n",
        "                severity=\"critical\",\n",
        "                suggestion=\"Re-translate or manually fix\",\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Same as original\n",
        "        if translated_text == original_text:\n",
        "            issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                region_id,\n",
        "                \"translation\",\n",
        "                \"Translation same as original English\",\n",
        "                severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                suggestion=\"May be proper noun or translation failed\",\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Too long\n",
        "        original_len = len(original_text)\n",
        "        translated_len = len(translated_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if original_len > 0:\n",
        "            ratio = translated_len / max(1, original_len)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if ratio > max_ratio and translated_len > 60:\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"translation\",\n",
        "                    f\"Translation too long ({ratio:.1f}x original)\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"Shorten translation to fit bubble\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Translation quality inspector ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 8. Render quality inspection\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def inspect_render_quality(page_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Rendering quality estimate করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Checks:\n",
        "    - page not rendered\n",
        "    - font at minimum size\n",
        "    - estimated overflow\n",
        "    - too many lines in small region\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    issues = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page is None:\n",
        "        return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "    rendered = bool(page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"render\", False))\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not rendered:\n",
        "        issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "            page_id,\n",
        "            0,\n",
        "            \"render\",\n",
        "            \"Page not rendered yet\",\n",
        "            severity=\"info\",\n",
        "            suggestion=\"Run render_page() or render_all_pages()\",\n",
        "        ))\n",
        "        return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_rows = translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    font_size_min = int(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"rendering\", {}).get(\"font_size_min\", 8)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    for _, row in page_rows.iterrows():\n",
        "        region_id = int(row.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        translated_text = str(row.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not translated_text or qa_is_special_marker(translated_text):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not qa_validate_bengali(translated_text):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        w = int(row.get(\"width\", 0))\n",
        "        h = int(row.get(\"height\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if w < 5 or h < 5:\n",
        "            issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                page_id,\n",
        "                region_id,\n",
        "                \"render\",\n",
        "                \"Region too small for rendering\",\n",
        "                severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                suggestion=\"Check detection box or delete false positive\",\n",
        "            ))\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        # If fit_font_size available, estimate overflow\n",
        "        if \"fit_font_size\" in globals():\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                region_type = str(row.get(\"region_type\", \"bubble\"))\n",
        "                bold = region_type in [\"sfx\", \"narrator\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "                font_size, lines, total_height = fit_font_size(\n",
        "                    translated_text,\n",
        "                    w,\n",
        "                    h,\n",
        "                    bold=bold,\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                if font_size <= font_size_min:\n",
        "                    issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                        page_id,\n",
        "                        region_id,\n",
        "                        \"render\",\n",
        "                        f\"Font at minimum size ({font_size}px)\",\n",
        "                        severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                        suggestion=\"Shorten translation or enlarge region\",\n",
        "                    ))\n",
        "\n",
        "                padding_ratio = float(\n",
        "                    MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"rendering\", {}).get(\n",
        "                        \"padding_ratio\",\n",
        "                        0.08,\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                padding = max(4, int(min(w, h) * padding_ratio))\n",
        "                available_height = max(10, h - 2 * padding)\n",
        "\n",
        "                if total_height > available_height * 1.05:\n",
        "                    issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                        page_id,\n",
        "                        region_id,\n",
        "                        \"render\",\n",
        "                        \"Estimated text overflow\",\n",
        "                        severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                        suggestion=\"Shorten translation or reduce font size\",\n",
        "                    ))\n",
        "\n",
        "                if len(lines) > 4 and h < 80:\n",
        "                    issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                        page_id,\n",
        "                        region_id,\n",
        "                        \"render\",\n",
        "                        f\"Too many lines ({len(lines)}) in small region\",\n",
        "                        severity=\"info\",\n",
        "                        suggestion=\"Consider shorter translation\",\n",
        "                    ))\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as exc:\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"render\",\n",
        "                    f\"Render estimation failed: {str(exc)[:60]}\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"info\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"Check renderer functions\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "    return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Render quality inspector ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 9. Page QA pipeline\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def compute_page_score(page_id):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর quality score compute করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    issues = get_page_issues(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    penalty = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for issue in issues:\n",
        "        severity = issue.get(\"severity\", \"info\")\n",
        "        penalty += SEVERITY_WEIGHTS.get(severity, 1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    score = max(0, 100 - penalty)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return score\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def inspect_page(page_id, force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    একটা page-এর full QA inspection চালায়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    page = get_page(page_id, required=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Cache check\n",
        "    if is_stage_done(page_id, \"qa\") and not force:\n",
        "        cached = None\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"load_json_artifact\" in globals():\n",
        "            cached = load_json_artifact(page_id, \"qa\", default=None)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if cached is not None:\n",
        "            print(f\"    ✅ QA cached: {page_id}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            quality_reports[page_id] = cached.get(\"issues\", [])\n",
        "\n",
        "            return cached\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"reset_page\" in globals():\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                reset_page(page_id, from_stage=\"qa\")\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    clear_page_qa(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    🔬 Inspecting OCR...\")\n",
        "    ocr_issues = inspect_ocr_quality(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    🔬 Inspecting translation...\")\n",
        "    translation_issues = inspect_translation_quality(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    🔬 Inspecting render...\")\n",
        "    render_issues = inspect_render_quality(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_issues = ocr_issues + translation_issues + render_issues\n",
        "\n",
        "    score = compute_page_score(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "    counts = {\n",
        "        \"critical\": sum(1 for i in all_issues if i[\"severity\"] == \"critical\"),\n",
        "        \"warning\": sum(1 for i in all_issues if i[\"severity\"] == \"warning\"),\n",
        "        \"info\": sum(1 for i in all_issues if i[\"severity\"] == \"info\"),\n",
        "        \"total\": len(all_issues),\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    report = {\n",
        "        \"page_id\": page_id,\n",
        "        \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "        \"score\": score,\n",
        "        \"counts\": counts,\n",
        "        \"issues\": all_issues,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    quality_reports[page_id] = all_issues\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"save_json_artifact\" in globals():\n",
        "        save_json_artifact(page_id, \"qa\", report)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"mark_stage_done\" in globals():\n",
        "        mark_stage_done(\n",
        "            page_id,\n",
        "            \"qa\",\n",
        "            meta={\n",
        "                \"qa_score\": score,\n",
        "                \"qa_issues\": counts[\"total\"],\n",
        "                \"qa_critical\": counts[\"critical\"],\n",
        "                \"qa_warning\": counts[\"warning\"],\n",
        "            },\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"QA completed [{page_id}]: score={score}, issues={counts['total']}\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "        page_id=page_id,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"    ✅ QA done: score={score}, \"\n",
        "        f\"critical={counts['critical']}, \"\n",
        "        f\"warning={counts['warning']}, \"\n",
        "        f\"info={counts['info']}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return report\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def inspect_all_pages(force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Manifest-এর সব pages QA inspect করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    target_pages = [\n",
        "        page for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"ocr\", False)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not target_pages:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No OCR-done pages found. আগে OCR চালান।\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📂 {len(target_pages)} pages QA হবে\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    reports = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for index, page in enumerate(target_pages, start=1):\n",
        "        page_id = page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╔═══════════════════════════════════════════╗\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ║  [{index}/{len(target_pages)}] {page_id}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ╚═══════════════════════════════════════════╝\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            report = inspect_page(page_id, force=force)\n",
        "            reports.append(report)\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"QA failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:160]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"ERROR\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            if \"add_page_error\" in globals():\n",
        "                add_page_error(page_id, \"qa\", str(exc)[:300])\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print_quality_summary()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return reports\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Page QA pipeline ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 10. Quality summary / report\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def compute_overall_quality_score():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Overall quality score compute করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_ids = [\n",
        "        page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "        for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"ocr\", False)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not page_ids:\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"score\": 0,\n",
        "            \"pages\": 0,\n",
        "            \"critical\": 0,\n",
        "            \"warning\": 0,\n",
        "            \"info\": 0,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    total_score = 0\n",
        "    total_critical = 0\n",
        "    total_warning = 0\n",
        "    total_info = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page_id in page_ids:\n",
        "        issues = get_page_issues(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "        critical = sum(1 for i in issues if i.get(\"severity\") == \"critical\")\n",
        "        warning = sum(1 for i in issues if i.get(\"severity\") == \"warning\")\n",
        "        info = sum(1 for i in issues if i.get(\"severity\") == \"info\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        penalty = (\n",
        "            critical * SEVERITY_WEIGHTS[\"critical\"]\n",
        "            + warning * SEVERITY_WEIGHTS[\"warning\"]\n",
        "            + info * SEVERITY_WEIGHTS[\"info\"]\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        score = max(0, 100 - penalty)\n",
        "\n",
        "        total_score += score\n",
        "        total_critical += critical\n",
        "        total_warning += warning\n",
        "        total_info += info\n",
        "\n",
        "    average_score = int(total_score / len(page_ids))\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"score\": average_score,\n",
        "        \"pages\": len(page_ids),\n",
        "        \"critical\": total_critical,\n",
        "        \"warning\": total_warning,\n",
        "        \"info\": total_info,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def print_quality_summary():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Overall quality summary print করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    summary = compute_overall_quality_score()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "    print(\"  Quality Summary\")\n",
        "    print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  Pages inspected : {summary['pages']}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  Overall score   : {summary['score']}/100\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  Critical issues : {summary['critical']}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  Warnings        : {summary['warning']}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  Info            : {summary['info']}\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if summary[\"score\"] >= 90:\n",
        "        print(\"  🌟 Excellent quality\")\n",
        "    elif summary[\"score\"] >= 75:\n",
        "        print(\"  ✅ Good quality\")\n",
        "    elif summary[\"score\"] >= 60:\n",
        "        print(\"  🟡 Acceptable quality\")\n",
        "    elif summary[\"score\"] >= 40:\n",
        "        print(\"  🟠 Needs improvement\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        print(\"  🔴 Poor quality — manual review strongly recommended\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def print_quality_report(page_id=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Page-wise বা full quality report print করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page_id is not None:\n",
        "        issues = get_page_issues(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "        print(f\"  Quality Report: {page_id}\")\n",
        "        print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not issues:\n",
        "            print(\"  ✅ No issues found\")\n",
        "            print()\n",
        "            return\n",
        "\n",
        "        for issue in issues:\n",
        "            severity_icon = \"🔴\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            if issue[\"severity\"] == \"warning\":\n",
        "                severity_icon = \"🟡\"\n",
        "            elif issue[\"severity\"] == \"info\":\n",
        "                severity_icon = \"ℹ️\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(\n",
        "                f\"  {severity_icon} \"\n",
        "                f\"[{issue['category']}] \"\n",
        "                f\"region {issue['region_id']}: \"\n",
        "                f\"{issue['issue']}\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            if issue.get(\"suggestion\"):\n",
        "                print(f\"      → {issue['suggestion']}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    # All pages\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "    print(\"  Full Quality Report\")\n",
        "    print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page in pages.values():\n",
        "        pid = page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"ocr\", False):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        issues = get_page_issues(pid)\n",
        "        score = compute_page_score(pid)\n",
        "\n",
        "        critical = sum(1 for i in issues if i[\"severity\"] == \"critical\")\n",
        "        warning = sum(1 for i in issues if i[\"severity\"] == \"warning\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"  {pid}: \"\n",
        "            f\"score={score}, \"\n",
        "            f\"critical={critical}, \"\n",
        "            f\"warning={warning}, \"\n",
        "            f\"total={len(issues)}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_quality_report_json():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Quality report JSON export করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir = Path(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"].get(\n",
        "            \"session_export_dir\",\n",
        "            MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"session_dir\"],\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    report_path = export_dir / \"quality_report.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    summary = compute_overall_quality_score()\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_reports = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page in pages.values():\n",
        "        pid = page.get(\"page_id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"ocr\", False):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        page_reports[pid] = {\n",
        "            \"score\": compute_page_score(pid),\n",
        "            \"issues\": get_page_issues(pid),\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    data = {\n",
        "        \"session_id\": MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"session_id\", \"unknown\"),\n",
        "        \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "        \"summary\": summary,\n",
        "        \"pages\": page_reports,\n",
        "        \"manual_review_flags\": list(manual_review_flags_dict.values()),\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    report_path.write_text(\n",
        "        json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),\n",
        "        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Quality report exported: {report_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(report_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Quality reporting ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 11. Manual fix helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def apply_translation_fixes(fixes_dict, reset_render=True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Translation fixes apply করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    fixes_dict format:\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            (page_id_or_filename, region_id): \"Bengali translation\",\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        if \"load_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "            load_translation_df()\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"  ❌ translation_df not found\")\n",
        "            return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(translation_df) == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ translation_df empty\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    applied = 0\n",
        "    pages_changed = set()\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key, text in fixes_dict.items():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            page_key, region_id = key\n",
        "            region_id = int(region_id)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        text = qa_clean_text(text)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not text:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        mask = (\n",
        "            (\n",
        "                (translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_key)\n",
        "                | (translation_df[\"page\"] == page_key)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            & (translation_df[\"id\"] == region_id)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not mask.any():\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        translation_df.loc[mask, \"translated_text\"] = text\n",
        "        translation_df.loc[mask, \"translator_engine\"] = \"manual_fix\"\n",
        "        translation_df.loc[mask, \"bengali_valid\"] = qa_validate_bengali(text)\n",
        "        translation_df.loc[mask, \"status\"] = \"translated\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        applied += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "        changed_pages = translation_df.loc[mask, \"page_id\"].unique()\n",
        "\n",
        "        for pid in changed_pages:\n",
        "            pages_changed.add(pid)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if applied > 0 and \"save_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "        save_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if reset_render and pages_changed and \"reset_page\" in globals():\n",
        "        for pid in pages_changed:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                reset_page(pid, from_stage=\"render\")\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Translation fixes applied: {applied}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if pages_changed:\n",
        "        print(f\"  🔄 Pages marked for re-render: {len(pages_changed)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Manual translation fixes applied: {applied}\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return applied\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def change_region_type(fixes_dict, reset_render=True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Region type change করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    fixes_dict format:\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            (page_id_or_filename, region_id): \"bubble\",\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        print(\"  ❌ translation_df not found\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    valid_types = {\n",
        "        \"bubble\",\n",
        "        \"thought\",\n",
        "        \"narrator\",\n",
        "        \"sfx\",\n",
        "        \"overlay\",\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    applied = 0\n",
        "    pages_changed = set()\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key, region_type in fixes_dict.items():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            page_key, region_id = key\n",
        "            region_id = int(region_id)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        region_type = str(region_type).strip().lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if region_type not in valid_types:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        mask = (\n",
        "            (\n",
        "                (translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_key)\n",
        "                | (translation_df[\"page\"] == page_key)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            & (translation_df[\"id\"] == region_id)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not mask.any():\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        translation_df.loc[mask, \"region_type\"] = region_type\n",
        "\n",
        "        applied += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "        changed_pages = translation_df.loc[mask, \"page_id\"].unique()\n",
        "\n",
        "        for pid in changed_pages:\n",
        "            pages_changed.add(pid)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if applied > 0 and \"save_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "        save_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if reset_render and pages_changed and \"reset_page\" in globals():\n",
        "        for pid in pages_changed:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                reset_page(pid, from_stage=\"render\")\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Region type changes applied: {applied}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return applied\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def delete_regions(region_keys, reset_render=True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    False positive regions delete করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    region_keys format:\n",
        "        [\n",
        "            (page_id_or_filename, region_id),\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        print(\"  ❌ translation_df not found\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    deleted = 0\n",
        "    pages_changed = set()\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key in region_keys:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            page_key, region_id = key\n",
        "            region_id = int(region_id)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        mask = (\n",
        "            (\n",
        "                (translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_key)\n",
        "                | (translation_df[\"page\"] == page_key)\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            & (translation_df[\"id\"] == region_id)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not mask.any():\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        changed_pages = translation_df.loc[mask, \"page_id\"].unique()\n",
        "\n",
        "        translation_df = translation_df[~mask].reset_index(drop=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "        deleted += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "        for pid in changed_pages:\n",
        "            pages_changed.add(pid)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if deleted > 0 and \"save_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "        save_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if reset_render and pages_changed and \"reset_page\" in globals():\n",
        "        for pid in pages_changed:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                reset_page(pid, from_stage=\"render\")\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Regions deleted: {deleted}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if pages_changed:\n",
        "        print(f\"  🔄 Pages marked for re-render: {len(pages_changed)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return deleted\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def generate_fixes_template():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    যাদের translation fix দরকার তাদের জন্য Python dict template print করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        print(\"  ❌ translation_df not found\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"  🤖 Auto-generated fixes template:\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(\"  fixes = {\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    count = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for _, row in translation_df.iterrows():\n",
        "        page_id = str(row.get(\"page_id\", \"\"))\n",
        "        region_id = int(row.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        original_text = str(row.get(\"original_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "        translated_text = str(row.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        needs_fix = False\n",
        "\n",
        "        if qa_is_special_marker(original_text):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not translated_text or qa_is_special_marker(translated_text):\n",
        "            needs_fix = True\n",
        "\n",
        "        elif not qa_validate_bengali(translated_text):\n",
        "            needs_fix = True\n",
        "\n",
        "        elif translated_text == original_text:\n",
        "            needs_fix = True\n",
        "\n",
        "        if needs_fix:\n",
        "            original_preview = original_text[:40].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n",
        "            print(f'      (\"{page_id}\", {region_id}): \"\",  # orig: \"{original_preview}\"')\n",
        "            count += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"  }\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(f\"  # {count} regions need fix\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(\"  # তারপর চালান:\")\n",
        "    print(\"  apply_translation_fixes(fixes)\")\n",
        "    print(\"  render_all_pages(force=True)\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Manual fix helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 12. Optional visual QA\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def visual_qa_page(page_id, sample_ratio=None, force=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Optional visual QA.\n",
        "\n",
        "    Inpainted region crops-এ Qwen OCR চালিয়ে residual text খোঁজে।\n",
        "    এটা expensive, তাই default off।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].get(\"visual_qa\", False) and not force:\n",
        "        print(\"  ℹ️ Visual QA disabled. Set MANGABD_CONFIG['qa']['visual_qa']=True\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"model_manager\" not in globals():\n",
        "        print(\"  ❌ model_manager not found\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"load_image_artifact\" not in globals():\n",
        "        print(\"  ❌ storage functions not found\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    inpainted_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"inpainted\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if inpainted_bgr is None:\n",
        "        print(f\"  ⚠️ Inpainted image missing: {page_id}\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    regions = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"load_json_artifact\" in globals():\n",
        "        regions = load_json_artifact(page_id, \"detections\", default=[])\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not regions:\n",
        "        print(f\"  ⚠️ No regions found: {page_id}\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if sample_ratio is None:\n",
        "        sample_ratio = float(\n",
        "            MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].get(\"visual_qa_sample_ratio\", 0.25)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    sample_ratio = max(0.05, min(1.0, sample_ratio))\n",
        "\n",
        "    step = max(1, int(1.0 / sample_ratio))\n",
        "    sampled_regions = regions[::step]\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    👁️ Visual QA sampling {len(sampled_regions)}/{len(regions)} regions\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    qwen = model_manager.ensure_qwen()\n",
        "\n",
        "    issues = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    min_conf = float(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"qa\"].get(\"visual_qa_min_confidence\", 0.55)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    for region in sampled_regions:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            region_id = int(region.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "            x = int(region.get(\"x\", 0))\n",
        "            y = int(region.get(\"y\", 0))\n",
        "            w = int(region.get(\"width\", 0))\n",
        "            h = int(region.get(\"height\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "            if w < 8 or h < 8:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            crop = inpainted_bgr[y:y + h, x:x + w]\n",
        "\n",
        "            if crop.size == 0:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            crop_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(crop, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)\n",
        "\n",
        "            result = qwen.recognize(crop_rgb)\n",
        "\n",
        "            text = str(result.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "            confidence = float(result.get(\"confidence\", 0.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "            language_score = result.get(\"language_score\", {})\n",
        "            en_score = float(language_score.get(\"en\", 0.0))\n",
        "\n",
        "            if (\n",
        "                text\n",
        "                and not qa_is_special_marker(text)\n",
        "                and confidence >= min_conf\n",
        "                and en_score >= 0.5\n",
        "            ):\n",
        "                issues.append(add_quality_issue(\n",
        "                    page_id,\n",
        "                    region_id,\n",
        "                    \"inpaint\",\n",
        "                    f\"Possible residual text after inpaint: '{text[:30]}'\",\n",
        "                    severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "                    suggestion=\"Re-inpaint with larger mask\",\n",
        "                ))\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Visual QA region failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"    ✅ Visual QA done: {len(issues)} residual flags\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return issues\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Optional visual QA ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 13. Self-tests\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  13. Self-tests\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "tests = []\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def run_test(name, condition, details=\"\"):\n",
        "    status = \"✅\" if condition else \"❌\"\n",
        "    print(f\"  {status} {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if details and not condition:\n",
        "        print(f\"     {details}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    tests.append(bool(condition))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 1: QA issue add/clear\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_page = \"__cell12_qa_test__\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    clear_page_qa(test_page)\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_quality_issue(\n",
        "        test_page,\n",
        "        1,\n",
        "        \"ocr\",\n",
        "        \"Test issue\",\n",
        "        severity=\"warning\",\n",
        "        suggestion=\"Test suggestion\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    issues = get_page_issues(test_page)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = len(issues) == 1 and issues[0][\"issue\"] == \"Test issue\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    clear_page_qa(test_page)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"QA issue add/clear\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"QA issue add/clear\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 2: score computation\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    test_page = \"__cell12_score_test__\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    clear_page_qa(test_page)\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_quality_issue(test_page, 1, \"ocr\", \"Critical\", severity=\"critical\")\n",
        "    add_quality_issue(test_page, 2, \"translation\", \"Warning\", severity=\"warning\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    score = compute_page_score(test_page)\n",
        "\n",
        "    expected = max(0, 100 - 20 - 7)\n",
        "\n",
        "    clear_page_qa(test_page)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"score computation\",\n",
        "        score == expected,\n",
        "        f\"score={score}, expected={expected}\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"score computation\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 3: Bengali validation\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    ok = (\n",
        "        qa_validate_bengali(\"বাংলা\") is True\n",
        "        and qa_validate_bengali(\"Hello\") is False\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"Bengali validation\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"Bengali validation\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 4: special marker\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    ok = (\n",
        "        qa_is_special_marker(\"[OCR_FAILED]\") is True\n",
        "        and qa_is_special_marker(\"হ্যালো\") is False\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"special marker\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"special marker\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 5: overall summary does not crash\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    summary = compute_overall_quality_score()\n",
        "\n",
        "    ok = (\n",
        "        isinstance(summary, dict)\n",
        "        and \"score\" in summary\n",
        "        and \"critical\" in summary\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\"overall summary\", ok)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"overall summary\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 6: apply_translation_fixes empty dict\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    applied = apply_translation_fixes({}, reset_render=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"apply_translation_fixes empty\",\n",
        "        applied == 0,\n",
        "        f\"applied={applied}\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"apply_translation_fixes empty\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Test 7: generate fixes template does not crash\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    generate_fixes_template()\n",
        "    run_test(\"generate_fixes_template\", True)\n",
        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"generate_fixes_template\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print()\n",
