        "            if not src_path.exists():\n",
        "                skipped.append(page_id)\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            dst_path = final_dir / src_path.name\n",
        "\n",
        "            # যদি format convert করতে হয়\n",
        "            src_suffix = src_path.suffix.lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "            if image_format == \"png\" and src_suffix != \".png\":\n",
        "                img = cv2.imread(str(src_path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)\n",
        "\n",
        "                if img is not None:\n",
        "                    dst_path = final_dir / (src_path.stem + \".png\")\n",
        "                    cv2.imwrite(str(dst_path), img)\n",
        "                    exported.append(str(dst_path))\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    skipped.append(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "            elif image_format in [\"jpg\", \"jpeg\"] and src_suffix not in [\".jpg\", \".jpeg\"]:\n",
        "                img = cv2.imread(str(src_path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)\n",
        "\n",
        "                if img is not None:\n",
        "                    dst_path = final_dir / (src_path.stem + \".jpg\")\n",
        "                    cv2.imwrite(\n",
        "                        str(dst_path),\n",
        "                        img,\n",
        "                        [int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), int(quality)],\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "                    exported.append(str(dst_path))\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    skipped.append(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                shutil.copy2(src_path, dst_path)\n",
        "                exported.append(str(dst_path))\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            skipped.append(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Final export failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Final images exported: {len(exported)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if skipped:\n",
        "        print(f\"  ⚠️ Skipped: {len(skipped)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📁 Directory: {final_dir}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return exported\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Final image export ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. Translation CSV export\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_translation_csv():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    translation_df CSV export করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        CSV path\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir = get_export_dir()\n",
        "    csv_path = export_dir / \"translation_data.csv\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        if \"load_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "            load_translation_df()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None:\n",
        "        translation_df.to_csv(csv_path, index=False, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n",
        "        print(f\"  ✅ Translation CSV exported: {csv_path}\")\n",
        "        return str(csv_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"  ⚠️ translation_df not found\")\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Translation CSV export ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. ai.Text export\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_ai_text_safe():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    ai.Text export করে। Cell 8-এর export_ai_text ব্যবহার করে।\n",
        "    যদি সেটা না থাকে, fallback তৈরি করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        (txt_path, json_path)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"export_ai_text\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            return export_ai_text()\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"export_ai_text failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Fallback\n",
        "    export_dir = get_export_dir()\n",
        "\n",
        "    txt_path = export_dir / \"ai_text_export.txt\"\n",
        "    json_path = export_dir / \"translation_export.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"# MangaBD V12 — ai.Text Export\")\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"# Session: {MANGABD_CONFIG.get('session_id', 'unknown')}\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"#\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"# Format:\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"#   [PAGE_FILENAME:REGION_ID] English text → Bengali translation\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    records = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None and len(translation_df) > 0:\n",
        "        pages = translation_df[\"page\"].unique()\n",
        "\n",
        "        for page in pages:\n",
        "            page_rows = translation_df[\n",
        "                translation_df[\"page\"] == page\n",
        "            ].sort_values(\"id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            for _, row in page_rows.iterrows():\n",
        "                region_id = int(row.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "                original_text = str(row.get(\"original_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "                translated_text = str(row.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "                original_text = \" \".join(original_text.split())\n",
        "\n",
        "                if not original_text:\n",
        "                    line = f\"[{page}:{region_id}] [SKIP:OCR_FAILED] →\"\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    line = f\"[{page}:{region_id}] {original_text} → {translated_text}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "                lines.append(line)\n",
        "\n",
        "                records.append({\n",
        "                    \"page\": page,\n",
        "                    \"page_id\": str(row.get(\"page_id\", page)),\n",
        "                    \"id\": region_id,\n",
        "                    \"original_text\": original_text,\n",
        "                    \"translated_text\": translated_text,\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "    txt_path.write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    json_path.write_text(\n",
        "        json.dumps(records, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),\n",
        "        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ ai.Text exported: {txt_path}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ JSON sidecar  : {json_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(txt_path), str(json_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ ai.Text export ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 7. Quality report export\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_quality_report_safe():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Quality report JSON export করে।\n",
        "    Cell 12-এর export_quality_report_json ব্যবহার করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"export_quality_report_json\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            return export_quality_report_json()\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"export_quality_report_json failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Fallback\n",
        "    export_dir = get_export_dir()\n",
        "    report_path = export_dir / \"quality_report.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    data = {\n",
        "        \"session_id\": MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"session_id\", \"unknown\"),\n",
        "        \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "        \"note\": \"Fallback quality report\",\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    report_path.write_text(\n",
        "        json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),\n",
        "        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Quality report exported: {report_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(report_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Quality report export ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 8. Manifest / config / logs export\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_manifest_json():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Session manifest JSON export করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir = get_export_dir()\n",
        "    manifest_export_path = export_dir / \"manifest.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    manifest_source = Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"manifest_file\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "    if manifest_source.exists():\n",
        "        shutil.copy2(manifest_source, manifest_export_path)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        manifest_export_path.write_text(\n",
        "            json.dumps(MANGABD_MANIFEST, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),\n",
        "            encoding=\"utf-8\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Manifest exported: {manifest_export_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(manifest_export_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def export_config_json():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Sanitized CONFIG JSON export করে।\n",
        "    Secrets বাদ দেওয়া হয়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir = get_export_dir()\n",
        "    config_export_path = export_dir / \"config_v12.json\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    forbidden_keys = [\n",
        "        \"api_key\",\n",
        "        \"token\",\n",
        "        \"secret\",\n",
        "        \"password\",\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    def sanitize(obj):\n",
        "        if isinstance(obj, dict):\n",
        "            clean = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "            for key, value in obj.items():\n",
        "                if any(bad in str(key).lower() for bad in forbidden_keys):\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                clean[key] = sanitize(value)\n",
        "\n",
        "            return clean\n",
        "\n",
        "        if isinstance(obj, list):\n",
        "            return [sanitize(item) for item in obj]\n",
        "\n",
        "        return obj\n",
        "\n",
        "    safe_config = sanitize(MANGABD_CONFIG)\n",
        "\n",
        "    config_export_path.write_text(\n",
        "        json.dumps(safe_config, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),\n",
        "        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Config exported: {config_export_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(config_export_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Manifest/config export ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 9. ZIP export\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def create_session_zip(include_images=True, image_format=None, quality=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    পুরো session export ZIP তৈরি করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    ZIP-এ থাকে:\n",
        "        final/\n",
        "        translation_data.csv\n",
        "        ai_text_export.txt\n",
        "        translation_export.json\n",
        "        quality_report.json\n",
        "        manifest.json\n",
        "        config_v12.json\n",
        "        events.jsonl\n",
        "        session.log\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        ZIP path\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if image_format is None:\n",
        "        image_format = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"export\"].get(\"image_format\", \"jpeg\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if quality is None:\n",
        "        quality = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"export\"].get(\"jpeg_quality\", 92))\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir = get_export_dir()\n",
        "\n",
        "    session_id = MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"session_id\", \"session\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    zip_path = Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"exports\"]) / (\n",
        "        f\"MangaBD_V12_{session_id}.zip\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    zip_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"  📦 Creating ZIP...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, \"w\", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n",
        "        # Final images\n",
        "        if include_images:\n",
        "            final_files = export_final_images(\n",
        "                image_format=image_format,\n",
        "                quality=quality,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            for file_path in final_files:\n",
        "                file_path = Path(file_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "                if file_path.exists():\n",
        "                    zf.write(\n",
        "                        file_path,\n",
        "                        arcname=f\"final/{file_path.name}\",\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Translation CSV\n",
        "        if MANGABD_CONFIG[\"export\"].get(\"include_translation_csv\", True):\n",
        "            csv_path = export_translation_csv()\n",
        "\n",
        "            if csv_path and Path(csv_path).exists():\n",
        "                zf.write(csv_path, arcname=\"translation_data.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ai.Text + JSON sidecar\n",
        "        if MANGABD_CONFIG[\"export\"].get(\"include_ai_text\", True):\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                txt_path, json_path = export_ai_text_safe()\n",
        "\n",
        "                if txt_path and Path(txt_path).exists():\n",
        "                    zf.write(txt_path, arcname=\"ai_text_export.txt\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                if json_path and Path(json_path).exists():\n",
        "                    zf.write(json_path, arcname=\"translation_export.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as exc:\n",
        "                log_event(\n",
        "                    f\"ai.Text ZIP export failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                    level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Quality report\n",
        "        if MANGABD_CONFIG[\"export\"].get(\"include_quality_report\", True):\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                quality_path = export_quality_report_safe()\n",
        "\n",
        "                if quality_path and Path(quality_path).exists():\n",
        "                    zf.write(quality_path, arcname=\"quality_report.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as exc:\n",
        "                log_event(\n",
        "                    f\"Quality report ZIP export failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                    level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Manifest\n",
        "        if MANGABD_CONFIG[\"export\"].get(\"include_manifest\", True):\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                manifest_path = export_manifest_json()\n",
        "\n",
        "                if manifest_path and Path(manifest_path).exists():\n",
        "                    zf.write(manifest_path, arcname=\"manifest.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as exc:\n",
        "                log_event(\n",
        "                    f\"Manifest ZIP export failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                    level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Config\n",
        "        if MANGABD_CONFIG[\"export\"].get(\"include_config\", True):\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                config_path = export_config_json()\n",
        "\n",
        "                if config_path and Path(config_path).exists():\n",
        "                    zf.write(config_path, arcname=\"config_v12.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as exc:\n",
        "                log_event(\n",
        "                    f\"Config ZIP export failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                    level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Events JSONL\n",
        "        if MANGABD_CONFIG[\"export\"].get(\"include_events\", True):\n",
        "            events_path = Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"].get(\"events_file\", \"\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "            if events_path.exists():\n",
        "                zf.write(events_path, arcname=\"events.jsonl\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            session_log_path = Path(\n",
        "                MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"].get(\"session_log_file\", \"\")\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            if session_log_path.exists():\n",
        "                zf.write(session_log_path, arcname=\"session.log\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    zip_size_mb = zip_path.stat().st_size / (1024 * 1024)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ ZIP created: {zip_path}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  📏 Size: {zip_size_mb:.2f} MB\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Session ZIP created: {zip_path.name} ({zip_size_mb:.2f} MB)\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(zip_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def download_session_zip(include_images=True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Session ZIP তৈরি করে Colab download trigger করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    zip_path = create_session_zip(include_images=include_images)\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from google.colab import files as colab_files\n",
        "\n",
        "        colab_files.download(zip_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"  ✅ Download started\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        print(f\"  ⚠️ Download trigger failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"     ZIP path: {zip_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return zip_path\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ ZIP export ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 10. Preview helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def create_before_after_image(page_id, max_width=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Original vs Final side-by-side image তৈরি করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        PIL Image or None\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if max_width is None:\n",
        "        max_width = int(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"export\"].get(\"preview_max_width\", 900))\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        original_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "        final_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"final\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        if original_bgr is None or final_bgr is None:\n",
        "            return None\n",
        "\n",
        "        h, w = original_bgr.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        scale = min(max_width / w, 1.0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        new_w = int(w * scale)\n",
        "        new_h = int(h * scale)\n",
        "\n",
        "        original_small = cv2.resize(original_bgr, (new_w, new_h))\n",
        "        final_small = cv2.resize(final_bgr, (new_w, new_h))\n",
        "\n",
        "        original_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(original_small, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)\n",
        "        final_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(final_small, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)\n",
        "\n",
        "        combined = np.ones((new_h, new_w * 2 + 20, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "        combined[:new_h, :new_w] = original_rgb\n",
        "        combined[:new_h, new_w + 20:] = final_rgb\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.putText(\n",
        "            combined,\n",
        "            \"ORIGINAL\",\n",
        "            (10, 25),\n",
        "            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,\n",
        "            0.7,\n",
        "            (0, 0, 0),\n",
        "            2,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.putText(\n",
        "            combined,\n",
        "            \"FINAL\",\n",
        "            (new_w + 30, 25),\n",
        "            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,\n",
        "            0.7,\n",
        "            (0, 0, 0),\n",
        "            2,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return PILImage.fromarray(combined)\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"Before/after preview failed [{page_id}]: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def preview_before_after(page_id=None, max_pages=3):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Before/after preview display করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from IPython.display import display\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ IPython display not available\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page_id is not None:\n",
        "        target_pages = [page_id]\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        target_pages = [\n",
        "            pid for pid, page in pages.items()\n",
        "            if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"render\", False)\n",
        "        ][:max_pages]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not target_pages:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No rendered pages found for preview\")\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "\n",
        "    previews = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for pid in target_pages:\n",
        "        print(f\"  📄 {pid}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        preview = create_before_after_image(pid)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if preview is not None:\n",
        "            display(preview)\n",
        "            previews.append(preview)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(\"    ⚠️ Preview unavailable\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return previews\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def preview_region_closeup(page_id, max_regions=5):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Per-region Original → Final closeup display করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from IPython.display import display\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ IPython display not available\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ translation_df not found\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    original_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"original\")\n",
        "    final_bgr = load_image_artifact(page_id, \"final\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if original_bgr is None or final_bgr is None:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ Original/final image missing\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_rows = translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(page_rows) == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No regions found\")\n",
        "        return\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_rows = page_rows.head(max_regions)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for _, row in page_rows.iterrows():\n",
        "        rid = int(row.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = int(row.get(\"x\", 0))\n",
        "        y = int(row.get(\"y\", 0))\n",
        "        w = int(row.get(\"width\", 0))\n",
        "        h = int(row.get(\"height\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        original_text = str(row.get(\"original_text\", \"\"))[:30]\n",
        "        translated_text = str(row.get(\"translated_text\", \"\"))[:30]\n",
        "\n",
        "        pad = max(10, max(w, h) // 2)\n",
        "\n",
        "        x1 = max(0, x - pad)\n",
        "        y1 = max(0, y - pad)\n",
        "        x2 = min(original_bgr.shape[1], x + w + pad)\n",
        "        y2 = min(original_bgr.shape[0], y + h + pad)\n",
        "\n",
        "        orig_crop = original_bgr[y1:y2, x1:x2]\n",
        "        final_crop = final_bgr[y1:y2, x1:x2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if orig_crop.size == 0 or final_crop.size == 0:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        orig_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(orig_crop, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)\n",
        "        final_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(final_crop, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)\n",
        "\n",
        "        h_c, w_c = orig_rgb.shape[:2]\n",
        "\n",
        "        combined = np.ones((h_c, w_c * 2 + 20, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 255\n",
        "\n",
        "        combined[:h_c, :w_c] = orig_rgb\n",
        "        combined[:h_c, w_c + 20:] = final_rgb\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.putText(\n",
        "            combined,\n",
        "            \"ORIGINAL\",\n",
        "            (5, 15),\n",
        "            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,\n",
        "            0.4,\n",
        "            (0, 0, 0),\n",
        "            1,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        cv2.putText(\n",
        "            combined,\n",
        "            \"FINAL\",\n",
        "            (w_c + 25, 15),\n",
        "            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,\n",
        "            0.4,\n",
        "            (0, 0, 0),\n",
        "            1,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"  [{rid}] '{original_text}' → '{translated_text}'\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        display(PILImage.fromarray(combined))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Preview helpers ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 11. Export summary\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",

        "    print(f\"  {status} {name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if details and not condition:\n",

        "except Exception as exc:\n",
        "    run_test(\"manifest export\", False, str(exc)[:160])\n",

        "        \"api_key\" in config_text\n",
        "        and \"gemini_api_key\" in config_text\n",
        "        and MANGABD_SECRETS.get(\"gemini_api_key\", \"\")\n",
        "        and MANGABD_SECRETS.get(\"gemini_api_key\", \"\").lower() in config_text\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    run_test(\n",
        "        \"config export sanitized\",\n",

        "    )\n",

        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def _get_export_dir_safe():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Export directory return করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"get_export_dir\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            return get_export_dir()\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir = Path(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"].get(\n",
        "            \"session_export_dir\",\n",
        "            MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"][\"session_dir\"],\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return export_dir\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _safe_int(value, default=0):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        return int(value)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return default\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _safe_float(value, default=0.0):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        return float(value)\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return default\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _format_duration(seconds):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Seconds কে human-readable duration-এ convert করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        seconds = int(seconds)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if seconds < 60:\n",
        "            return f\"{seconds}s\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        minutes = seconds // 60\n",
        "        remaining_seconds = seconds % 60\n",
        "\n",
        "        if minutes < 60:\n",
        "            return f\"{minutes}m {remaining_seconds}s\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        hours = minutes // 60\n",
        "        remaining_minutes = minutes % 60\n",
        "\n",
        "        return f\"{hours}h {remaining_minutes}m\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",
        "        return \"unknown\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Helper utilities ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 3. Session statistics\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def compute_session_stats():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    পুরো session-এর statistics compute করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    stats = {\n",
        "        \"session_id\": MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"session_id\", \"unknown\"),\n",
        "        \"generated_at\": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n",
        "        \"total_pages\": len(pages),\n",
        "        \"stages\": {\n",
        "            \"upload\": 0,\n",
        "            \"detect\": 0,\n",
        "            \"ocr\": 0,\n",
        "            \"translate\": 0,\n",
        "            \"inpaint\": 0,\n",
        "            \"render\": 0,\n",
        "            \"qa\": 0,\n",
        "            \"export\": 0,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "        \"regions\": {\n",
        "            \"total\": 0,\n",
        "            \"translated\": 0,\n",
        "            \"bengali_valid\": 0,\n",
        "            \"low_confidence\": 0,\n",
        "            \"non_english\": 0,\n",
        "            \"failed\": 0,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "        \"quality\": {\n",
        "            \"score\": 0,\n",
        "            \"critical\": 0,\n",
        "            \"warning\": 0,\n",
        "            \"info\": 0,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "        \"models\": {\n",
        "            \"loaded\": [],\n",
        "            \"load_times\": {},\n",
        "            \"gpu\": {},\n",
        "        },\n",
        "        \"errors\": {\n",
        "            \"page_errors\": 0,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Stage counts\n",
        "    for page in pages.values():\n",
        "        stages = page.get(\"stages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "        for stage_name in stats[\"stages\"].keys():\n",
        "            if stages.get(stage_name, False):\n",
        "                stats[\"stages\"][stage_name] += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Page errors\n",
        "        page_error_list = page.get(\"errors\", [])\n",
        "\n",
        "        if isinstance(page_error_list, list):\n",
        "            stats[\"errors\"][\"page_errors\"] += len(page_error_list)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Translation DataFrame stats\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            df = translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "            stats[\"regions\"][\"total\"] = len(df)\n",
        "\n",
        "            if len(df) > 0:\n",
        "                translated_mask = (\n",
        "                    df[\"translated_text\"].astype(str).str.strip().ne(\"\")\n",
        "                )\n",
        "\n",
        "                stats[\"regions\"][\"translated\"] = int(translated_mask.sum())\n",
        "\n",
        "                if \"bengali_valid\" in df.columns:\n",
        "                    stats[\"regions\"][\"bengali_valid\"] = int(\n",
        "                        df[\"bengali_valid\"].astype(bool).sum()\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "\n",
        "                if \"confidence\" in df.columns:\n",
        "                    low_conf_threshold = _safe_float(\n",
        "                        MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"qa\", {}).get(\n",
        "                            \"low_confidence_threshold\",\n",
        "                            0.65,\n",
        "                        ),\n",
        "                        0.65,\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "\n",
        "                    stats[\"regions\"][\"low_confidence\"] = int(\n",
        "                        (df[\"confidence\"].astype(float) < low_conf_threshold).sum()\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "\n",
        "                if \"is_english\" in df.columns:\n",
        "                    stats[\"regions\"][\"non_english\"] = int(\n",
        "                        (~df[\"is_english\"].astype(bool)).sum()\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "\n",
        "                if \"status\" in df.columns:\n",
        "                    failed_mask = df[\"status\"].astype(str).str.contains(\n",
        "                        \"failed\",\n",
        "                        case=False,\n",
        "                        na=False,\n",
        "                    )\n",
        "\n",
        "                    stats[\"regions\"][\"failed\"] = int(failed_mask.sum())\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Session stats translation_df error: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Quality stats\n",
        "    if \"compute_overall_quality_score\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            quality = compute_overall_quality_score()\n",
        "\n",
        "            stats[\"quality\"][\"score\"] = _safe_int(quality.get(\"score\", 0))\n",
        "            stats[\"quality\"][\"critical\"] = _safe_int(quality.get(\"critical\", 0))\n",
        "            stats[\"quality\"][\"warning\"] = _safe_int(quality.get(\"warning\", 0))\n",
        "            stats[\"quality\"][\"info\"] = _safe_int(quality.get(\"info\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Session stats quality error: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Model stats\n",
        "    if \"model_manager\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            model_status = model_manager.status()\n",
        "\n",
        "            stats[\"models\"][\"loaded\"] = list(\n",
        "                model_status.get(\"loaded\", {}).keys()\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            stats[\"models\"][\"load_times\"] = model_status.get(\n",
        "                \"load_times\",\n",
        "                {},\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "            stats[\"models\"][\"gpu\"] = model_status.get(\"gpu\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            log_event(\n",
        "                f\"Session stats model error: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "                level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return stats\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Session statistics ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 4. Session log builder\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_session_log_text():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Full session log text তৈরি করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    stats = compute_session_stats()\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "    lines.append(\"MangaBD V12 — Session Log\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"Session ID : {stats['session_id']}\")\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"Generated  : {stats['generated_at']}\")\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"Device     : {MANGABD_CONFIG.get('runtime', {}).get('device', 'unknown')}\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "    lines.append(\"PAGE STAGES\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for stage_name, count in stats[\"stages\"].items():\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"{stage_name:12s}: {count}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "    lines.append(\"REGIONS\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key, value in stats[\"regions\"].items():\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"{key:16s}: {value}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "    lines.append(\"QUALITY\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"score           : {stats['quality']['score']}/100\")\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"critical issues : {stats['quality']['critical']}\")\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"warnings        : {stats['quality']['warning']}\")\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"info            : {stats['quality']['info']}\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "    lines.append(\"MODELS\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if stats[\"models\"][\"loaded\"]:\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"loaded: {', '.join(stats['models']['loaded'])}\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        lines.append(\"loaded: none\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if stats[\"models\"][\"load_times\"]:\n",
        "        lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "        lines.append(\"load times:\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        for model_name, load_time in stats[\"models\"][\"load_times\"].items():\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"  {model_name:10s}: {_safe_float(load_time):.1f}s\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    gpu = stats[\"models\"].get(\"gpu\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    if gpu:\n",
        "        lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "        lines.append(\"GPU:\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        for key, value in gpu.items():\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"  {key}: {value}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "    lines.append(\"ERRORS\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(f\"page errors: {stats['errors']['page_errors']}\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Events\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "    lines.append(\"RECENT EVENTS\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"-\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "    events_added = False\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"MANGABD_EVENTS\" in globals() and isinstance(MANGABD_EVENTS, list):\n",
        "        for event in MANGABD_EVENTS[-50:]:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                timestamp = event.get(\"timestamp\", \"\")\n",
        "                level = event.get(\"level\", \"INFO\")\n",
        "                message = event.get(\"message\", \"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                lines.append(f\"[{timestamp}] [{level}] {message}\")\n",
        "                events_added = True\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not events_added:\n",
        "        events_path = Path(MANGABD_CONFIG[\"paths\"].get(\"events_file\", \"\"))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if events_path.exists():\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                all_lines = events_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\n",
        "\n",
        "                for line in all_lines[-50:]:\n",
        "                    lines.append(line)\n",
        "\n",
        "                events_added = True\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not events_added:\n",
        "        lines.append(\"No events found\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "    lines.append(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def print_session_log():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Session log print করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = build_session_log_text()\n",
        "    print(text)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_session_log():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Session log file save করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_dir = _get_export_dir_safe()\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_path = export_dir / \"session_summary.txt\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = build_session_log_text()\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_path.write_text(text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Session log saved: {log_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    log_event(\n",
        "        f\"Session log saved: {log_path.name}\",\n",
        "        level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return str(log_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Session log builder ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 5. Dashboard HTML builder\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_dashboard_html():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Static HTML dashboard তৈরি করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    stats = compute_session_stats()\n",
        "\n",
        "    session_id = stats[\"session_id\"]\n",
        "    device = MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"runtime\", {}).get(\"device\", \"unknown\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    total_pages = stats[\"total_pages\"]\n",
        "    rendered_pages = stats[\"stages\"].get(\"render\", 0)\n",
        "    total_regions = stats[\"regions\"][\"total\"]\n",
        "    bengali_valid = stats[\"regions\"][\"bengali_valid\"]\n",
        "    quality_score = stats[\"quality\"][\"score\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if quality_score >= 90:\n",
        "        quality_label = \"Excellent\"\n",
        "        quality_color = \"#16a34a\"\n",
        "    elif quality_score >= 75:\n",
        "        quality_label = \"Good\"\n",
        "        quality_color = \"#2563eb\"\n",
        "    elif quality_score >= 60:\n",
        "        quality_label = \"Acceptable\"\n",
        "        quality_color = \"#d97706\"\n",
        "    elif quality_score >= 40:\n",
        "        quality_label = \"Needs improvement\"\n",
        "        quality_color = \"#ea580c\"\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        quality_label = \"Poor\"\n",
        "        quality_color = \"#dc2626\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    loaded_models = stats[\"models\"][\"loaded\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if loaded_models:\n",
        "        models_text = \", \".join(loaded_models)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        models_text = \"none loaded\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    gpu = stats[\"models\"].get(\"gpu\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    if gpu.get(\"device\", \"cpu\") != \"cpu\":\n",
        "        gpu_text = (\n",
        "            f\"{gpu.get('device', 'GPU')} | \"\n",
        "            f\"{gpu.get('allocated_gb', 0)} GB allocated / \"\n",
        "            f\"{gpu.get('total_gb', 0)} GB total\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        gpu_text = \"CPU only\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    page_rows_html = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page_id, page in pages.items():\n",
        "        stages = page.get(\"stages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "        def stage_icon(stage_name):\n",
        "            return \"✅\" if stages.get(stage_name, False) else \"⬜\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        page_short = str(page_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if len(page_short) > 36:\n",
        "            page_short = page_short[:33] + \"...\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        row = f\"\"\"\n",
        "        <tr>\n",
        "            <td>{page_short}</td>\n",
        "            <td>{stage_icon('upload')}</td>\n",
        "            <td>{stage_icon('detect')}</td>\n",
        "            <td>{stage_icon('ocr')}</td>\n",
        "            <td>{stage_icon('translate')}</td>\n",
        "            <td>{stage_icon('inpaint')}</td>\n",
        "            <td>{stage_icon('render')}</td>\n",
        "            <td>{stage_icon('qa')}</td>\n",
        "        </tr>\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        page_rows_html.append(row)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if page_rows_html:\n",
        "        page_table = f\"\"\"\n",
        "        <table>\n",
        "            <thead>\n",
        "                <tr>\n",
        "                    <th>Page</th>\n",
        "                    <th>Upload</th>\n",
        "                    <th>Detect</th>\n",
        "                    <th>OCR</th>\n",
        "                    <th>Translate</th>\n",
        "                    <th>Inpaint</th>\n",
        "                    <th>Render</th>\n",
        "                    <th>QA</th>\n",
        "                </tr>\n",
        "            </thead>\n",
        "            <tbody>\n",
        "                {''.join(page_rows_html)}\n",
        "            </tbody>\n",
        "        </table>\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        page_table = \"<p>No pages registered yet.</p>\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    html = f\"\"\"\n",
        "    <div style=\"font-family: Arial, sans-serif; max-width: 1000px; margin: 0 auto;\">\n",
        "        <h1 style=\"margin-bottom: 4px;\">MangaBD V12 Dashboard</h1>\n",
        "        <p style=\"color: #555; margin-top: 0;\">\n",
        "            Session: <b>{session_id}</b> |\n",
        "            Device: <b>{device}</b> |\n",
        "            GPU: <b>{gpu_text}</b>\n",
        "        </p>\n",
        "\n",
        "        <div style=\"display: flex; gap: 12px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin: 18px 0;\">\n",
        "            <div style=\"flex: 1; min-width: 160px; padding: 14px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 10px;\">\n",
        "                <div style=\"font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold;\">{total_pages}</div>\n",
        "                <div style=\"color: #555;\">Pages</div>\n",
        "            </div>\n",
        "\n",
        "            <div style=\"flex: 1; min-width: 160px; padding: 14px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 10px;\">\n",
        "                <div style=\"font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold;\">{rendered_pages}</div>\n",
        "                <div style=\"color: #555;\">Rendered</div>\n",
        "            </div>\n",
        "\n",
        "            <div style=\"flex: 1; min-width: 160px; padding: 14px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 10px;\">\n",
        "                <div style=\"font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold;\">{total_regions}</div>\n",
        "                <div style=\"color: #555;\">Regions</div>\n",
        "            </div>\n",
        "\n",
        "            <div style=\"flex: 1; min-width: 160px; padding: 14px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 10px;\">\n",
        "                <div style=\"font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold;\">{bengali_valid}</div>\n",
        "                <div style=\"color: #555;\">Bengali valid</div>\n",
        "            </div>\n",
        "\n",
        "            <div style=\"flex: 1; min-width: 160px; padding: 14px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 10px;\">\n",
        "                <div style=\"font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold; color: {quality_color};\">\n",
        "                    {quality_score}/100\n",
        "                </div>\n",
        "                <div style=\"color: #555;\">Quality: {quality_label}</div>\n",
        "            </div>\n",
        "        </div>\n",
        "\n",
        "        <h2>Models</h2>\n",
        "        <p><b>Loaded:</b> {models_text}</p>\n",
        "\n",
        "        <h2>Pipeline Status</h2>\n",
        "        {page_table}\n",
        "\n",
        "        <h2>Commands</h2>\n",
        "        <pre style=\"background: #f6f6f6; padding: 12px; border-radius: 8px; overflow-x: auto;\">\n",
        "upload_images()\n",
        "run_detection_ocr_all()\n",
        "run_translation_all(engine=\"gemini\")\n",
        "run_inpaint_render_all()\n",
        "inspect_all_pages()\n",
        "print_quality_report()\n",
        "download_session_zip()\n",
        "        </pre>\n",
        "    </div>\n",
        "\n",
        "    <style>\n",
        "        table {{\n",
        "            border-collapse: collapse;\n",
        "            width: 100%;\n",
        "            margin: 10px 0 20px 0;\n",
        "        }}\n",
        "        th, td {{\n",
        "            border: 1px solid #ddd;\n",
        "            padding: 8px;\n",
        "            text-align: center;\n",
        "            font-size: 13px;\n",
        "        }}\n",
        "        th {{\n",
        "            background: #f3f4f6;\n",
        "        }}\n",
        "        td:first-child {{\n",
        "            text-align: left;\n",
        "        }}\n",
        "    </style>\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return html\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_dashboard():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    HTML dashboard display করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from IPython.display import HTML, display\n",
        "\n",
        "        html = build_dashboard_html()\n",
        "        display(HTML(html))\n",
        "\n",
        "        log_event(\"Dashboard displayed\", level=\"INFO\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"Dashboard display failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ Dashboard display failed\")\n",
        "        print(\"  Using text summary instead:\")\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "        print_session_log()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Dashboard builder ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 6. Production readiness checklist\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def final_production_checklist():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Production readiness checklist check করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    checks = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    def add_check(name, ok, details=\"\"):\n",
        "        checks.append({\n",
        "            \"name\": name,\n",
        "            \"ok\": bool(ok),\n",
        "            \"details\": details,\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 1. Environment\n",
        "    env_ok = False\n",
        "    env_details = \"MANGABD_ENV missing\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"MANGABD_ENV\" in globals():\n",
        "        libraqm_ok = bool(MANGABD_ENV.get(\"libraqm_ok\", False))\n",
        "        critical_imports = MANGABD_ENV.get(\"critical_imports\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "        env_ok = libraqm_ok and all(critical_imports.values())\n",
        "\n",
        "        env_details = (\n",
        "            f\"libraqm={'OK' if libraqm_ok else 'missing'}, \"\n",
        "            f\"imports={'OK' if all(critical_imports.values()) else 'missing'}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\"Environment & libraqm\", env_ok, env_details)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 2. Fonts\n",
        "    regular_font = Path(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"rendering\", {}).get(\"font_regular\", \"\")\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    bold_font = Path(\n",
        "        MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"rendering\", {}).get(\"font_bold\", \"\")\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    fonts_ok = regular_font.exists() and bold_font.exists()\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"Bengali fonts\",\n",
        "        fonts_ok,\n",
        "        f\"regular={regular_font.name}, bold={bold_font.name}\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 3. Pages\n",
        "    pages = MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {})\n",
        "\n",
        "    total_pages = len(pages)\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"Pages registered\",\n",
        "        total_pages > 0,\n",
        "        f\"{total_pages} pages\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 4. Detection\n",
        "    detected_count = sum(\n",
        "        1 for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"detect\", False)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"Detection completed\",\n",
        "        detected_count > 0,\n",
        "        f\"{detected_count}/{total_pages} pages\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 5. OCR\n",
        "    ocr_count = sum(\n",
        "        1 for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"ocr\", False)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"OCR completed\",\n",
        "        ocr_count > 0,\n",
        "        f\"{ocr_count}/{total_pages} pages\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 6. Translation\n",
        "    translated_count = sum(\n",
        "        1 for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"translate\", False)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"Translation completed\",\n",
        "        translated_count > 0,\n",
        "        f\"{translated_count}/{total_pages} pages\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 7. Bengali validity\n",
        "    bengali_ratio = 0.0\n",
        "    bengali_details = \"no regions\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None:\n",
        "        total_regions = len(translation_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if total_regions > 0 and \"bengali_valid\" in translation_df.columns:\n",
        "            bengali_valid = int(translation_df[\"bengali_valid\"].astype(bool).sum())\n",
        "            bengali_ratio = bengali_valid / total_regions\n",
        "\n",
        "            bengali_details = (\n",
        "                f\"{bengali_valid}/{total_regions} \"\n",
        "                f\"({bengali_ratio * 100:.0f}%)\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"Bengali validity ratio\",\n",
        "        bengali_ratio >= 0.80 or total_pages == 0,\n",
        "        bengali_details,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 8. Inpainting\n",
        "    inpainted_count = sum(\n",
        "        1 for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"inpaint\", False)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"Inpainting completed\",\n",
        "        inpainted_count > 0,\n",
        "        f\"{inpainted_count}/{total_pages} pages\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 9. Rendering\n",
        "    rendered_count = sum(\n",
        "        1 for page in pages.values()\n",
        "        if page.get(\"stages\", {}).get(\"render\", False)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"Rendering completed\",\n",
        "        rendered_count > 0,\n",
        "        f\"{rendered_count}/{total_pages} pages\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 10. Quality score\n",
        "    quality_score = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"compute_overall_quality_score\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            quality = compute_overall_quality_score()\n",
        "            quality_score = _safe_int(quality.get(\"score\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            quality_score = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"Quality score\",\n",
        "        quality_score >= 75 or total_pages == 0,\n",
        "        f\"{quality_score}/100\",\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 11. Export directory\n",
        "    export_dir = _get_export_dir_safe()\n",
        "\n",
        "    export_ok = export_dir.exists()\n",
        "\n",
        "    add_check(\n",
        "        \"Export directory\",\n",
        "        export_ok,\n",
        "        str(export_dir),\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 12. Secrets safety\n",
        "    secrets_safe = True\n",
        "    secrets_details = \"OK\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        config_text = json.dumps(MANGABD_CONFIG).lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"MANGABD_SECRETS\" in globals():\n",
        "            for secret_value in MANGABD_SECRETS.values():\n",
        "                if secret_value and str(secret_value).lower() in config_text:\n",
        "                    secrets_safe = False\n",
        "                    secrets_details = \"secret leaked in CONFIG\"\n",
        "                    break\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception:\n",

        "        \"Secrets not in CONFIG\",\n",
        "        secrets_safe,\n",
        "        secrets_details,\n",

        "    # Print report\n",

        "    print(\"  Production Readiness Checklist\")\n",
        "    print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "    print()\n",

        "\n",

        "# MangaBD V12 — Hotfix: Rescue Manual Translation + Fix NLLB\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",

        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 1. Rescue translations from your uploaded ai.Text file\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def apply_translations_from_ai_file(file_path=None, assume_left_is_translation=True):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    ai.Text file থেকে translation apply করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    যদি →-এর ডান পাশে translation থাকে, সেটা ব্যবহার করে।\n",
        "    যদি ডান পাশ খালি থাকে কিন্তু বাম পাশে Bengali থাকে,\n",
        "    বাম পাশকে translation হিসেবে ধরে নেয়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if file_path is None:\n",
        "        files = sorted(\n",
        "            glob.glob(\"/content/ai_text_export*.txt\"),\n",
        "            key=os.path.getmtime,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not files:\n",
        "            print(\"  ❌ No ai_text_export*.txt file found in /content\")\n",
        "            return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        file_path = files[-1]\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📄 Reading: {file_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    content = open(file_path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\").read()\n",
        "\n",
        "    parsed = parse_ai_text(content)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  🔎 Parsed entries: {len(parsed)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    translations = {}\n",
        "    rescued_count = 0\n",
        "    normal_count = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key, value in parsed.items():\n",
        "        original_side = str(value.get(\"original_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "        translated_side = str(value.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Normal case: translation after arrow\n",
        "        if translated_side:\n",
        "            translations[key] = translated_side\n",
        "            normal_count += 1\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Rescue case: left side is Bengali translation\n",
        "        if assume_left_is_translation and original_side:\n",
        "            if qa_validate_bengali(original_side):\n",
        "                translations[key] = original_side\n",
        "                rescued_count += 1\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                # If left side is not Bengali, cannot safely rescue\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Normal translations: {normal_count}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Rescued translations: {rescued_count}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  📊 Total usable: {len(translations)}\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not translations:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No usable translations found\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    applied = apply_manual_translation(translations)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return applied\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ apply_translations_from_ai_file ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 2. Fix NLLB src_lang error\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def translate_with_nllb(text, region_type=\"bubble\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Fixed NLLB translator.\n",
        "\n",
        "    Newer transformers tokenizer call-এ src_lang keyword নেয় না।\n",
        "    তাই tokenizer.src_lang set করে call করা হয়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        instance = ensure_nllb()\n",
        "\n",
        "        model = instance[\"model\"]\n",
        "        tokenizer = instance[\"tokenizer\"]\n",
        "        device = instance[\"device\"]\n",
        "        forced_bos_id = instance[\"bengali_bos_id\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        nllb_cfg = MANGABD_CONFIG[\"translation\"][\"nllb\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        source_lang = nllb_cfg.get(\"source_lang_code\", \"eng_Latn\")\n",
        "        max_length = int(nllb_cfg.get(\"max_length\", 512))\n",
        "        num_beams = int(nllb_cfg.get(\"num_beams\", 4))\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Correct way for newer transformers\n",
        "        tokenizer.src_lang = source_lang\n",
        "\n",
        "        inputs = tokenizer(\n",
        "            str(text),\n",
        "            return_tensors=\"pt\",\n",
        "            max_length=max_length,\n",
        "            truncation=True,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if device == \"cuda\":\n",
        "            inputs = {\n",
        "                key: value.to(device)\n",
        "                for key, value in inputs.items()\n",
        "            }\n",
        "\n",
        "        with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "            output = model.generate(\n",
        "                **inputs,\n",
        "                forced_bos_token_id=forced_bos_id,\n",
        "                max_length=max_length,\n",
        "                num_beams=num_beams,\n",
        "            )\n",
        "\n",
        "        translated = tokenizer.batch_decode(\n",
        "            output,\n",
        "            skip_special_tokens=True,\n",
        "        )[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if \"clean_translation\" in globals():\n",
        "            return clean_translation(translated)\n",
        "\n",
        "        return str(translated).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        log_event(\n",
        "            f\"NLLB translation failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\",\n",
        "            level=\"WARN\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ translate_with_nllb fixed\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"  Hotfix SUCCESS\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)"
      ]
    },
