        "# Local helpers\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def _validate_bengali_local(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Bengali character আছে কিনা check।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not text:\n",
        "        return False\n",
        "\n",
        "    return any(\"\\u0980\" <= ch <= \"\\u09FF\" for ch in str(text))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _parse_ai_text_local(content):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    ai.Text content parse করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Supports:\n",
        "        [page.jpg:1] English text → বাংলা\n",
        "        [page.jpg:1] English text -> বাংলা\n",
        "        [page.jpg:1] English text => বাংলা\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    translations = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not content:\n",
        "        return translations\n",
        "\n",
        "    content = str(content).lstrip(\"\\ufeff\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    arrow_candidates = [\"→\", \"➔\", \"➡\", \"->\", \"=>\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "    for line in content.splitlines():\n",
        "        line = line.strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not line:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        if line.startswith(\"#\"):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        match = re.match(r\"^\\[([^:\\]]+):(\\d+)\\]\\s*(.*)$\", line)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not match:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        page = match.group(1).strip()\n",
        "        region_id = int(match.group(2))\n",
        "        rest = match.group(3).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        original_text = \"\"\n",
        "        translated_text = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        used_arrow = None\n",
        "\n",
        "        for arrow in arrow_candidates:\n",
        "            if arrow in rest:\n",
        "                used_arrow = arrow\n",
        "                break\n",
        "\n",
        "        if used_arrow is not None:\n",
        "            original_text, translated_text = rest.rsplit(used_arrow, 1)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            original_text = rest\n",
        "            translated_text = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        translations[(page, region_id)] = {\n",
        "            \"original_text\": original_text.strip(),\n",
        "            \"translated_text\": translated_text.strip(),\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    return translations\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# Main functions\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def show_text_for_translation():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    ai.Text content print করে copy করার জন্য।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "    print(\"  Copy this text, translate after →, then paste\")\n",
        "    print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    txt_path = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"export_ai_text_safe\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            txt_path, _ = export_ai_text_safe()\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            txt_path = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    if txt_path is None and \"export_ai_text\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            txt_path, _ = export_ai_text()\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            txt_path = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    content = \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if txt_path is not None and Path(txt_path).exists():\n",
        "        content = Path(txt_path).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        # Fallback: translation_df থেকে directly বানানো\n",
        "        if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None:\n",
        "            lines = []\n",
        "\n",
        "            lines.append(\"# MangaBD V12 — ai.Text Export\")\n",
        "            lines.append(\"# Format:\")\n",
        "            lines.append(\"#   [PAGE:ID] English text → বাংলা অনুবাদ\")\n",
        "            lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            for page in translation_df[\"page\"].unique():\n",
        "                page_rows = translation_df[\n",
        "                    translation_df[\"page\"] == page\n",
        "                ].sort_values(\"id\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                lines.append(f\"# Page: {page}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                for _, row in page_rows.iterrows():\n",
        "                    rid = int(row[\"id\"])\n",
        "                    original = str(row.get(\"original_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "                    original = \" \".join(original.split())\n",
        "\n",
        "                    if not original:\n",
        "                        original = \"[SKIP:OCR_FAILED]\"\n",
        "\n",
        "                    lines.append(f\"[{page}:{rid}] {original} →\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            content = \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"──── COPY FROM BELOW ────\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(content)\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(\"──── COPY UNTIL HERE ────\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(\"এখন একটা নতুন cell-এ এভাবে paste করুন:\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(\"pasted = '''\")\n",
        "    print(\"[02.jpg:1] English text → বাংলা অনুবাদ\")\n",
        "    print(\"[02.jpg:2] English text → বাংলা অনুবাদ\")\n",
        "    print(\"'''\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(\"apply_pasted_translation(pasted)\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    return content\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def apply_translations_from_text(content):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Pasted ai.Text content থেকে translation apply করে।\n",
        "\n",
        "    Example:\n",
        "        pasted = '''\n",
        "        [02.jpg:1] Hello → হ্যালো\n",
        "        [02.jpg:2] World → বিশ্ব\n",
        "        '''\n",
        "\n",
        "        apply_translations_from_text(pasted)\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not content or not str(content).strip():\n",
        "        print(\"  ❌ No text provided\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Parse\n",
        "    if \"parse_ai_text\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            parsed = parse_ai_text(content)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            parsed = _parse_ai_text_local(content)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        parsed = _parse_ai_text_local(content)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not parsed:\n",
        "        print(\"  ❌ No valid lines found\")\n",
        "        print(\"      Format: [page.jpg:1] English text → বাংলা\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    translations = {}\n",
        "    normal_count = 0\n",
        "    rescued_count = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key, value in parsed.items():\n",
        "        original_side = str(value.get(\"original_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "        translated_side = str(value.get(\"translated_text\", \"\")).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Normal: translation after arrow\n",
        "        if translated_side:\n",
        "            translations[key] = translated_side\n",
        "            normal_count += 1\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Rescue: left side is Bengali\n",
        "        if original_side and _validate_bengali_local(original_side):\n",
        "            translations[key] = original_side\n",
        "            rescued_count += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  🔎 Parsed entries: {len(parsed)}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Normal translations: {normal_count}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Rescued translations: {rescued_count}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  📊 Total usable: {len(translations)}\")\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "\n",
        "    if not translations:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ No usable translations found\")\n",
        "        print(\"      Make sure Bengali is after →\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Apply\n",
        "    if \"apply_manual_translation\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            applied = apply_manual_translation(translations)\n",
        "        except Exception as exc:\n",
        "            print(f\"  ⚠️ apply_manual_translation failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\")\n",
        "            applied = _apply_translations_fallback(translations)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        applied = _apply_translations_fallback(translations)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print()\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ Applied translations: {applied}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if applied > 0:\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "        print(\"  Next command:\")\n",
        "        print(\"      step_4_make_final()\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return applied\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def _apply_translations_fallback(translations):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Fallback apply যদি robust apply_manual_translation না থাকে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None:\n",
        "        print(\"  ❌ translation_df not found\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(translation_df) == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"  ❌ translation_df empty\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    def normalize_page(value):\n",
        "        return str(value).strip().replace(\" \", \"_\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    key_to_index = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    for idx, row in translation_df.iterrows():\n",
        "        region_id = int(row.get(\"id\", 0))\n",
        "\n",
        "        page_values = [\n",
        "            row.get(\"page\", \"\"),\n",
        "            row.get(\"page_id\", \"\"),\n",
        "            Path(str(row.get(\"page\", \"\"))).name,\n",
        "            Path(str(row.get(\"page_id\", \"\"))).name,\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "\n",
        "        for page_value in page_values:\n",
        "            if not page_value:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            key_to_index[(normalize_page(page_value), region_id)] = idx\n",
        "\n",
        "    applied = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for key, translated_text in translations.items():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            page_key, region_id = key\n",
        "            region_id = int(region_id)\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        translated_text = str(translated_text).strip()\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not translated_text:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        lookup_key = (normalize_page(page_key), region_id)\n",
        "        idx = key_to_index.get(lookup_key)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if idx is None:\n",
        "            basename_key = (normalize_page(Path(page_key).name), region_id)\n",
        "            idx = key_to_index.get(basename_key)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if idx is None:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"translated_text\"] = translated_text\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"translator_engine\"] = \"manual_paste\"\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"bengali_valid\"] = _validate_bengali_local(translated_text)\n",
        "        translation_df.at[idx, \"status\"] = \"translated\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        applied += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    if applied > 0 and \"save_translation_df\" in globals():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            save_translation_df()\n",
        "        except Exception:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    return applied\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# Easy alias\n",
        "apply_pasted_translation = apply_translations_from_text\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# Optional Textarea UI\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "def step_3_manual_translate_paste():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Copy/paste manual translation UI।\n",
        "\n",
        "    1. ai.Text print হবে\n",
        "    2. নিচে একটা textbox আসবে\n",
        "    3. translated text paste করে Apply চাপুন\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    show_text_for_translation()\n",
        "\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from ipywidgets import Textarea, Button, Output\n",
        "        from IPython.display import display\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "        print(\"  Paste translated text below\")\n",
        "        print(\"─\" * 70)\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "\n",
        "        textbox = Textarea(\n",
        "            placeholder=(\n",
        "                \"Paste translated ai.Text here...\\n\\n\"\n",
        "                \"Example:\\n\"\n",
        "                \"[02.jpg:1] Hello → হ্যালো\\n\"\n",
        "                \"[02.jpg:2] World → বিশ্ব\"\n",
        "            ),\n",
        "            layout={\n",
        "                \"width\": \"95%\",\n",
        "                \"height\": \"320px\",\n",
        "            },\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        apply_button = Button(\n",
        "            description=\"✅ Apply Translation\",\n",
        "            button_style=\"success\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        output_box = Output()\n",
        "\n",
        "        def on_apply_click(button):\n",
        "            with output_box:\n",
        "                output_box.clear_output()\n",
        "                applied = apply_translations_from_text(textbox.value)\n",
        "\n",
        "                if applied > 0:\n",
        "                    print()\n",
        "                    print(\"  ✅ Translation applied\")\n",
        "                    print(\"  এবার final image বানাতে চালান:\")\n",
        "                    print(\"      step_4_make_final()\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        apply_button.on_click(on_apply_click)\n",
        "\n",
        "        display(textbox)\n",
        "        display(apply_button)\n",
        "        display(output_box)\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as exc:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ Textarea UI unavailable\")\n",
        "        print(\"      Use triple-quote paste method instead:\")\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "        print(\"      pasted = '''\")\n",
        "        print(\"      [02.jpg:1] English → বাংলা\")\n",
        "        print(\"      '''\")\n",
        "        print()\n",
        "        print(\"      apply_pasted_translation(pasted)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Copy/paste translation ready\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "print(\"  Commands:\")\n",
        "print(\"      show_text_for_translation()\")\n",
        "print(\"      apply_pasted_translation(pasted)\")\n",
        "print(\"      step_3_manual_translate_paste()\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)"
      ]
    },

        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 🔧 FIX — Translate Stage Sync + Robust Inpaint/Render\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# সমস্যা: Translation apply হলেও manifest-এ 'translate' stage\n",
        "#          mark হয়নি, তাই Inpaint+Render skip করছিল।\n",
        "# সমাধান: 1) আসল data দেখে translate stage sync করা\n",
        "#          2) Inpaint/Render-কে translate flag-এর উপর\n",
        "#             hard-dependent না রাখা\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "print(\"  🔧 Fix: Translate Stage Sync\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Step 1: আগে কোথায় আটকে ছিল দেখি (diagnostic) ──\n",
        "print(\"  📊 আগের অবস্থা:\")\n",
        "if \"MANGABD_MANIFEST\" in globals():\n",
        "    for pid, pg in MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\", {}).items():\n",
        "        st = pg.get(\"stages\", {})\n",
        "        print(f\"     {pid}: detect={'✅' if st.get('detect') else '❌'}  \"\n",
        "              f\"ocr={'✅' if st.get('ocr') else '❌'}  \"\n",
        "              f\"translate={'✅' if st.get('translate') else '❌'}  \"\n",
        "              f\"inpaint={'✅' if st.get('inpaint') else '❌'}\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def sync_translate_stage():\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    translation_df-এর আসল বাংলা অনুবাদ দেখে\n",
        "    manifest-এ 'translate' stage mark করে।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None or len(translation_df) == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ translation_df খালি — আগে OCR + translation করো\")\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    synced = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    for page_id in translation_df[\"page_id\"].unique():\n",
        "        page_rows = translation_df[translation_df[\"page_id\"] == page_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "        valid_bengali = int(page_rows[\"bengali_valid\"].astype(bool).sum())\n",
        "        has_translation = int(\n",
        "            page_rows[\"translated_text\"].astype(str).str.strip().ne(\"\").sum()\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if valid_bengali > 0 or has_translation > 0:\n",
        "            # Translation artifact save করার চেষ্টা\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if \"save_translation_artifact\" in globals():\n",
        "                    save_translation_artifact(page_id)\n",
        "            except Exception:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "            mark_stage_done(page_id, \"translate\", meta={\n",
        "                \"translator_engine\": \"manual\",\n",
        "                \"translated_count\": has_translation,\n",
        "                \"bengali_count\": valid_bengali,\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "            synced += 1\n",
        "            print(f\"  ✅ {page_id}: translate mark হয়েছে ({valid_bengali} টি বাংলা)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if synced == 0:\n",
        "        print(\"  ⚠️ কোনো বাংলা অনুবাদ পাওয়া যায়নি — আগে translation apply করো\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return synced\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Step 2: Robust Inpaint+Render (translate flag-এ আটকাবে না) ──\n",
        "def run_inpaint_render_all(force=False, require_translation=False):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Inpaint + Render — এখন আর translate stage-এর উপর\n",
        "    hard dependency নেই। আগে translate stage auto-sync করে,\n",
        "    তারপর inpaint ও render চালায়।\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    # আগে translate stage ঠিক করে নিই\n",
        "    sync_translate_stage()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Inpaint (শুধু detect লাগে)\n",
        "    run_inpaint_all(force=force, require_translation=require_translation)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Render (inpaint + translation_df থেকে বাংলা পড়বে)\n",
        "    run_render_all(force=force, require_translation=require_translation)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ── Step 3: এখনই sync করে দিই ──\n",
        "print(\"  🔄 Translate stage sync হচ্ছে...\")\n",
        "sync_translate_stage()\n",
        "\n",
        "print()\n",
        "print(\"═\" * 60)\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Fix complete!\")\n",
        "print(\"  👉 এবার UI-এর '🎨 Inpaint + Render বাংলা' button চাপো\")\n",
        "print(\"═\" * 60)"
      ]

        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# 🩹 PATCH — (A) Mobile Single-Download  +  (B) Long-Strip Slicer\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "# A) files.download কে base64 tap-link দিয়ে replace → মোবাইলে ১ ট্যাপ=১ ডাউনলোড\n",
        "# B) লম্বা strip ইমেজকে overlap-সহ slice → detection/OCR full-res এ\n",
        "#    (সাধারণ পেজে অটো বন্ধ থাকে; SLICE_MODE=False করলে সব আগের মতো)\n",
        "# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n",
        "import os, base64, numpy as np, cv2\n",
        "from IPython.display import display, HTML\n",
        "from google.colab import files as colab_files\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 62)\n",
        "print(\"  🩹 Patch: Single-Download + Long-Strip Slicer\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 62)\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "# (A) MOBILE SINGLE-DOWNLOAD\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def mbd_safe_download(path, label=None):\n",
        "    \"\"\"ছোট ফাইল → base64 tap-link (মোবাইলে ১বার); বড় হলে fallback।\"\"\"\n",
        "    label = label or os.path.basename(path)\n",
        "    size = os.path.getsize(path)\n",
        "    ext = os.path.splitext(path)[1].lower()\n",
        "    mime = {'.zip':'application/zip','.jpg':'image/jpeg','.jpeg':'image/jpeg',\n",
        "            '.png':'image/png','.csv':'text/csv','.txt':'text/plain',\n",
        "            '.json':'application/json'}.get(ext, 'application/octet-stream')\n",
        "    if size <= 25_000_000:   # 25MB পর্যন্ত safe single-tap\n",
        "        with open(path, 'rb') as f:\n",
        "            b64 = base64.b64encode(f.read()).decode()\n",
        "        btn = (f'<a href=\"data:{mime};base64,{b64}\" download=\"{label}\" '\n",
        "               f'style=\"display:inline-block;margin:8px 0;padding:14px 22px;'\n",
        "               f'background:#1f9d44;color:#fff;font-family:Arial;font-weight:bold;'\n",
        "               f'font-size:16px;text-decoration:none;border:3px solid #171512;'\n",
        "               f'box-shadow:4px 4px 0 #171512;\">'\n",
        "               f'⬇️ {label} ({size/1e6:.1f} MB) — একবার ট্যাপ করো</a>')\n",
        "        display(HTML(btn))\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        colab_files.__orig_download(path)   # বড় ফাইলে fallback\n",
        "    return path\n",
        "\n",
        "# colab_files.download কে globally patch → সব বাটন (ZIP/single/CSV/aiText) একসাথে ঠিক\n",
        "if not getattr(colab_files, '__mbd_patched', False):\n",
        "    colab_files.__orig_download = colab_files.download\n",
        "    colab_files.download = mbd_safe_download\n",
        "    colab_files.__mbd_patched = True\n",
        "    print(\"  ✅ (A) Download fix active — এখন সব ডাউনলোড ১ ট্যাপে ১বার\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"  ℹ️  (A) Download fix আগেই active ছিল\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "# (B) LONG-STRIP SLICER (overlap-সহ, কিছুই কাটবে না)\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "SLICE_MODE        = True     # ⛔ বন্ধ করতে চাইলে এটা False করে আবার চালাও\n",
        "SLICE_H           = 1400     # প্রতি টুকরোর উচ্চতা\n",
        "OVERLAP           = 240      # overlap (সীমানার লেখা বাঁচাতে)\n",
        "SLICE_TRIGGER     = 2.0      # height > width×এটা হলেই slice (লম্বা strip)\n",
        "\n",
        "# original functions capture (একবারই — double-wrap আটকাতে)\n",
        "if not globals().get('_MBD_SLICED'):\n",
        "    _orig_rtdetr = rtdetr_detect\n",
        "    _orig_ctd    = ctd_detect_boxes_and_mask\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _off(boxes, dy):\n",
        "        out = []\n",
        "        for b in boxes:\n",
        "            b2 = dict(b); b2['y'] = int(b['y'] + dy); out.append(b2)\n",
        "        return out\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _iou(a, b):\n",
        "        ax1,ay1,ax2,ay2 = a['x'],a['y'],a['x']+a['w'],a['y']+a['h']\n",
        "        bx1,by1,bx2,by2 = b['x'],b['y'],b['x']+b['w'],b['y']+b['h']\n",
        "        iw = max(0, min(ax2,bx2)-max(ax1,bx1)); ih = max(0, min(ay2,by2)-max(ay1,by1))\n",
        "        inter = iw*ih; ua = a['w']*a['h']+b['w']*b['h']-inter\n",
        "        return inter/ua if ua > 0 else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _dedupe(bl, iou=0.5):\n",
        "        bl = sorted(bl, key=lambda b: -float(b.get('score',0)))\n",
        "        keep = []\n",
        "        for b in bl:\n",
        "            if not any(_iou(b,k) > iou for k in keep): keep.append(b)\n",
        "        return keep\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _segs(h):\n",
        "        res=[]; y=0\n",
        "        while y < h:\n",
        "            y2 = min(y+SLICE_H, h); res.append((y,y2))\n",
        "            if y2 >= h: break\n",
        "            y = y2 - OVERLAP\n",
        "        return res\n",
        "\n",
        "    def _need_slice(img):\n",
        "        h,w = img.shape[:2]\n",
        "        return SLICE_MODE and (h > w*SLICE_TRIGGER) and (h > SLICE_H+OVERLAP)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def rtdetr_detect(image_bgr, rtdetr_instance=None):\n",
        "        if not _need_slice(image_bgr):\n",
        "            return _orig_rtdetr(image_bgr, rtdetr_instance)\n",
        "        h,w = image_bgr.shape[:2]; agg = {'boxes':[],'bubble_boxes':[],\n",
        "                                           'text_bubble_boxes':[],'text_free_boxes':[]}\n",
        "        for (y1,y2) in _segs(h):\n",
        "            r = _orig_rtdetr(image_bgr[y1:y2], rtdetr_instance)\n",
        "            for k in agg: agg[k].extend(_off(r.get(k,[]), y1))\n",
        "        for k in agg: agg[k] = _dedupe(agg[k])\n",
        "        return agg\n",
        "\n",
        "    def ctd_detect_boxes_and_mask(image_bgr):\n",
        "        if not _need_slice(image_bgr):\n",
        "            return _orig_ctd(image_bgr)\n",
        "        h,w = image_bgr.shape[:2]; full = np.zeros((h,w), dtype=np.uint8); allb=[]\n",
        "        for (y1,y2) in _segs(h):\n",
        "            r = _orig_ctd(image_bgr[y1:y2]); allb.extend(_off(r.get('boxes',[]), y1))\n",
        "            m = r.get('raw_mask')\n",
        "            if m is not None:\n",
        "                mh,mw = m.shape[:2]\n",
        "                full[y1:y1+mh, :mw] = np.maximum(full[y1:y1+mh, :mw], m)\n",
        "        return {'boxes':_dedupe(allb), 'raw_mask':full, 'textlines':[]}\n",
        "\n",
        "    globals()['rtdetr_detect'] = rtdetr_detect\n",
        "    globals()['ctd_detect_boxes_and_mask'] = ctd_detect_boxes_and_mask\n",
        "    globals()['_MBD_SLICED'] = True\n",
        "    print(f\"  ✅ (B) Slicer installed — slice={SLICE_H}px, overlap={OVERLAP}px\")\n",
        "    print(f\"     শুধু লম্বা strip-এ চালু (height > width×{SLICE_TRIGGER})\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"  ℹ️  (B) Slicer আগেই installed ছিল\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "# PROOF: লম্বা strip-এ slice করলে বেশি লেখা ধরে (কাটে না)\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 62)\n",
        "print(\"  🧪 Proof: 02.jpg কে ৫× লম্বা strip বানিয়ে whole vs sliced তুলনা\")\n",
        "print(\"─\" * 62)\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    orig = load_image_artifact('02.jpg', 'original')\n",
        "    if orig is None: raise RuntimeError(\"02.jpg নেই\")\n",
        "    tall = np.vstack([orig]*5)                 # fake long strip\n",
        "    h,w = tall.shape[:2]\n",
        "    print(f\"  📐 Fake strip size: {w}×{h}  (slice হবে? {_need_slice(tall)})\")\n",
        "    whole = _orig_ctd(tall)                    # পুরোটা চেপে ১০২৪ → লেখা ছোট\n",
        "    sliced = ctd_detect_boxes_and_mask(tall)   # প্রতি টুকরো full-res\n",
        "    nw, ns = len(whole.get('boxes',[])), len(sliced.get('boxes',[]))\n",
        "    print(f\"  🔻 Whole-image (downscaled) detected : {nw} text regions\")\n",
        "    print(f\"  🔺 Sliced (full-res, overlap)        : {ns} text regions\")\n",
        "    if ns > nw:\n",
        "        print(f\"  ✅ Slicing {ns-nw} টি EXTRA region উদ্ধার করেছে — কোনোটা কাটেনি (overlap)\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        print(f\"  ℹ️  এই ইমেজে পার্থক্য কম; আসল লম্বা strip-এ পার্থক্য বড় হবে\")\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(f\"  ⏭️  Proof skipped ({str(e)[:50]}) — patch তবু active\")\n",
        "print()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 62)\n",
        "print(\"  ✅ Patch complete\")\n",
        "print(\"  ⛔ সব আগের মতো করতে চাইলে:  SLICE_MODE = False  (তারপর detection আবার চালাও)\")\n",
        "print(\"  🧪 টেস্ট: একটা লম্বা strip upload → Detect+OCR → region সংখ্যা আগে থেকে বেশি হবে\")\n",
        "print(\"  📥 ডাউনলোড টেস্ট: যেকোনো ⬇️ বাটন → সবুজ tap-link আসবে, একবার নামবে\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 62)"
      ]

        "# 2. STATE / HELPERS\n",

        "def mbd_stats():\n",
        "    s={\"pages\":0,\"regions\":0,\"translated\":0,\"bengali\":0,\"rendered\":0,\"session\":\"—\"}\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if \"MANGABD_CONFIG\" in globals(): s[\"session\"]=str(MANGABD_CONFIG.get(\"session_id\",\"—\"))[:12]\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None and len(translation_df)>0:\n",
        "            s[\"regions\"]=len(translation_df); s[\"pages\"]=translation_df[\"page\"].nunique()\n",
        "            if \"translated_text\" in translation_df.columns:\n",
        "                s[\"translated\"]=int(translation_df[\"translated_text\"].astype(str).str.strip().ne(\"\").sum())\n",
        "            if \"bengali_valid\" in translation_df.columns:\n",
        "                s[\"bengali\"]=int(translation_df[\"bengali_valid\"].astype(bool).sum())\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if \"MANGABD_MANIFEST\" in globals():\n",
        "            s[\"rendered\"]=sum(1 for p in MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\",{}).values()\n",
        "                              if p.get(\"stages\",{}).get(\"render\",False))\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    return s\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_header_html():\n",
        "    s=mbd_stats()\n",
        "    return f\"\"\"\n",
        "    <div class=\"mbd-header\">\n",
        "      <span class=\"mbd-live\"><span class=\"dot\"></span>LIVE</span>\n",
        "      <span class=\"mbd-title\">MANGABD</span><span class=\"mbd-vburst\">V12 📱</span>\n",
        "      <div class=\"mbd-sub\">🇧🇩 বাংলা মাঙ্গা ট্রান্সলেটর • Session: <b>{s['session']}</b></div>\n",
        "      <div class=\"mbd-speedbar\"></div>\n",
        "      <div class=\"mbd-stats\">\n",
        "        <div class=\"mbd-stat\"><div class=\"num\">{s['pages']}</div><div class=\"lbl\">PAGES</div></div>\n",
        "        <div class=\"mbd-stat\"><div class=\"num\">{s['regions']}</div><div class=\"lbl\">REGIONS</div></div>\n",
        "        <div class=\"mbd-stat\"><div class=\"num\">{s['translated']}</div><div class=\"lbl\">TRANSLATED</div></div>\n",
        "        <div class=\"mbd-stat\"><div class=\"num\">{s['bengali']}</div><div class=\"lbl\">বাংলা OK</div></div>\n",
        "        <div class=\"mbd-stat\"><div class=\"num\">{s['rendered']}</div><div class=\"lbl\">RENDERED</div></div>\n",
        "      </div>\n",
        "    </div>\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def _safe(fn,msg):\n",
        "    if fn in globals(): return globals()[fn]\n",
        "    with console: print(f\"⚠️  {fn}() পাওয়া যায়নি — {msg}\")\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "def build_ai_text_content():\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None or len(translation_df)==0:\n",
        "        return \"# কোনো OCR data নেই। আগে Upload + Detect+OCR চালাও।\"\n",
        "    lines=[\"# MangaBD V12 — ai.Text\",\"# Format: [page:id] English → বাংলা\",\"\"]\n",
        "    for page in translation_df[\"page\"].unique():\n",
        "        prows=translation_df[translation_df[\"page\"]==page].sort_values(\"id\")\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"# ═══ Page: {page} ═══\")\n",
        "        for _,r in prows.iterrows():\n",
        "            orig=str(r.get(\"original_text\",\"\")).strip()\n",
        "            orig=\"[SKIP:OCR_FAILED]\" if (not orig or orig in (\"[EMPTY]\",\"[OCR_FAILED]\",\"nan\")) else \" \".join(orig.split())\n",
        "            lines.append(f\"[{page}:{int(r['id'])}] {orig} →\")\n",
        "        lines.append(\"\")\n",
        "    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n",
        "\n",
        "def download_ai_text_once():\n",
        "    c=build_ai_text_content(); p=\"/content/ai_text_export.txt\"\n",
        "    open(p,\"w\",encoding=\"utf-8\").write(c); colab_files.download(p); return p\n",
        "\n",
        "def collect_final_images():\n",
        "    res={}\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if \"MANGABD_MANIFEST\" in globals() and \"get_artifact_path\" in globals():\n",
        "            for pid,pg in MANGABD_MANIFEST.get(\"pages\",{}).items():\n",
        "                if not pg.get(\"stages\",{}).get(\"render\",False): continue\n",
        "                orig=pg.get(\"original_filename\",pid)\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    fp=str(get_artifact_path(pid,\"final\"))\n",
        "                    if os.path.exists(fp): res[orig]=PILImage.open(fp).convert(\"RGB\")\n",
        "                except Exception: pass\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    if not res:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            if \"output_images\" in globals() and output_images:\n",
        "                for n,p in output_images.items(): res[n]=p\n",
        "        except Exception: pass\n",
        "    return res\n",
        "\n",
        "def _save_orig(pil,name,base=\"/content\"):\n",
        "    ext=os.path.splitext(name)[1].lower(); path=os.path.join(base,name)\n",
        "    if ext in (\".jpg\",\".jpeg\"): pil.save(path,format=\"JPEG\",quality=95)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        if ext!=\".png\": path=os.path.splitext(path)[0]+\".png\"\n",
        "        pil.save(path,format=\"PNG\")\n",
        "    return path\n",
        "\n",
        "def download_all_pages_zip():\n",
        "    finals=collect_final_images()\n",
        "    if not finals:\n",
        "        with console: print(\"⚠️  কোনো rendered page নেই! আগে Inpaint+Render চালাও।\"); return None\n",
        "    z=f\"/content/MangaBD_V12_{mbd_stats()['session']}_ALL.zip\"\n",
        "    with zipfile.ZipFile(z,\"w\",zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n",
        "        for n,p in finals.items():\n",
        "            b=io.BytesIO(); ext=os.path.splitext(n)[1].lower()\n",
        "            if ext in (\".jpg\",\".jpeg\"): p.save(b,format=\"JPEG\",quality=95); arc=n\n",
        "            else: p.save(b,format=\"PNG\"); arc=os.path.splitext(n)[0]+\".png\"\n",
        "            zf.writestr(arc,b.getvalue())\n",
        "    colab_files.download(z)\n",
        "    with console: print(f\"📦 {len(finals)} টি page ZIP-এ (original নাম সহ)\")\n",
        "    return z\n",
        "\n",
        "def _sync_translate():\n",
        "    if \"translation_df\" not in globals() or translation_df is None or len(translation_df)==0: return\n",
        "    if \"MANGABD_MANIFEST\" not in globals(): return\n",
        "    col=\"page_id\" if \"page_id\" in translation_df.columns else \"page\"\n",
        "    for pid in translation_df[col].unique():\n",
        "        rows=translation_df[translation_df[col]==pid]\n",
        "        beng=int(rows[\"bengali_valid\"].astype(bool).sum()) if \"bengali_valid\" in rows.columns else 0\n",
        "        tr=int(rows[\"translated_text\"].astype(str).str.strip().ne(\"\").sum()) if \"translated_text\" in rows.columns else 0\n",
        "        if beng>0 or tr>0:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if \"save_translation_artifact\" in globals(): save_translation_artifact(pid)\n",
        "            except Exception: pass\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if \"mark_stage_done\" in globals():\n",
        "                    mark_stage_done(pid,\"translate\",meta={\"translator_engine\":\"manual\",\"translated_count\":tr,\"bengali_count\":beng})\n",
        "            except Exception: pass\n",
        "\n",
        "def reset_checkpoint():\n",
        "    global translation_df\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if \"translation_df\" in globals() and translation_df is not None: translation_df=translation_df.iloc[0:0]\n",
        "    except Exception: translation_df=None\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        if \"MANGABD_MANIFEST\" in globals():\n",
        "            MANGABD_MANIFEST[\"pages\"]={}\n",
        "            if \"save_manifest\" in globals(): save_manifest()\n",
        "    except Exception: pass\n",
        "    for d in [\"uploaded_images\",\"inpainted_images\",\"output_images\"]:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            if d in globals() and isinstance(globals()[d],dict): globals()[d].clear()\n",
        "        except Exception: pass\n",
        "    with console: print(\"🔄 Checkpoint RESET — নতুন page upload করো\")\n",
        "    refresh_header()\n",
        "\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "# 3. WIDGETS  (button_style = Colab-mobile guaranteed visible)\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "header_box=widgets.HTML(value=build_header_html())\n",
        "console=widgets.Output(layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\",min_height=\"150px\"))\n",
        "console.add_class(\"mbd-console\")\n",
        "def refresh_header(): header_box.value=build_header_html()\n",
        "def say(m,i=\"✅\"):\n",
        "    with console: print(f\"{i}  {m}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "FULL=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\",height=\"54px\")\n",
        "def mbtn(desc,bs):\n",
        "    b=widgets.Button(description=desc,button_style=bs,layout=FULL); return b\n",
        "\n",
        "btn_upload   =mbtn(\"📤 Upload Images\",\"warning\")\n",
        "btn_ocr      =mbtn(\"🔍 Detect + OCR\",\"info\")\n",
        "btn_load_src =mbtn(\"🔄 Load Original Text\",\"info\")\n",
        "btn_apply    =mbtn(\"✅ Apply Translation\",\"success\")\n",
        "btn_export   =mbtn(\"⬇️ Export ai.Text (১ বার)\",\"info\")\n",
        "btn_import   =mbtn(\"⬆️ Upload Translated File\",\"success\")\n",
        "btn_gemini   =mbtn(\"💎 Translate (Gemini)\",\"danger\")\n",
        "btn_nllb     =mbtn(\"📚 Translate (NLLB offline)\",\"info\")\n",
        "btn_final    =mbtn(\"🎨 Inpaint + Render বাংলা\",\"danger\")\n",
        "btn_zip_all  =mbtn(\"📦 Download ALL Pages (ZIP)\",\"success\")\n",
        "btn_dl_single=mbtn(\"📥 Download This Page\",\"info\")\n",
        "btn_dl_ai    =mbtn(\"📄 Download ai.Text (১ বার)\",\"info\")\n",
        "btn_reset    =mbtn(\"🔄 CHECKPOINT RESET\",\"danger\")\n",
        "\n",
        "mode_sel=widgets.ToggleButtons(\n",
        "    options=[(\"📝 MANUAL (নিজে লিখব)\",\"manual\"),(\"💎 GEMINI (auto)\",\"gemini\"),(\"📚 NLLB (offline auto)\",\"nllb\")],\n",
        "    value=\"manual\",button_style=\"danger\",layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\"))\n",
        "try: mode_sel.style.button_width=\"100%\"\n",
        "except Exception: pass\n",
        "manual_sub=widgets.ToggleButtons(\n",
        "    options=[(\"📋 COPY-PASTE\",\"paste\"),(\"📁 FILE\",\"file\")],\n",
        "    value=\"paste\",button_style=\"info\",layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\"))\n",
        "try: manual_sub.style.button_width=\"100%\"\n",
        "except Exception: pass\n",
        "\n",
        "ta_src=widgets.Textarea(value=\"\",placeholder=\"Original English text এখানে…\",description=\"ORIGINAL:\",\n",
        "    disabled=True,layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\",height=\"120px\"),style={\"description_width\":\"75px\"})\n",
        "ta_tgt=widgets.Textarea(value=\"\",placeholder=\"[02.jpg:1] English → বাংলা এখানে পেস্ট/লিখো…\",description=\"বাংলা:\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\",height=\"160px\"),style={\"description_width\":\"75px\"})\n",
        "gemini_key=widgets.Password(value=\"\",placeholder=\"GOOGLE_GEMINI_API_KEY…\",description=\"🔑 KEY:\",\n",
        "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\"),style={\"description_width\":\"70px\"})\n",
        "page_dd=widgets.Dropdown(options=[],description=\"Page:\",layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\"))\n",
        "mode_panel=widgets.VBox([])\n",
        "\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "# 4. CALLBACKS\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def on_upload(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output(); say(\"Upload dialog খুলছে…\",\"📤\")\n",
        "    fn=_safe(\"upload_images\",\"V12 Cell 11 চালাও\")\n",
        "    if fn:\n",
        "        with console:\n",
        "            try: fn()\n",
        "            except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "    refresh_header()\n",
        "def on_ocr(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output(); say(\"Detect + OCR চলছে…\",\"🔍\")\n",
        "    fn=_safe(\"run_detection_ocr_all\",\"V12 Cell 11 চালাও\")\n",
        "    if fn:\n",
        "        with console:\n",
        "            try: fn()\n",
        "            except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "    refresh_header()\n",
        "def on_load_src(_):\n",
        "    ta_src.value=build_ai_text_content(); say(\"Original load হয়েছে — নিচে বাংলা লেখো/পেস্ট করো\",\"🔄\")\n",
        "def on_export(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output()\n",
        "    with console:\n",
        "        try: print(f\"⬇️  ai.Text download (১ বার): {download_ai_text_once()}\")\n",
        "        except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "def on_import(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output(); say(\"অনুবাদ করা file select করো…\",\"⬆️\")\n",
        "    with console:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            up=colab_files.upload()\n",
        "            if not up: print(\"⚠️  কোনো file আসেনি\"); return\n",
        "            c=up[list(up.keys())[0]].decode(\"utf-8\",errors=\"ignore\")\n",
        "            fn=_safe(\"apply_translations_from_text\",\"Copy-Paste cell চালাও\")\n",
        "            if fn: print(f\"✅ {fn(c)} translations apply হয়েছে\")\n",
        "        except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "    refresh_header()\n",
        "def on_apply(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output(); c=ta_tgt.value.strip()\n",
        "    if not c: say(\"আগে বাংলা লেখো/পেস্ট করো!\",\"⚠️\"); return\n",
        "    fn=_safe(\"apply_translations_from_text\",\"Copy-Paste cell চালাও\")\n",
        "    if fn:\n",
        "        with console:\n",
        "            try: print(f\"✅ {fn(c)} translations apply হয়েছে\")\n",
        "            except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "    refresh_header()\n",
        "def on_gemini(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output()\n",
        "    if gemini_key.value.strip() and \"MANGABD_SECRETS\" in globals(): MANGABD_SECRETS[\"gemini_api_key\"]=gemini_key.value.strip()\n",
        "    fn=_safe(\"run_translation_all\",\"V12 Cell 8 চালাও\")\n",
        "    if fn:\n",
        "        with console:\n",
        "            try: fn(engine=\"gemini\",force=True)\n",
        "            except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "    refresh_header()\n",
        "def on_nllb(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output(); say(\"NLLB offline translation চলছে…\",\"📚\")\n",
        "    fn=_safe(\"run_translation_all\",\"V12 Cell 8 চালাও\")\n",
        "    if fn:\n",
        "        with console:\n",
        "            try: fn(engine=\"nllb\",force=True)\n",
        "            except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "    refresh_header()\n",
        "def on_final(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output(); say(\"Translate sync + Inpaint + Render চলছে…\",\"🎨\")\n",
        "    with console:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            _sync_translate()\n",
        "            if \"run_inpaint_all\" in globals() and \"run_render_all\" in globals():\n",
        "                run_inpaint_all(force=False,require_translation=False)\n",
        "                run_render_all(force=True,require_translation=False)\n",
        "            elif \"run_inpaint_render_all\" in globals():\n",
        "                run_inpaint_render_all(force=False,require_translation=False)\n",
        "            else: print(\"❌ inpaint/render function পাওয়া যায়নি\")\n",
        "        except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "    refresh_header(); page_dd.options=list(collect_final_images().keys())\n",
        "def on_zip_all(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output(); say(\"সব pages ZIP হচ্ছে…\",\"📦\")\n",
        "    with console:\n",
        "        try: download_all_pages_zip()\n",
        "        except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "def on_dl_single(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output(); n=page_dd.value\n",
        "    if not n: say(\"আগে একটা page বাছো!\",\"⚠️\"); return\n",
        "    f=collect_final_images()\n",
        "    if n in f:\n",
        "        with console:\n",
        "            try: colab_files.download(_save_orig(f[n],n)); print(f\"📥 Download: {n}\")\n",
        "            except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "def on_dl_ai(_):\n",
        "    console.clear_output()\n",
        "    with console:\n",
        "        try: print(f\"⬇️  ai.Text (১ বার): {download_ai_text_once()}\")\n",
        "        except Exception as e: print(f\"❌ {e}\")\n",
        "reset_armed={\"v\":False}\n",
        "def on_reset(_):\n",
        "    if not reset_armed[\"v\"]:\n",
        "        reset_armed[\"v\"]=True; btn_reset.description=\"⚠️ নিশ্চিত? আবার চাপো!\"; say(\"Confirm করতে আরেকবার চাপো\",\"⚠️\")\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        reset_armed[\"v\"]=False; btn_reset.description=\"🔄 CHECKPOINT RESET\"; console.clear_output(); reset_checkpoint()\n",
        "\n",
        "for b,f in [(btn_upload,on_upload),(btn_ocr,on_ocr),(btn_load_src,on_load_src),(btn_apply,on_apply),\n",
        "            (btn_export,on_export),(btn_import,on_import),(btn_gemini,on_gemini),(btn_nllb,on_nllb),\n",
        "            (btn_final,on_final),(btn_zip_all,on_zip_all),(btn_dl_single,on_dl_single),\n",
        "            (btn_dl_ai,on_dl_ai),(btn_reset,on_reset)]:\n",
        "    b.on_click(f)\n",
        "\n",
        "def rebuild_mode_panel(*_):\n",
        "    m=mode_sel.value\n",
        "    if m==\"manual\":\n",
        "        if manual_sub.value==\"file\":\n",
        "            body=widgets.VBox([widgets.HTML(\"<div class='mbd-bubble'>📁 Export → অনুবাদ → Upload (১ বার download)</div>\"),btn_export,btn_import])\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            body=widgets.VBox([widgets.HTML(\"<div class='mbd-bubble'>📋 Load → বাংলা লেখো/পেস্ট → Apply</div>\"),btn_load_src,ta_src,ta_tgt,btn_apply])\n",
        "        mode_panel.children=[manual_sub,body]\n",
        "    elif m==\"gemini\":\n",
        "        mode_panel.children=[widgets.HTML(\"<div class='mbd-bubble'>💎 Gemini API (key দরকার)</div>\"),gemini_key,btn_gemini]\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        mode_panel.children=[widgets.HTML(\"<div class='mbd-bubble'>📚 NLLB offline — key লাগে না</div>\"),btn_nllb]\n",
        "mode_sel.observe(rebuild_mode_panel,names=\"value\"); manual_sub.observe(rebuild_mode_panel,names=\"value\")\n",
        "rebuild_mode_panel()\n",
        "\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "# 5. ASSEMBLE (single column, mobile-safe, no fixed overlay)\n",
        "# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
        "def section(tag,children,red=False):\n",
        "    tc=\"mbd-panel-tag red\" if red else \"mbd-panel-tag\"\n",
        "    box=widgets.VBox(children)\n",
        "    return widgets.VBox([widgets.HTML(f\"<div class='mbd-panel'><span class='{tc}'>{tag}</span></div>\"),box])\n",
        "\n",
        "studio_root=widgets.VBox([\n",
        "    header_box,\n",
        "    section(\"STEP 1–2 • INPUT + OCR\",[btn_upload,btn_ocr]),\n",
        "    section(\"STEP 3 • TRANSLATION\",[mode_sel,mode_panel],red=True),\n",
        "    section(\"STEP 4 • RENDER\",[btn_final]),\n",
        "    section(\"STEP 5 • DOWNLOAD (original নাম)\",[btn_zip_all,btn_dl_ai,page_dd,btn_dl_single],red=True),\n",
        "    section(\"⚠️ DANGER ZONE\",[btn_reset]),\n",
        "    section(\"📟 LIVE CONSOLE\",[console],red=True),\n",
        "])\n",
        "studio_root.add_class(\"mbd-studio\")\n",
        "display(studio_root)\n",
        "\n",
        "with console:\n",
        "    print(\"🎨 MangaBD V12 Control Studio v4 ready! (Mobile-Fixed)\")\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ Button এখন দেখা যাবে (লাল/সবুজ/নীল/হলুদ)\")\n",
        "    print(\"   👆 উপর থেকে নিচে scroll করে ব্যবহার করো\")\n",
        "    page_dd.options=list(collect_final_images().keys())"
